Result: 研究发现:(1)在弱驱动条件下,足够强的非线性耦合可以主导退相干效应,显著缩短充电时间;(2)在强驱动和最优驱动条件下(即线性耦合下最大化稳态遍历性的驱动强度),非线性耦合产生了超越线性耦合的新最优充电时间,并伴随近临界阻尼动力学特性;(3)即使在没有外部驱动的情况下,仅依靠非线性耦合就能大幅减少充电时间,提供了一条实现快速充电的替代路径。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了非线性充电器-电池耦合作为加速量子电池充电的有效资源,拓展了相互作用驱动的量子优势在能量传输和存储领域的应用范围。非线性耦合机制为设计高效量子电池提供了新的理论基础和实现途径,展示了量子系统在能量存储方面相对于经典系统的潜在优势。

Abstract: Charger-mediated quantum batteries offer promising opportunities for efficient energy transfer and storage at the quantum level, with potential advantages beyond their classical counterparts. Here, we theoretically demonstrate that nonlinear charger-battery interactions can substantially accelerate the charging of quantum batteries. In the weak-driving regime, sufficiently strong nonlinear coupling can dominate over the effect of dephasing and significantly reduce the charging time. In the strong- and optimal-driving regimes, where the optimal driving strength maximizes the steady-state ergotropy in the linear-coupling regime, nonlinear coupling gives rise to a new optimum in the charging time beyond that of the linear-coupling regime, accompanied by nearly critically damped dynamics. Remarkably, even in the absence of external driving, nonlinear coupling alone can substantially reduce the charging time, providing an alternative route toward fast battery charging. Our results establish nonlinear charger-battery coupling as an effective resource for accelerating quantum-battery charging and broaden the scope of interaction-enabled quantum advantages in energy transfer and storage.

</details>


### [126] [From Syndromes to Computation: A General Framework for Logical Operations in Quantum Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17612)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要能够保护量子信息并支持实用逻辑操作的纠错码。现有方法缺乏统一框架来系统地设计CSS码和非CSS码上的Clifford门及更高阶逻辑门。特别是对于新兴的量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC码），如何构造低权重、可寻址的逻辑门操作仍是关键挑战。需要建立码的稳定子结构与逻辑门设计之间的直接联系。

Method: 1. 提出基于对易Pauli旋转的稳定子症候群框架，推导任意旋转角度下的精确码空间保持条件；2. 引入等症候群（equal-syndrome）特化条件，将Clifford门设计简化为稳定子分解问题，并推导出距离为d的码的生成元权重界限⌈d/2⌉；3. 将框架系统扩展到Clifford层级中的更高阶逻辑旋转；4. 应用于bivariate-bicycle（BB）码和非CSS Mirror qLDPC码，构造显式低权重门；5. 开发基于Clifford代数基的解析构造方法，将症候群约束转化为显式代数关系，实现逻辑可寻址性；6. 在递归超立方码和中间量子Reed-Muller码上进行独立构造验证。

Result: 1. 在[[16,4,4]] BB码上实现可寻址的T门；2. 在[[64,8,8]] BB码上实现T型旋转；3. 在[[36,6,6]] Mirror码上实现CCZ、CS和T型操作；4. 在递归超立方码上解析构造可寻址的CNOT、SWAP、controlled和CCZ型门；5. 在中间量子Reed-Muller码上构造饱和等症候群权重界限的C^r Z门；6. 证明了生成元权重界限⌈d/2⌉的普遍性；7. 所有构造均为低权重实现，提高了物理实现的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了稳定子症候群作为量子纠错码的稳定子结构、其可直接支持的逻辑计算以及物理实现的容错性质之间的构造性桥梁。等症候群条件和Clifford代数方法为系统化设计低权重、可寻址的逻辑门提供了实用工具，特别适用于qLDPC码。该框架统一了CSS码和非CSS码、Clifford操作和非Clifford操作的描述，为容错量子计算中的逻辑门实现提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires error-correcting codes that not only protect quantum information but also support useful logical operations with practical physical realizations. Here we develop a general framework in which the stabilizer syndromes of commuting Pauli rotations directly organize logical-gate design. We derive an exact codespace-preservation condition valid at arbitrary rotation angles, providing a common stabilizer-level description for CSS and non-CSS codes and for both Clifford and non-Clifford operations. A particularly useful equal-syndrome specialization reduces Clifford gate design to stabilizer factorization, yields a general generator weight bound of $\lceil d/2\rceil$ for distance-$d$ codes, and systematically extends to logical rotations higher in the Clifford hierarchy. Applied to bivariate-bicycle (BB) and non-CSS Mirror qLDPC codes, the framework gives explicit low-weight Clifford and controlled-phase gates. Our implementations yield new low-weight gates, including an addressed $T$ gate on the $[[16,4,4]]$ BB code, $T$-type rotations on the $[[64,8,8]]$ BB code, and CCZ-, CS-, and $T$-type operations on the $[[36,6,6]]$ Mirror code. We also develop analytic constructions using Clifford algebra bases, which turn the syndrome constraints into explicit algebraic relations and provide a systematic route to logical addressability. We use this approach to analytically construct addressed CNOT, SWAP, controlled, and CCZ-type gates on recursive hypercube codes. An independent construction on middle quantum Reed--Muller codes yields C${}^r$Z gates that saturate the equal-syndrome weight bound. These results establish stabilizer syndromes as a constructive link between the stabilizer structure of a quantum code, the logical computations it can directly support, and the fault-tolerance properties of their physical realizations.

</details>

Result: 三种量子生成器在少量参数配置下成功恢复了有意义的边际分布和时间分布特征。单层配置与更深层变体保持竞争力。在相同深度下，独立训练的ZZ-CRX配置相比ZZ表现出杠杆中位数的主要下移趋势，这与受控旋转构造的预期方向性作用一致。参数空间分析揭示了边际目标和时间目标之间存在竞争性的参数偏好，解释了难以找到在两个目标上都表现良好的参数设置的原因。

Conclusion: 研究初步证明了结构化假设可以指导紧凑型量子生成器的设计，并将电路架构与可观察的统计行为联系起来。小参数量设计不仅提高了可解释性，还能进行直接的参数空间分析，揭示训练困难的根源。这为更系统化和参数高效的量子电路构建方法提供了动力，表明问题导向的结构设计是提升量子机器学习模型可解释性和实用性的有效途径。

Abstract: When used as function approximators, quantum circuits often lack interpretability and face a fundamental trade-off between expressivity and trainability. We propose a problem-informed framework for financial return generation that uses relational structure and symmetry-inspired parameter sharing to guide the construction of quantum circuits with few trainable parameters and empirically testable operator roles. We evaluate three quantum generator architectures, referred to as ZZ, directed ZZ-CRX, and structured time evolution, within a hybrid Wasserstein quantum generative adversarial network trained to generate 16-step Bitcoin return windows. Across circuits with one to four layers and one to eight trainable coefficients, these generators recover meaningful marginal and temporal distributional features, while single-layer configurations remain competitive with deeper variants. Across matched depths, independently trained ZZ-CRX configurations exhibit a predominantly downward shift in median leverage relative to ZZ, consistent with the intended directional role of the controlled-rotation construction. The small number of coefficients also enables direct landscape analysis, revealing competing parameter preferences between marginal and temporal objectives and helping to explain the difficulty of finding parameter settings that perform well on both. These results provide an initial demonstration that structural assumptions can guide the design of compact quantum generators and link circuit architecture to observable statistical behaviour, motivating more systematic and parameter-efficient approaches to quantum circuit construction.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子学习算法对于稀疏傅里叶系数布尔函数的样本复杂度为O(k^(3/2)log²k)，尚未达到最优。研究者希望探索是否能够进一步降低样本复杂度至接近理论下界，同时明确量子学习相对于经典学习的优势边界。

Method: 提出了一种新的量子学习算法，核心技术包括：(1)从独立傅里叶采样组中通过子集的对称差构造候选集；(2)使用多线性多数投票多项式将无偏随机近似组合成准确的见证函数，其傅里叶支撑位于候选集内；(3)利用粒度性质强制候选集包含完整的傅里叶支撑，包括从未直接采样的子集。作为对比，通过结合已知的稀疏恢复和下界结果推导出经典学习的紧致样本复杂度Θ(nk)。

Result: 实现了样本复杂度为Õ(k)的量子学习算法，该复杂度独立于输入长度n，仅依赖于非零傅里叶系数的数量k。该算法将之前的O(k^(3/2)log²k)上界改进到近乎最优的Õ(k)。确定了经典学习的紧致样本复杂度为Θ(nk)，明确展示了量子学习相对于经典学习在输入长度依赖性上的指数级优势。

Conclusion: 本文为稀疏傅里叶系数布尔函数的量子学习提供了近乎最优的算法，样本复杂度达到Õ(k)，显著改进了现有结果。通过对称差构造候选集和多线性多数投票技术的创新组合，成功解决了样本效率问题。与经典Θ(nk)复杂度的对比清晰地展示了量子计算在学习理论中的本质优势，即能够消除对输入维度的依赖性，这为量子机器学习的理论基础提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: We study the number of uniform quantum examples needed to exactly learn a Boolean function with at most $k$ nonzero Fourier coefficients. We give a learner with near-optimal $\widetilde{O}(k)$ sample complexity independent of the input length $n$. The learner uses only separate measurements of individual examples. This improves the previous $O(k^{3/2}\log^2 k)$ upper bound (Arunachalam, Chakraborty, Lee, Paraashar, and de Wolf, 2021). The main ingredient is a candidate set formed by symmetric differences of subsets from independent groups of Fourier samples. A multilinear majority polynomial combines unbiased random approximations into an accurate witness whose Fourier support lies in this set. Granularity then forces the set to contain the entire Fourier support, including subsets never directly sampled. For comparison, we derive the tight classical sample complexity $Θ(nk)$ by combining known sparse-recovery and lower-bound results.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子计量学中，突破标准量子极限（SQL）实现更高测量精度是一个基本目标。现有方法在噪声环境和短探测时间条件下面临挑战，需要开发能够同时利用多种量子资源、在实际环境中保持量子增强优势的传感协议。

Method: 提出了一种针对囚禁离子自旋-振子系统的简化弱信号传感协议，该方法同时利用两种量子资源：（1）失谐运动压缩态（detuned motional squeezing）；（2）自旋-振子纠缠（spin-oscillator entanglement）。通过结合这两种量子资源，显著加速自旋系统中的相位积累过程。

Result: 相比于非压缩情况，该协议在灵敏度上实现了指数级改进。在噪声环境中，协议提供的计量学增益依然显著，灵敏度保持在标准量子极限以下。特别是在短探测时间regime下，该协议保持了明显的优势，展现了量子增强传感的实用潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了一种结合失谐运动压缩和自旋-振子纠缠的简化协议，能够在囚禁离子系统中实现突破标准量子极限的弱信号传感。该方案在噪声鲁棒性和短时间探测方面具有实际应用优势，为量子增强传感技术的实用化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving measurement precision beyond the standard quantum limit (SQL) is a fundamental object in quantum metrology. In this manuscript, we propose a streamlined protocol for weak signal sensing in a trapped-ion spin-oscillator system that simultaneously exploits detuned motional squeezing and spin-oscillator entanglement. By combining these two quantum resources, the phase accumulation in this spin system is significantly accelerated, as a result achieving an exponential improvement in the sensitivity over unsqueezed cases. Remarkably, we show that the significant metrological gain provided by our protocol persists in noisy environments, with the sensitivity remaining below the SQL. In particular, our protocol retains a distinct advantage in the short interrogation time regime, thus demonstrating the practical potential in quantum-enhanced sensing.

</details>


### [130] [GWPTools: An extensible Wolfram Language framework for time-dependent Gaussian wave packet dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17683)
*Jeremy B. Maddox,Ismail Giliani,Carter M. Heck,Connor S. Mattingly*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GWPTools，一个基于Wolfram Language的高斯波包动力学计算框架，通过符号-数值混合架构实现精确的含时量子现象建模，可用于教学演示和数值方法基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯波包能够在经典轨迹与全量子力学之间架起桥梁，为含时量子现象提供精确解析基础。然而，缺乏一个可扩展的统一框架来进行高斯波包动力学的精确计算、教学演示以及对数值方法进行严格基准测试。

Method: 开发了GWPTools框架，采用统一的符号-数值架构，基于"参数总线"和多态调度机制构建。1.0.0版本聚焦于受驱动一维二次势中的精确时间演化。框架原生扩展至量子流体力学和相空间表示，支持精确能量分解、解析Bohmian轨迹、准概率分布和信息度量的符号推导。通过原型验证了叠加态干涉、开放系统退相干和耦合二维振子等模块的可扩展性。

Result: 成功实现了基于Wolfram Language的高度可扩展框架，能够精确计算含时高斯波包动力学。框架既可作为交互式教学工具探索量子理论，又可作为精确代数引擎为基于网格和轨迹的数值方法提供严格基准。演示了在干涉、退相干和耦合振子等复杂场景中的模块化扩展能力。

Conclusion: GWPTools框架为精确解析建模建立了多功能基础平台，通过其模块化架构和符号-数值混合能力，能够将精确解析方法的应用范围扩展至日益复杂的受驱动量子系统，为量子动力学研究、教学和数值方法验证提供了强大工具。

Abstract: Gaussian wave packets provide an exact analytical foundation for modeling time-dependent quantum phenomena, effectively bridging the gap between classical trajectories and full quantum mechanics. We introduce GWPTools, a highly extensible Wolfram Language framework designed to compute exact time-dependent Gaussian wave packet dynamics using a unified symbolic-numeric architecture. Built upon a robust ``parameter bus'' and polymorphic dispatch, the software serves as an interactive pedagogical tool for exploring quantum theory and an exact algebraic engine for rigorously benchmarking numerical grid-based and trajectory methodologies. Version 1.0.0 focuses on exact time evolution in driven one-dimensional quadratic potentials. Native extensions into quantum hydrodynamics and phase space representations enable exact energy decompositions, analytical Bohmian trajectories, and symbolic derivations of quasi-probability distributions and information metrics. We demonstrate the framework's modular scalability by prototyping interference in superpositions, open-system decoherence, and coupled two-dimensional oscillators. Ultimately, these capabilities establish a versatile foundation for maximizing the reach of exact analytical modeling toward increasingly complex driven quantum systems.

</details>


### [131] [QEDIA: Quantum Program Verification in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17708)
*Yuanjie,Ren,Zhicheng Zhang,Junyi Liu,Jinzheng Li,Yidi Qi,Mingsheng Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QEDIA是一个在Lean 4中验证带有经典变量的量子程序的框架，基于量子Hoare逻辑QHL+，并利用LLM辅助证明代理在人类监督下构建形式化验证库和程序证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子程序交织了量子计算和经典计算，经典数据影响量子操作，测量结果又影响后续经典计算。对这类程序进行形式化验证需要在证明辅助工具中机械化一个程序逻辑，从量子力学数学基础、程序语法语义到规范和证明规则，在每个层级上一致地处理这种交互。现有的机械化及其程序证明通常需要大量人工工作，因此需要一个自动化程度更高的验证框架。

Method: 构建了QEDIA框架，其核心是在Lean 4中形式化量子Hoare逻辑QHL+的开放库，涵盖从量子力学数学基础到程序规范的所有层级，并证明该逻辑对部分正确性和完全正确性均是可靠的。扩展了通用LLM证明代理的核心功能，使其适用于量子程序验证，在人类监督下利用该代理构建形式化库和程序证明。

Result: 使用QEDIA成功验证了多个量子程序实例，包括量子傅里叶变换（QFT）、相位估计、重复直到成功电路以及哈密顿量模拟等经典量子算法和电路。

Conclusion: QEDIA提供了一个有效的量子程序形式化验证框架，通过将量子Hoare逻辑在Lean 4中完整机械化，并结合LLM辅助证明技术，显著降低了量子程序验证的人工工作量，为处理量子-经典混合计算的形式化验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Practical quantum programs interleave quantum and classical computation: classical data shape the quantum operations that are performed, and measurement outcomes shape the classical computation that follows. Formal verification of such programs in a proof assistant requires mechanizing a program logic that handles this interplay consistently at every level, from the mathematics of quantum mechanics, through program syntax and semantics, to specifications and proof rules. Such mechanizations, along with the program proofs built on them, typically require substantial manual effort.
  We present QEDIA, a framework for verifying quantum programs with classical variables in Lean 4. At its foundation is an open library that formalizes the quantum Hoare logic QHL+ across all these levels and proves the logic sound for both partial and total correctness. We construct the library and the program proofs, under human supervision, using an LLM-based prover agent, whose general-purpose core we extend for quantum program verification. We illustrate QEDIA with verified programs including the quantum Fourier transform, phase estimation, repeat-until-success circuits, and Hamiltonian simulation.

</details>


### [132] [Representation Geometry and Inductive Biases in Quantum Classifiers across Classical Visual Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17722)
*Yu Yamaoka,Weng Ian Chan,Kosuke Mitarai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子机器学习在真实图像数据上的表现，通过使用经典特征提取器并分析量子分类器的泛化能力，发现量子分类器的有效性强烈依赖于输入表示的几何结构，并提出核对齐方法可识别与量子特征空间兼容的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子机器学习在特定合成数据上具有理论优势，但其在自然图像等真实数据上是否具有实际意义尚不清楚。量子结构特性引入的归纳偏置与经典模型不同，为公平比较量子分类器性能，需要研究其在经典强特征表示上的泛化行为，以及量子酉结构如何影响真实数据的学习效果。

Method: 采用经典特征提取器为自然图像提供强表示，控制数据规模和监督强度，专注于分析量子分类器层面的性能。通过研究量子分类器的酉结构如何影响泛化能力，探讨输入表示的几何结构与量子分类器有效性之间的关系。使用核对齐(kernel alignment)方法识别与量子特征空间兼容的表示。

Result: 研究表明量子分类器的有效性强烈依赖于输入表示的几何结构，当经典特征与量子特征空间对齐时，量子分类器能够从中获益。核对齐方法能够成功识别与量子特征空间兼容的表示，为理解量子分类器在真实数据上的行为提供了见解。

Conclusion: 量子分类器在真实图像数据上的优势取决于经典特征表示与量子特征空间的对齐程度。通过核对齐方法可以识别适合量子处理的表示，这为将经典模型转化为量子算法提供了指导思路，揭示了量子机器学习在实际应用中需要考虑特征空间几何兼容性的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum Machine Learning (QML) has a theoretical advantage over classical algorithms for specific synthetic data that is computationally hard. However, it remains unclear whether such theoretical advantages play a meaningful role for realistic classical data, such as natural images. Quantum structural properties induced by unitary operations or entanglement introduce inductive biases that differ from those of classical models, potentially leading to distinct learning behavior on real-world data. In a fully quantum learning pipeline, classical real-world data are first encoded into quantum states via a quantum feature mapping, analogous to a classical feature extractor, and a quantum classifier then classifies the resulting states. Quantum feature mappings generate representations that are difficult to realize classically, while also inducing representation-level biases that do not necessarily match the structure of realistic data. To enable a fair comparison at the classifier level, we focus on the classifier and use classical feature extractors that already provide powerful representations for natural images. By controlling the data scale and supervision strength, we analyze how the unitary structure of quantum classifiers affects generalization, and show that their effectiveness depends strongly on the geometry of the input representations. These results indicate that quantum classifiers benefit from classical features that are aligned with quantum feature spaces. Our analysis shows that kernel alignment identifies representations compatible with quantum feature spaces, providing insights into translating classical models into quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [133] [PADO-Pauli: A GPU-Accelerated Implementation of Pauli Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17755)
*Hyunwoo Kim,Youngseok Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了PADO-Pauli，一个基于GPU的Pauli传播引擎，用于经典地近似变分量子算法中参数化电路的期望值计算。通过将电路和可观测量编译为稀疏矩阵并集成PyTorch自动微分，该引擎在4-12量子比特上比cuPauliProp快10倍，在16量子比特上内存使用减少3.1倍，为训练变分量子算法提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在训练过程中需要对参数化电路进行大量重复评估，包括在不同参数值、数据批次和梯度计算时。现有方法效率不足，因此需要一个高效的经典模拟工具来加速Pauli传播计算，以支持量子机器学习模型的训练。

Method: PADO-Pauli采用以下关键技术：(1)将电路和可观测量一次性编译为稀疏矩阵，其中每个旋转角度仅贡献余弦和正弦因子，实现编译程序的重复使用；(2)支持参数和数据输入批量处理，这些输入作为旋转角度嵌入；(3)通过向量-雅可比积与PyTorch自动微分集成，使梯度能够回传到产生角度的任何经典层；(4)提供按Pauli权重和系数大小的截断功能以及噪声通道；(5)零过滤技术移除在全零参考态下不贡献期望值的项，使批量前向传播速度提升约1.5倍。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100 GPU上的实验表明：与cuPauliProp相比，PADO-Pauli在4到12量子比特上的评估速度提升最高达10倍，在16量子比特上内存使用减少最高达3.1倍。通过与精确态矢量模拟对比，验证了该方法在最多28量子比特上的准确性。零过滤技术使批量前向传播速度提升约1.5倍。

Conclusion: PADO-Pauli通过高效的编译-重用机制、批处理能力、自动微分集成和优化技术(如零过滤)，为训练变分量子算法(包括量子机器学习模型)提供了一个实用且高效的工具包。其在速度和内存效率上相比现有方法有显著提升，同时保持了高精度，适用于实际的量子算法训练场景。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms utilize parameterized circuits many times during training, at new parameter values, over batches of data, and with gradients. Pauli propagation approximates the expectation values of such circuits classically by evolving the observable in the Heisenberg picture as a truncated sum of Pauli strings. We present PADO-Pauli, a GPU engine for Pauli propagation that compiles a circuit and its observables once into sparse matrices in which each rotation angle contributes only a cosine and a sine factor, so every later evaluation reuses the compiled program. It evaluates batches of parameters and data inputs embedded as rotation angles. By default its gradients come from a vector-Jacobian product registered with PyTorch's automatic differentiation, so they flow through the angles to any classical layer that produces them. PADO-Pauli also provides truncation by Pauli weight and coefficient size, and noise channels. Zero-filtering removes terms that do not contribute to an expectation value in the all-zero reference state and makes the batched forward pass about 1.5 times faster. Compared to cuPauliProp on an NVIDIA A100, PADO-Pauli evaluates up to 10 times faster on 4 to 12 qubits and uses up to 3.1 times less memory on 16 qubits. We check its accuracy against exact statevector simulation up to 28 qubits. These features make PADO-Pauli a practical toolkit for training variational quantum algorithms, including quantum machine learning models.

</details>


### [134] [Boson Sampling Using Fock States from On-Demand Single Photon Source Arrays: Quantum Complexity & Impact of Dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17773)
*Swarnabha Chattaraj,Anupam Madhukar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架,用于直接关联单光子源的退相干物理过程与多光子关联测量结果,并预测了使用台面顶量子点单光子源实现片上玻色采样时,退相干和衰减时间尺度对N光子纠缠态概率分布的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管台面顶单量子点(MTSQD)阵列在按需产生高纯度、高不可分辨性的多光子资源态方面取得了重要进展,为片上玻色采样提供了可能性,但在规模化扩展过程中,单个按需单光子的退相干和衰减时间尺度如何影响N光子纠缠态的最终概率分布仍是一个关键问题,需要建立从物理退相干源到多光子测量结果的直接联系。

Method: 研究者建立了一个理论框架,在考虑单个波包形状和退相干的同时,显式地纳入了N光子态的对称化处理。利用该框架,模拟了使用4个相同单光子波包(模拟MTSQD单光子源发射的光子)初始化光学回路来生成4光子NOON态的概率,并分析了双光子干涉可见度(由纯退相干时间尺度与辐射衰减时间尺度的关系决定)对各种4光子纠缠态生成概率的影响。

Result: 该框架成功给出了4光子NOON态生成概率的预测估计,并揭示了不同4光子纠缠态的生成概率如何依赖于双光子干涉可见度。研究结果建立了温度等物理过程与量子光学回路输出态之间的定量联系,为测量光学回路中生成的4光子及更多光子纠缠态提供了定量指导。

Conclusion: 本工作为理解和优化基于按需单光子源的片上玻色采样提供了重要的理论工具,通过将单光子源的物理退相干机制(如纯退相干时间尺度、辐射衰减时间尺度)与多光子纠缠态的测量结果直接关联,为规模化量子光学回路的设计和实现提供了定量准则,并明确了温度等外部条件对量子态生成的影响途径。

Abstract: Boson sampling with array of on-demand single photons (discreet variable Fock states) as resource states provides distinct advantages in complexity scaling compared to single photon resource states mimicked by the states (continuous variable) generated in nonlinear photonics. Recent progress in realization of regular ordered array of mesa top single quantum dots (MTSQDs) enabling on-demand generation of many photon resource states with high single photon purity and indistinguishability [1,2] has opened the possibility of implementing chip-scale Boson sampling with single photons. An important question towards scale up of such circuits is how the dephasing and decay timescale of individual on-demand single photons affect the final probability distribution of the N-photon entangled state. In this work we report a framework that provides direct link between the physical sources of decoherence to the outcome of multiphoton correlation measurements. We explicitly incorporate the symmetrization of the N-photon states while accounting for the shape of the individual wave packets and dephasing. Using this framework, we show predictive estimates of the probability of generation of a 4-photon NOON state using an optical circuit initialized with 4 identical single photon wave packets, mimicking the emitted photons from MTSQD single photon sources reported in [2]. Specifically, we show how the probability of generating various 4-photon entangled states depend on the two-photon interference visibility which is determined by the pure dephasing timescale in relation to the radiative decay timescale. The results provide quantitative guidelines to measure 4 photon and higher number of entangled photon states generated in an optical circuit with on demand single photon sources and provide a link between physical processes such as temperature and the output state of the quantum optical circuits.

</details>


### [135] [Multi-parameter estimation enhanced by level crossing Hamiltonian structure with Bayesian feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17790)
*Ryo Tatsumi,Le Bin Ho,Hiroaki Matsueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究建立了量子系统中能级交叉结构与量子度量学精度极限之间的联系框架，提出了一种基于幺正淬火动力学和贝叶斯估计的资源高效传感协议，该协议无需纠缠或复杂控制即可实现高精度多参数估计，其精度达到Holevo Cramér-Rao下界，揭示了能级交叉点可作为精密量子度量学的通用工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能级交叉现象在量子系统中普遍存在且对控制量子动力学至关重要，但其与量子度量学精度极限的内在联系尚未被系统阐明。现有的多参数量子传感方法通常需要纠缠态或复杂控制，限制了实际应用的可扩展性。因此，研究者希望探索能级交叉结构在精密量子度量学中的基础作用，并开发简单、可扩展且资源高效的多参数传感方案。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：(1) 分析具有能级交叉结构的哈密顿量，识别在能级交叉点附近可同时以最大灵敏度估计多个参数的条件；(2) 构建基于幺正淬火动力学(unitary quench dynamics)和贝叶斯估计的传感协议；(3) 理论证明该协议的估计精度可达到Holevo Cramér-Rao下界，即多参数估计中最严格的精度界限；(4) 以两能级模型和双自旋伊辛模型为例，演示协议在具有能级交叉哈密顿结构的系统中的应用。

Result: 研究成功构建了连接能级交叉结构与量子度量学精度极限的理论框架，提出的传感协议实现了以下结果：(1) 协议的估计精度饱和Holevo Cramér-Rao下界，达到理论最优精度；(2) 无需使用纠缠态或复杂量子控制即可实现高精度、低成本的多参数估计；(3) 通过两能级系统和双自旋伊辛模型的具体示例，验证了协议在具有能级交叉结构系统中的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了能级交叉点可作为精密量子度量学的通用工作点，为实际多参数量子传感提供了简单且可扩展的实现路径。研究建立的理论框架和资源高效传感协议为跨越不同量子平台的实用化多参数传感开辟了新方向，具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。能级交叉结构作为量子系统的普遍特征，其在量子度量学中的核心作用为未来量子传感技术的发展提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Level crossings, where two quantum energy levels intersect, are ubiquitous in quantum systems and play a central role in controlling quantum dynamics. In this study, we establish a framework that connects these level crossing structures to the ultimate precision limits of quantum metrology. By analyzing a Hamiltonian with the level crossings structure, we identify the conditions under which multiple parameters can be estimated simultaneously with maximal sensitivity near the level crossing point. We then construct a resource efficient sensing protocol based on unitary quench dynamics and Bayesian estimation, enabling high precision, low cost parameter estimation without requiring entanglement or complex control. We present a protocol whose estimation precision saturates the Holevo Cramer Rao bound, the tightest achievable precision bound in multi parameter estimation. Using the two level model and the two spin Ising model as illustrative examples, we demonstrate how the protocol can be applied to a system with level crossing Hamiltonian structure. Our findings reveal that level crossings serve as universal operating points for precision quantum metrology, offering a simple and scalable path toward practical multi parameter sensing across diverse quantum platforms.

</details>


### [136] [Broadening the reflection band of atomic mirrors by engineering the emitter-waveguide coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17834)
*C. -Y. Lee,G. -D. Lin,H. H. Jen,K. -T. Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了具有空间调制发射体-波导耦合的有限波导量子电动力学(QED)阵列中的单光子相干输运。通过精确散射计算、谱因式分解和半经典WKB分析,揭示了耦合轮廓如何控制反射带宽及其有限尺寸依赖性,并推导出1/N标度律,为原子-纳米光纤和电路QED平台中宽带原子镜的设计提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀波导QED阵列存在反射带宽受限、边界诱导的法布里-珀罗振荡干扰以及亚辐射共振终止反射窗口等问题。需要通过空间耦合工程来优化原子镜的性能,实现宽带高反射率,并抑制有限尺寸效应带来的不利影响,以满足原子-纳米光纤和电路QED平台的实际应用需求。

Method: 采用多种理论方法组合分析:1)精确散射计算求解单光子在有限QED阵列中的输运特性;2)谱因式分解技术分析系统的极点和零点分布;3)半经典WKB近似分析空间调制耦合的影响;4)研究不同耦合轮廓(特别是高斯横向位移产生的指数-高斯衰减率轮廓)对反射特性的调控;5)推导有限尺寸修正的代数标度律。

Result: 研究发现:1)高斯横向位移产生的指数-高斯衰减率轮廓能显著拓宽高反射率窗口,并抑制边界诱导的法布里-珀罗振荡;2)在非布拉格间距下,任意正耦合轮廓的透射零点在原子共振处保持精确简并;3)空间调制使集体极点重新组织,重新分配辐射宽度,防止单个亚辐射共振终止反射窗口;4)推导出带宽向体极限收缩的代数1/N标度律;5)体带宽由最大局域衰减率决定,而有限尺寸修正随轮廓变尖锐而增加,存在最优轮廓宽度。

Conclusion: 空间耦合工程是设计宽带原子镜的实用工具。通过优化耦合轮廓(如指数-高斯型),可以有效拓宽反射带宽、抑制有限尺寸效应、控制集体共振特性。研究建立的1/N标度律和最优轮廓宽度理论为原子-纳米光纤和电路QED平台中高性能量子光学器件的实际设计提供了明确的理论指导和优化策略。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate single-photon coherent transport through a finite waveguide quantum electrodynamics (QED) array with spatially modulated emitter-waveguide couplings. By combining exact scattering calculations with spectral factorization and a semiclassical WKB analysis, we identify how the coupling profile controls the reflection bandwidth and its finite-size dependence. A Gaussian transverse displacement produces an exponential-of-Gaussian decay-rate profile that broadens the high-reflectivity window and suppresses the boundary-induced Fabry-Perot oscillations compared with a uniform array. The transmission zeros remain exactly degenerate at the atomic resonance for arbitrary positive coupling profiles at non-Bragg spacings. In contrast, the collective poles reorganize under the spatial modulation, redistributing their radiative widths and preventing individual subradiant resonances from terminating the reflection window. We further derive an algebraic 1/N scaling law for the contraction of the bandwidth toward its bulk value. The bulk bandwidth is determined by the largest local decay rate, whereas the finite-size correction increases as the profile becomes sharper, leading to an optimal profile width. These results establish spatial coupling engineering as a practical tool for designing broadband atomic mirrors in atom-nanofiber and circuit-QED platforms.

</details>


### [137] [A Tutorial on Quantum Amplitude Embedding with Data Re-Uploading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17836)
*Hyojun Ahn,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子机器学习中数据重上传技术对振幅编码模型的增益效果,揭示了每次重上传可将模型可达多项式阶数提升2阶,并通过实验验证了重上传次数与任务多项式阶数的匹配关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振幅编码虽能用少量量子比特读取大量经典特征,但模型输出仅为归一化输入的二次型,表达能力受限。数据重上传可丰富模型表达力,但每次重上传都需要昂贵的状态制备成本。因此需要在实际投入门操作资源前,明确量化重上传带来的收益大小,以指导设计者合理配置重上传次数。

Method: 1) 建立统一的数学表示框架,将顺序重上传和双副本并行重上传纳入同一符号体系;2) 理论分析每次上传对可达多项式阶数的提升规律;3) 在固定可训练电路族和参数预算下,通过仿真实验测试不同阶数目标函数(二次、四次、六次)上的性能;4) 在手写数字分类任务上评估重上传对门误差容忍度的影响;5) 使用决定系数(R²)作为评估指标,跨10个随机种子验证结果稳定性。

Result: 理论结果表明每次重上传将可达多项式阶数提升2阶。仿真实验显示:在四阶目标上,重上传将测试决定系数从0.689提升至0.883-0.975;在六阶目标上从0.337提升至0.642-0.783(所有10个种子均达到)。对于二次目标和手写数字分类,单次上传即为最优选择。每增加一次重上传,分类器对门误差的容忍度相应降低。

Conclusion: 数据重上传为振幅编码的变分量子模型提供了系统性的表达力提升机制,其收益与任务的多项式阶数直接相关。研究结果为量子机器学习模型设计者提供了明确指导:应根据任务的多项式复杂度匹配重上传次数,避免过度重上传导致的门资源浪费和误差敏感性增加。该工作为量子模型的资源效率优化提供了理论基础和实践依据。

Abstract: Amplitude embedding lets a variational quantum model read many classical features with few qubits, but the model output is then only a quadratic form in the normalized input. Data re-uploading, which encodes the input repeatedly between trainable blocks, can be used to enrich such a model. Because every additional upload of an amplitude embedding repeats a costly state preparation, the gain that an upload buys has to be known before the gates are spent. This tutorial thus addresses the problem of explaining what data re-uploading adds to an amplitude-embedded model and how large the gain is. We express sequential re-uploading and parallel re-uploading with two copies in one notation and show that each upload raises the reachable polynomial order by two. Under one trainable circuit family and one budget of trainable parameters, simulation results show that re-uploading raises the test coefficient of determination from 0.689 to between 0.883 and 0.975 on targets of order four and from 0.337 to between 0.642 and 0.783 on targets of order six, on all ten seeds. On a quadratic target and on handwritten digits, one upload remains the best choice, and every additional upload lowers the gate error that the classifier tolerates. These results help designers match the number of uploads to the polynomial order of the task at hand.

</details>


### [138] [Emergent Non-Biorthogonal Born Rule at Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17855)
*Wei-Wei Yang,Henning Schomerus,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从全局厄米系统出发,将动力学量子相变推广到物理子空间,为非厄米动力学中的左右本征态提供了明确的概率诠释,提出了非双正交Born规则,并识别出例外点动力学量子相变(EP-DQPTs)这一新现象类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米动力学具有不同的左右本征态,但其归一化自由度阻碍了直接的概率诠释。现有理论缺乏将非厄米量子力学的概率解释建立在厄米量子力学坚实基础上的方法,需要从标准Born规则出发对非厄米系统提供清晰的物理图像。

Method: 从遵循标准Born规则的全局厄米系统出发,将动力学量子相变(DQPTs)从参考态推广到物理子空间。通过将左右态分别对自身归一化,利用它们的相互重叠来确定可直接测量的动力学跃迁速率,从而建立非双正交Born规则框架。

Result: 建立了非厄米量子力学中左右态的明确概率诠释:当每个态对自身归一化时,其相互重叠决定了可直接获取的动力学跃迁速率。发现了由新兴的非双正交Born规则支配的新现象,并识别出一类例外点动力学量子相变(EP-DQPTs),在该相变点临界传播子的本征态发生简并。

Conclusion: 该理论成功地将非厄米量子力学的概率诠释建立在厄米量子力学的坚实基础上,通过子空间推广和非双正交Born规则为非厄米动力学提供了清晰的物理图像,揭示了例外点动力学量子相变等新物理现象,为非厄米量子系统的研究提供了严格的理论框架。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian dynamics possesses distinct left and right eigenstates, but their normalization freedom obscures a direct probabilistic interpretation.
  Starting from a globally Hermitian system governed by the standard Born rule, we generalize dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) from a reference state to a physical subspace.
  This generalization provides a clear probabilistic interpretation of left and right states: when each state is normalized to itself, their mutual overlap determines a directly accessible dynamical transition rate.
  We discuss the new phenomena governed by this emergent non-biorthogonal Born rule and further identify a class of exceptional-point DQPTs (EP-DQPTs), at which the eigenstates of the critical propagator coalesce.
  Our theory places the probabilistic interpretation of non-Hermitian quantum mechanics on the firm footing of Hermitian quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [139] [Development and experimental implementation of noise characterisation techniques on a superconducting quantum processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18000)
*Nelson M. M. Fernández,Francesco Grieco,Hetta Vappula,Stefano Mangini,Boris Sokolov,Elsi-Mari Borrelli,Matteo A. C. Rossi,Sergey N. Filippov,N. Walter Talarico*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对超导量子处理器开发并实现了高效准确的噪声表征方法，通过改进循环基准测试协议和扩展张量网络层析成像技术，成功绘制噪声结构图、跟踪噪声漂移，并在实际物理和化学电路中验证了噪声模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声表征不仅能够实现错误缓解，还能提供关于量子设备内在性能的宝贵信息，识别主要错误来源并约束噪声稳定性。然而，在具有特定硬件拓扑结构（如方形晶格）的超导处理器上部署此类方法需要兼顾效率和准确性，评估噪声稳定性，并验证重构的噪声模型。现有方法存在不可学习自由度导致的偏差问题，且需要针对特定设备布局进行优化。

Method: 1. 改进循环基准测试（cycle-benchmarking）协议：通过正则化代价函数减少不可学习自由度引起的偏差；2. 扩展张量网络层析成像方法：使其适配方形晶格设备布局，并通过重复层执行提高准确性；3. 从父噪声模型中提取近似子层噪声模型；4. 利用这些方法绘制噪声结构图、跟踪噪声漂移，并在基准物理电路和化学电路中验证学习到的噪声模型与实验观测的噪声估计的一致性。

Result: 成功在方形晶格拓扑的超导处理器上实现了高效准确的噪声表征。改进的循环基准测试协议有效降低了偏差，扩展的张量网络层析成像方法提高了噪声模型的准确性。通过噪声表征工具成功绘制了设备的噪声结构图，跟踪了噪声漂移现象，并在实际的物理和化学基准电路中验证了学习到的噪声模型与实验观测结果的一致性。

Conclusion: 本研究开发的噪声表征方法为超导量子处理器提供了有效的噪声诊断和验证工具。研究结果不仅为底层技术的进一步改进提供了指导信息，还能够估算设备可支持的噪声缓解电路的规模上限，对量子计算设备的性能优化和实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Noise characterisation not only enables error mitigation but also provides valuable information about the intrinsic performance of a quantum device, identifying dominant error contributions and bounding noise stability well beyond the aggregated calibration data. Deploying such methods on a specific hardware topology must combine efficiency and accuracy, assess noise stability, and validate the reconstructed noise model. Here we develop and implement these methods on a superconducting processor with square-lattice topology. First, we revise the cycle-benchmarking protocol and reduce the bias arising from unlearnable degrees of freedom via a regularised cost function. Second, we extend a previously developed tensor-network tomography method to match the device layout and show how its accuracy can be improved by repeated layer execution. Both methods allow approximate sublayer noise models to be extracted from parent ones. Using noise characterisation as a tool, we map the noise structure, track drift, and validate the adequacy of the learnt noise models against experimentally observed noisy estimations in benchmark physical and chemistry circuits. These results inform further improvements in the underlying technology and provide estimates of the size of noise-mitigated circuits that the device can support.

</details>


### [140] [Ergotropy of multipartite entangled states in the quantum atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18028)
*Ruopu Sun,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwarzschild黑洞量子大气中三体GHZ态和W态的ergotropy（可提取功），揭示了两类多体纠缠态在Hawking热化效应下表现出本质不同的可提取功结构：GHZ态的ergotropy分为两个简并族，而W态遵循线性平均关系；Hartle-Hawking常数和归一化径向距离对可提取功的演化起竞争作用，为弯曲时空中区分GHZ型和W型量子关联提供了热力学特征标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弯曲时空背景下，特别是黑洞环境中，量子纠缠态的热力学性质会受到引力效应的影响。现有研究缺乏对不同类型多体纠缠态（GHZ态和W态）在黑洞Hawking辐射诱导热化过程中可提取功（ergotropy）重新分配机制的系统性理解。需要研究多体纠缠结构本身如何影响弯曲时空中的热力学行为，以及这种影响能否成为区分不同量子关联类型的物理标志。

Method: 采用量子热力学中的ergotropy理论框架，分析Schwarzschild黑洞量子大气中三体GHZ态和W态的可提取功。具体方法包括：(1)计算不同可观测子系统的ergotropy分布；(2)分析Hawking热化效应对ergotropy结构的影响；(3)研究Hartle-Hawking常数D_HH和归一化径向距离r/r_h两个参数对可提取功演化的作用；(4)在对称引力热修正条件下比较GHZ态和W态的ergotropy特性差异。

Result: 研究发现：(1)GHZ态和W态展现出本质不同的ergotropy结构——GHZ态的ergotropy按不同可观测子系统组织成两个简并族，而W态遵循无GHZ对应物的线性平均关系；(2)在对称引力热修正下，GHZ态的两个ergotropy族之间保持恒定能隙，该能隙与径向位置无关；(3)Hartle-Hawking常数D_HH的增加抑制可提取功向渐近Hawking极限的恢复，而归一化径向距离r/r_h的增加则增强观测者可获得的ergotropy；(4)这两个参数在可提取功演化中起竞争作用。

Conclusion: 黑洞环境中可提取功的重新分配不仅受引力诱导热化的控制，还受底层多体纠缠结构的本质影响。GHZ态和W态表现出的ergotropy差异为弯曲时空中区分这两类量子关联提供了热力学特征标志。这一结果揭示了量子信息的拓扑结构（纠缠类型）与相对论性热力学效应（Hawking辐射）之间的深层联系，对理解极端引力环境中的量子资源演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the ergotropy of tripartite Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) and W states in the quantum atmosphere of a Schwarzschild black hole, focusing on how the extractable work is redistributed under Hawking-induced thermalization. We find that the two classes of multipartite entanglement exhibit qualitatively different ergotropy structures. For GHZ states, the ergotropies associated with different accessible subsystems organize into two degenerate families, whereas for W states they obey a linear averaging relation with no counterpart in the GHZ case. Under symmetric gravitational thermal corrections, the two GHZ families remain separated by a constant energy gap that is independent of the radial position. We further show that the Hartle-Hawking constant $D_{HH}$ and the normalized radial distance $r/r_h$ play competing roles in the evolution of extractable work: increasing $D_{HH}$ suppresses its recovery toward the asymptotic Hawking limit, while increasing $r/r_h$ enhances the ergotropy accessible to the observers. These results show that the redistribution of extractable work in a black-hole environment is governed not only by gravitationally induced thermalization but also by the structure of the underlying multipartite entanglement, providing a thermodynamic signature that distinguishes GHZ- and W-type quantum correlations in curved spacetime.

</details>


### [141] [Lossless Reductions and Hybrid Sampling for Phylogeny Using the Maximum Compatibility Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18029)
*Orson Ye,James Bonfield,Joshua Cudby,Richard Durbin,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对系统发育重建中的最大兼容性问题，提出了基于图约简和量子采样的增强算法。通过将问题转化为补图上的最大权重独立集枚举，结合无损图约简技术和量子近似优化算法（QAOA），在保持精确重建的同时有效解决了枚举瓶颈，并在SARS-CoV-2序列数据上验证了方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大兼容性系统发育重建方法（如Cherry算法）在处理最优解集合枚举时存在计算瓶颈。传统方法将问题表示为加权图上的最大权重团问题，但在实际应用中，找到单个最优解与恢复完整最优解集合之间存在显著差异。此外，量子计算在组合优化问题上的潜在优势尚未在该领域得到充分探索，特别是在含噪声设备上的应用需要重新审视传统的参数选择策略。

Method: 1. 问题重构：将最大兼容字符集问题转化为补图上的最大权重独立集枚举问题；2. 图约简技术：引入无损图约简方法及相应的重建规则，确保保留所有最优解；3. 量子采样：构建定制的校正循环量子近似优化算法（QAOA）实例，对采样的比特串进行确定性后处理以映射到最大可行解；4. 参数优化：针对含噪声设备上的枚举问题，重新评估传统拉格朗日乘数的选择策略；5. 混合方法：结合图约简和采样技术共同处理枚举问题，在残差最优集完全解析时保持精确重建能力。

Result: 经典基准测试表明，找到单个最优解与恢复完整最优解集合在实际性能上存在明显差异。图约简和采样技术的结合有效缓解了最大兼容性系统发育中的枚举瓶颈。在SARS-CoV-2序列构建的图上进行的实验验证了该方法的实用价值。研究还发现，对于含噪声量子设备上的枚举问题，传统的拉格朗日乘数选择可能并非最优。当残差最优集完全解析时，方法能够保持精确重建。

Conclusion: 本文成功增强了Cherry的最大兼容性方法，通过引入图约简技术和量子采样显著改善了算法性能。研究证明了图约简与采样技术结合的有效性，为解决系统发育重建中的枚举难题提供了新途径。在保证精确性的前提下，该混合方法在实际生物序列数据（如SARS-CoV-2）上展现出良好的应用前景，为量子计算在系统发育学中的应用探索了可行方向。

Abstract: Maximum compatibility provides a criterion for phylogenetic reconstruction in which the objective is to retain a maximum set of mutually compatible characters. For binary characters, pairwise compatibility can be represented by a weighted graph whose vertices correspond to characters and whose edges connect compatible pairs. A leading algorithm by Cherry [BMC Bioinformatics, 18(1):127, 2017] uses this representation to identify maximum compatible character sets as maximum weight cliques.
  We formulate the problem as maximum weight independent set enumeration on the complement graph and use deterministic postprocessing to map sampled bit strings to maximal feasible solutions. To explore the prospects of quantum computational advantage, we further construct bespoke correction-in-the-loop Quantum Approximate Optimisation Algorithm instances and find that traditional Lagrange multiplier choices may not be optimal for the enumeration problem on noisy devices.
  We also introduce lossless graph reductions with reconstruction rules that preserve the complete set of optimal solutions. Our results indicate that graph reduction and sampling can be effectively combined to address the enumeration bottleneck in maximum compatibility phylogeny, while retaining exact reconstruction when the residual optimum set is completely resolved.
  We enhance Cherry's approach to maximum compatibility by introducing graph reduction techniques and quantum sampling. Our classical benchmarks show a clear difference between finding one optimum and recovering the optimum set in practice. We demonstrate the utility of our methods on graphs constructed from SARS-CoV-2 sequences.

</details>


### [142] [Optimal Lower Bound on the Sample Complexity of Coherent Classical Shadows for Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18031)
*Pavel Svetlichnyy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未知量子态的相干经典阴影协议，证明了在给定错误概率和可观测量数量下所需副本数的下界，该下界几乎匹配已知上界，并通过高斯提升噪声核和Schur-Weyl结构实现了对任意相干测量的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的经典阴影协议研究主要关注独立测量，而对允许在所有副本上进行任意联合测量的相干协议缺乏紧致的理论下界。为了理解相干测量的真实代价和协议的最优性能极限，需要建立与已知上界相匹配的下界理论。

Method: 采用受布尔隐藏匹配问题下界证明启发的框架，开发了高斯提升噪声核技术，将前向和反向高斯超压缩性转移到Grassmann流形上。利用Schur-Weyl结构和后验矩界来控制源端的任意相干测量。通过辅助博弈中揭示查询可观测量来界定区分优势，从而推导出所需副本数的下界。

Result: 在温和假设log(2M)≲d·min{r,F}下，证明了副本数下界T=Ω(min{√(rFℓ)/ε, (Fℓ)^(2/3)/ε^(4/3)} + ℓ/ε²)，其中ℓ=max{log(2M), log(1/δ)}。该下界几乎匹配已知上界，并与已知上界的猜想强化版本完全匹配，表明现有协议接近最优。

Conclusion: 本文为相干经典阴影协议建立了接近紧致的理论下界，证明了在考虑任意联合相干测量时，协议所需的副本数存在基本限制。通过创新的高斯提升技术和Schur-Weyl结构分析，成功处理了相干测量带来的技术挑战，为量子态学习和经典阴影理论提供了重要的理论基础，明确了协议性能的理论极限。

Abstract: We study coherent classical shadows protocols for an unknown $d$-dimensional quantum state of rank at most $r$, allowing an arbitrary joint measurement on all available copies. For observables with operator norm at most one and centered squared Frobenius norm at most $F$, we study the minumum number of copies $T$ needed for the protocol to successed with propbability $1 - δ$. We prove a lower bound on $T$, that depends both on $δ$ and the number $M$ of post-measurement observables. Denoting $\ell=\max\{\log(2M),\log(1/δ)\}$, we show that $T=Ω\left(\min\left\{\frac{\sqrt{rF\ell}}{\varepsilon},\frac{(F\ell)^{2/3}}{\varepsilon^{4/3}}\right\}+\frac{\ell}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ under the mild assumption $\log(2M)\lesssim d\min\{r,F\}$. The lower bound nearly matches the known upper bound and matches the conjectural strengthening of the known upper bound.
  The proof is inspired by the framework of the proof of the lower bound for Boolean Hidden Matching. We develop a Gaussian-lift noise kernel that transfers forward and reverse Gaussian hypercontractivity to the Grassmannian, allowing us to bound the distinguishing advantage in an auxiliary game with a revealed query observable. We use Schur--Weyl structure and posterior moment bounds to control arbitrary coherent measurements on the source side.

</details>


### [143] [Scalable long-distance quantum fan-out gates with reduced resource overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18049)
*Wen-Qiang Liu,Hai-Rui Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在空间分离节点间实现n量子比特扇出门的高效方案,通过预共享GHZ态和局域操作,显著减少了量子门资源需求,并设计了两种光学实现架构,为实用化远程量子计算铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展的远程量子门对于分布式量子计算和可扩展量子网络至关重要,但现有基于标准分解的方法需要大量的量子门资源。因此需要开发更高效的方案来减少资源消耗,特别是CNOT门和Hadamard门的数量,以实现实用化的远程量子计算。

Method: 提出了一种高效的n量子比特扇出门(C^n门,即受控多重NOT门)实现方案。该方案仅需要:(1)预共享一个n量子比特的GHZ(Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger)纠缠态;(2)n个局域CNOT门;(3)前馈操作。此外,还开发了两种光学架构分别实现预示型和后选择型的n光子扇出门。

Result: 与基于标准分解的方法相比,该方案将CNOT门和Hadamard门的数量均减少了(n-2)个。所提出的光学实现方案需要更少的单光子资源,并实现了比基于分解方法更高的成功概率,代表了显著的改进。

Conclusion: 该方法在实验上是可行的,通过显著降低量子门资源需求和提高成功概率,为实现实用化的远程量子计算铺平了道路,对分布式量子计算和可扩展量子网络的发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: Scalable long-distance quantum gates are essential for distributed quantum computation and scalable quantum networks. We propose an efficient scheme for implementing an $n$-qubit fan-out gate $C^n$ (also known as a controlled-NOT-$\cdots$-NOT gate) across spatially separated parties. Our protocol requires only a pre-shared $n$-qubit Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state, $n$ local controlled-NOT (CNOT) gates, and feed-forward operations, thereby reducing both the CNOT and Hadamard gate counts by $(n-2)$ when compared with the standard decomposition-based approach. We further develop two optical architectures to implement the heralded and post-selected $n$-photon fan-out gates, respectively. These schemes require fewer single photons resources and achieve higher success probabilities than their decomposition-based counterparts, representing significant improvements. The results indicate that our approach is experimentally feasible and pave the way toward practical long-distance quantum computation.

</details>


### [144] [Local error correction prolongs time-crystalline memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18124)
*Benedikt Fauseweh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超导量子处理器上利用随机局部比特翻转纠错延长了二维Ising模型离散时间晶体的寿命，通过距离为3和5的重复码在蜂窝晶格上实现了记忆衰减时间提升达2倍，证明了局部纠错可以有效保护时间晶体的时空有序性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间晶体作为具有时空有序性的非平衡物质相，其寿命在实验中受到环境耦合的严重限制。虽然理论上相互作用、空间无序和相关随机驱动可以稳定这种有序性，但实验实现中时间晶体响应的快速衰减是亟需解决的关键问题，因此需要探索量子纠错技术来延长时间晶体的记忆时间。

Method: 研究使用超导量子处理器实现驱动的空间无序二维Ising模型，采用随机局部比特翻转纠错方案。具体方法包括：(1)在蜂窝晶格上构建距离为3和5的重复码，使用多达68个物理量子比特；(2)引入辅助量子比特进行中间电路的syndrome测量；(3)通过解码和条件纠正实现随机驱动；(4)分析自旋构型分布以理解纠错对空间有序性和键不一致性的影响。

Result: 随机驱动使记忆衰减时间延长了最多2倍。在晚期时间观察到恢复操作能够稳定整个晶格的空间有序性。自旋构型分布分析表明，纠错抑制了不一致键的产生，同时拓宽了完全反转态的占据。仅进行syndrome测量而不进行纠正会加速记忆衰减，证明了条件纠正的重要性。局部比特翻转纠错使时间晶体记忆能够保持的驱动周期数超过了裸动力学系统。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了局部比特翻转纠错可以有效延长量子处理器上离散时间晶体的寿命，通过条件纠正机制成功抑制环境耦合导致的退相干。结果表明量子纠错技术是保护非平衡量子相的有力工具，为在噪声量子设备上实现更长寿命的时间晶体和其他非平衡量子物态提供了可行路径，推动了量子模拟和量子计算在复杂多体系统研究中的应用。

Abstract: Discrete time crystals are non-equilibrium phases of matter with spatiotemporal order and a response that breaks the time-translation symmetry of a periodic external drive. Interactions, spatial disorder and correlated random driving can in theory stabilize this order. In experiments, however, coupling to the environment limits the time crystal lifetime. Here we use stochastic local bit-flip error correction to prolong the time-crystalline response of a driven, spatially disordered two-dimensional Ising model on a superconducting quantum processor, using a repetition code with distances three and five on a honeycomb lattice with up to 68 physical qubits. Even though ancilla qubits, midcircuit syndrome measurements and decoding increase the required quantum and classical resources, the stochastic drive increases the memory decay time by a factor of up to two. At late times we observe that recovery stabilizes spatial order across the lattice. The spin-configuration distributions explain this separation, as recovery suppresses disagreeing bonds while broadening occupies completely reversed states. Syndrome measurements without correction accelerate the memory decay, demonstrating the importance of conditional correction. Our results show that local bit-flip error correction can preserve time-crystalline memory over more drive cycles than the bare dynamics.

</details>


### [145] [Distance Prediction for Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18130)
*Nadav Schneider,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了BB-Net，一种基于注意力机制的神经网络架构，用于预测双变量自行车(BB)量子纠错码的距离。该方法在测试集上达到96.39%的准确率，相比最先进的启发式算法计算效率提升了10^4倍，显著加速了量子纠错码的发现过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子低密度奇偶校验码(qLDPC)如双变量自行车码是实现低开销容错量子计算的重要途径，但计算其距离参数（决定纠错能力的关键指标）面临严重的计算瓶颈。现有的精确求解器和启发式搜索方法计算成本极高，严重限制了最优码的发现。因此需要一种高效的距离预测方法来突破这一瓶颈，加速量子纠错码的筛选和发现。

Method: 1) 构建了包含BB码及其距离上界的数据集，距离上界通过高预算启发式搜索获得；2) 设计了BB-Net，一种基于注意力机制的神经网络架构，专门用于预测BB码的距离；3) 在包含参考距离的测试集和具有精确认证距离的子集上评估模型性能；4) 将BB-Net集成到机器学习驱动的搜索管道中，用于大规模码空间的快速筛选。

Result: BB-Net在测试集参考距离上达到96.39%的预测准确率，显著优于最先进启发式方法的94.86%准确率，且计算时间缩短了10^4倍。在具有精确认证距离的子集上，准确率进一步提升至98.94%。该方法成功缓解了距离计算的瓶颈问题，使大规模码空间的快速筛选成为可能。

Conclusion: BB-Net通过深度学习方法有效解决了BB量子码距离计算的效率问题，在保持高准确率的同时实现了数量级的速度提升。这项工作为机器学习驱动的量子纠错码搜索管道提供了关键技术，能够加速新型量子纠错码的发现，对推进可扩展容错量子计算具有重要意义。该方法展示了将神经网络应用于量子编码理论中计算密集型问题的潜力。

Abstract: The distance of a quantum code is a fundamental metric that dictates its error-correction capability. While quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) families such as Bivariate Bicycle (BB) codes offer a promising pathway to low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computing, computing their distance is challenging. Current exact solvers and even search heuristics that provide a distance upper bound suffer from severe computational bottlenecks, restricting the discovery of optimal codes. In this paper, we construct a dataset of BB codes with corresponding distance upper bounds obtained through high-budget heuristic searches. We introduce BB-Net, an attention-based neural network architecture for predicting the distance of BB codes. It achieves 96.39% accuracy on the reference distances in the test set, compared with 94.86% for state-of-the-art heuristics with a budget of up to $10^{4}$ times more computational runtime. On a subset with certified exact distances, BB-Net achieves 98.94%. By relieving the distance-calculation bottleneck, BB-Net enables faster screening of large code spaces, particularly in ML-driven search pipelines, and thereby can accelerate the discovery of novel quantum error-correcting codes, an important capability for advancing scalable fault-tolerant quantum computing. Our code is publicly available: https://github.com/NadavSc/BBNet

</details>


### [146] [Theory of Trotter Error with Variable-Weight Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18202)
*Henrique Ennes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"调度Trotterization"方法,通过允许局部线性分解系数在不同步骤中变化,将传统固定权重Trrotterization的全局误差从O(N^-1)改进到O(N^-2),为量子演化模拟中的Trotter误差分析提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Trotterization算法使用固定权重的局部线性分解来近似目标幺正演化,存在固有的全局累积误差。现有方法未能充分利用权重可调节性来减少误差,且缺乏将内部Trotter误差与外部累积误差分离的系统性框架。需要一种新方法来显著降低全局近似误差,同时保持算法的基本脉冲结构不变。

Method: 1) 引入调度Trotterization框架,允许局部线性分解的系数在不同步骤中变化;2) 利用幺正算子李群的几何性质,将全局端点误差表示为传输局部缺陷的乘积;3) 通过两次应用Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式(一次在局部步骤内部,一次在不同传输缺陷之间)获得误差的主导项,从而分离内部Trotter误差和外部累积误差;4) 基于该框架提出变权重调度方案;5) 将框架扩展到不完美的无穷小合成情况,使用局部覆盖参数度量可用系数集的质量;6) 通过随机SU(2)目标的数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: 1) 在权重固定时,重现了最近关于全局近似误差更紧界的结果;2) 对于精确无穷小合成,证明了变权重调度将传统固定权重的O(N^-1)误差行为改进到O(N^-2),且无需改变基本脉冲骨架;3) 在不完美无穷小合成(如量子门合成问题)中,误差由局部覆盖参数控制;4) 随机SU(2)目标的数值实验进一步支持了理论预测的改进;5) 验证了将Trotter误差视为可调度量并在端点相干抵消的新思路的有效性。

Conclusion: 调度Trotterization为量子演化模拟提供了一种新的误差分析和控制范式。通过允许局部分解系数随步骤变化,该方法在不改变算法基本结构的前提下,将全局误差从O(N^-1)显著改进到O(N^-2)。基于李群几何的误差分解框架不仅提供了更紧的误差界,还揭示了Trotter误差可以作为可调度量在端点进行相干抵消的本质。该框架对精确和不完美的无穷小合成均适用,为量子算法设计和量子门合成问题提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: Trotterization is a widely used algorithm for simulating quantum evolutions and consists of approximating a target unitary through a local linear decomposition of its generator that is repeated over $N$ steps. We introduce {scheduled trotterization}, a variable-weight modification in which the coefficients of the local linear decomposition are allowed to change from step to step. Using the geometry of the Lie group of unitary operators, we express the global endpoint error as a product of transported local defects, whose leading terms are obtained by two applications of the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula, one inside each local step and one between different transported defects, separating the internal Trotter error from the external accumulation error. While we recover recent tighter bounds on the global approximation error when the weights are held fixed, the new framework allows us to propose a weight-varying schedule that significantly decreases the global error. For exact infinitesimal synthesis, we prove that this improves the usual fixed-weight $O(N^{-1})$ behavior to $O(N^{-2})$, without changing the basic pulse skeleton. We also extend the framework to imperfect infinitesimal synthesis, as arises in quantum gate synthesis problems, where admissible coefficients may only approximate the desired local corrections. In this case, the error is controlled by a local covering parameter measuring the quality of the available coefficient set. Numerical experiments for random $\SU(2)$ targets further support the predicted theoretical improvements. The results suggest a novel perspective for the analysis of Trotter errors, which can be treated as schedulable quantities to be coherently canceled at the endpoint.

</details>


### [147] [Methods for Automated Equivalence Checking of Quantum Error-Correction Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18218)
*Maxie Bichmann,Ludwig Schmid,Robert Wille*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架和混合决策策略,用于自动判定量子纠错码在特定变换下的等价性,通过组合多种方法(群作用搜索、同构方法和约束求解)实现了高效的等价性检验,对于大多数n≤37的结构化码平均运行时间低于10秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码和电路构造是活跃的研究领域,需要自动化方法来识别等价的设计方案。现有技术缺乏统一的框架来处理不同类型的码等价性判定问题,且单一方法难以在所有场景下取得最优性能。因此需要开发系统化的自动判定策略来解决稳定子码和CSS码的置换等价性以及稳定子码的局部Clifford等价性问题。

Method: 1) 建立统一框架:将稳定子码和CSS码的置换等价性以及稳定子码的局部Clifford等价性形式化,区分物理自由度和表示自由度;2) 开发三种决策策略:群作用搜索、基于同构的方法和约束求解;3) 设计不变量和签名用于预处理;4) 构建输入依赖的混合检验器,采用分阶段的"反驳-精化-判定"(Refute-Refine-Decide)架构,组合不同方法的互补优势。

Result: 实验评估表明没有单一范式在所有等价性概念、码族和参数范围内占据主导地位。混合检验器对于大多数n≤37的结构化码案例,平均运行时间保持在10秒以下;对于n=144的非等价码实例,置换等价性检验约需310秒,局部Clifford等价性检验约需16秒。混合策略成功结合了不同方法的互补优势,在不同场景下展现出良好的性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了量子纠错码等价性判定的自动化方法体系,通过统一框架整合了置换等价性和局部Clifford等价性的判定问题。混合决策架构有效克服了单一方法的局限性,在实际码族上实现了高效的等价性检验。该工作为量子纠错码的自动化分析和优化提供了实用工具,对量子计算和量子信息处理领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Quantum error-correction code and circuit construction is an active field of research, motivating automated methods for identifying equivalent designs. We develop and evaluate automated strategies for deciding whether two descriptions of quantum error-correction codes are equivalent under specified transformations. We formalize permutation equivalence for stabilizer and CSS codes and local-Clifford equivalence for stabilizer codes within a unified framework that separates physical from representation freedom. We develop three decision strategies (group-action search, isomorphism-based methods, and constraint solving), together with invariants and signatures for preprocessing. The empirical evaluation shows that no paradigm dominates across equivalence notions, code families, and parameter ranges. We therefore combine their complementary strengths in input-dependent hybrid checkers following a staged Refute-Refine-Decide architecture. Mean hybrid runtimes remain below 10 s for most evaluated structured-code cases with $n \leq 37$; for inequivalent code instances with $n=144$, they are about 310 s for permutation and 16 s for local-Clifford equivalence.

</details>


### [148] [PPVM: A Circuit Simulation Framework for Digital Twins of Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18225)
*Xiu-Zhe Luo,David Plankensteiner,Kai-Hsin Wu,Rob Patterson,Alexander Schuckert,Oriol Rubies-Bigorda,Refaat Ismail,Jonhas Colina,Neelay Fruitwala,Danylo Lykov,Tyson Jones,Stefan Krastanov,Rafael Haenel,Stefan Ostermann,Sheng-Tao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了可编程泡利虚拟机(PPVM)，这是一个用于量子处理器数字孪生的开源框架，统一了泡利传播和广义表列模拟，支持任意经典控制流和测量条件前馈，并在中性原子硬件上演示了超过千个物理量子比特的容错魔术态蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算机接近早期容错阶段，验证下一代量子硬件需要联合模拟量子处理器、硬件噪声以及经典控制和解码逻辑。现有工具缺乏统一的框架来同时处理这些复杂的模拟需求，特别是在大规模物理量子比特系统中进行容错量子计算的仿真验证。

Method: 开发了可编程泡利虚拟机(PPVM)框架，该框架在单一虚拟机内统一了泡利传播和广义表列模拟两种方法。其字节码支持任意经典控制流，广义表列后端支持基于测量的条件前馈。针对中性原子硬件，推导了独立和相关的原子损失信道，并将其集成到两个后端中。通过Bloqade QEC Studio实现了与经典解码的集成。

Result: 成功在超过一千个物理量子比特上演示了损失感知的魔术态蒸馏(loss-aware magic state distillation)，实现了与经典解码逻辑的有效集成，验证了PPVM框架在大规模量子系统数字孪生模拟中的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: PPVM为早期容错量子计算机的验证提供了一个强大的开源工具，通过统一的抽象层和模块化设计，能够有效模拟量子处理器、硬件噪声和经典控制逻辑的协同工作。该框架特别适用于中性原子等新型量子硬件平台的大规模仿真，为推进容错量子计算的实际应用奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Quantum computers are approaching the early fault-tolerant regime. Verifying the next generation of quantum hardware requires joint emulation of the quantum processor, hardware noise, and classical control and decoding logic. We introduce the Programmable Pauli Virtual Machine (PPVM), an open-source framework for digital twins of quantum processors. PPVM unifies Pauli propagation and generalized tableau simulation under a common abstraction within a single virtual machine. Its bytecode supports arbitrary classical control flow, with measurement-conditioned feed-forward available in the generalized tableau backend. For neutral-atom hardware, we derive independent and correlated atom-loss channels and integrate them into both backends. We demonstrate loss-aware magic state distillation on over a thousand physical qubits and integration with classical decoding through Bloqade QEC Studio.

</details>


### [149] [Resource-based reliability of quantum-coherent devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18236)
*Bowen Sun,D. L. Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于资源的可靠性框架，用于量化量子设备在未进入固定故障子空间时仍保留的任务相关资源容量，并通过相对熵相干性和对数l_1相干性分别分析了可信量子随机数生成器和校准双端口干涉仪的资源保留情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子设备可能在未进入固定故障子空间的情况下丧失执行特定功能所需的资源，传统的固定故障投影算符不足以评估此类情况下的可靠性。因此需要一种新的框架来量化设备相对于理想容量所保留的任务相关资源，并将保留的资源容量与经典生存概率区分开来。

Method: 提出基于资源的可靠性框架，通过资源度量与任务及允许操作的操作性联系来选择合适的资源测度。针对可信量子随机数生成器使用相对熵相干性来确定非相干操作下标准相干量子比特的最优渐近产率；针对校准双端口干涉仪使用对数l_1相干性量化条纹资源并确定其校准正确端口概率。采用可精确求解的多自旋-玻色子模型来说明环境记录如何在相同的孤立自旋退相位强度下影响这些资源，并通过有限记录模拟将资源定义与测量计数联系起来。

Result: 相对熵相干性决定了量子随机数生成器在非相干操作下的最优渐近产率；对数l_1相干性量化了干涉仪的条纹资源并确定其校准正确端口概率。多自旋-玻色子模型表明，集体噪声在乘积输入下保持磁化扇区内的相干性，但增强了GHZ分支相干性的衰减。有限记录模拟显示，增加样本量可以减少统计波动，但无法消除来自不正确多自旋噪声模型的偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种任务特定的可靠性评估途径，适用于固定故障投影算符不足的情况，同时将保留的资源容量与经典生存概率明确区分。该方法通过操作性定义的资源度量，能够量化量子设备在部分失效时仍保留的功能容量，为量子设备可靠性评估提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Quantum devices can lose the resource needed for a specified function without entering a fixed failure subspace. We propose a resource-based reliability framework for this situation, quantifying the remaining task-relevant resource relative to a stated ideal capacity. The resource measure is selected through its operational connection to the task and the allowed operations. We develop this approach for a trusted quantum random-number generator and a calibrated two-port interferometer. Relative-entropy coherence determines the optimal asymptotic yield of standard coherent qubits under incoherent operations for the former, while logarithmic $l_1$ coherence quantifies the fringe resource of the latter and determines its calibrated correct-port probability. An exactly solvable multi-spin spin-boson model illustrates how environmental records affect these resources at the same isolated-spin dephasing strength. Collective noise preserves coherence within magnetization sectors for product inputs, but enhances the decay of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger branch coherence. Finite-record simulations connect the resource definitions to measurement counts and show that increasing the sample size reduces statistical fluctuations without eliminating bias from an incorrect many-spin noise model. The resulting framework provides a task-specific route to reliability assessment when a fixed failure projector is insufficient, while keeping retained resource capacity distinct from a classical survival probability.

</details>


### [150] [Towards an autonomous optomechanical Maxwell Demon engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18237)
*Sander Stammbach,Giulio Gasbarri,Stefan Nimmrichter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个全自主的机械麦克斯韦妖引擎模型,通过阻尼机械振子作为指针实现信息获取和反馈,将量子比特吸收的热量转化为量子电池的布居数反转,无需外部测量和控制操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统麦克斯韦妖引擎需要外部测量和控制操作来实现信息获取和反馈,限制了其自主性和实用性。本研究旨在构建一个完全自主的麦克斯韦妖引擎模型,通过物理机制内在地实现妖的功能,从而将热能有效转化为量子电池中的可用能量。

Method: 采用Holstein型哈密顿量使阻尼机械振子的平衡位置依赖于量子比特状态,从而诱导状态依赖的共振频移。机械振子充当指针,自主完成对量子比特状态的信息获取和反馈。通过这种机制实现向自旋系统(量子电池)的选择性能量转移。推导了耦合系统的局域和全局Lindblad主方程,并识别引擎高效运行的参数区间。

Result: 成功建立了全自主麦克斯韦妖引擎的理论模型,该模型能够将热量转化为量子电池的稳态布居数反转。通过ergotropy(遍历功)量化了电池的布居数反转作为性能指标,确定了引擎高效运行所需的参数范围,并分析了不同参数区间下的引擎性能表现。

Conclusion: 研究证明了通过Holstein型耦合和机械振子指针可以实现完全自主的麦克斯韦妖引擎,无需外部干预即可将热能转化为量子电池的可用能量。基于理论分析结果,讨论了在现实实验平台上实现该妖引擎的必要条件,为量子热机和量子电池的实际应用提供了新的理论基础和实现方案。

Abstract: We present a fully autonomous model of a mechanical Maxwell demon engine that converts heat absorbed by a qubit into population inversion in an attached quantum battery. A damped mechanical oscillator acts as a pointer that incorporates the essential demon function of information acquisition about the qubit state and subsequent feedback into the system dynamics, replacing external measurements and control operations. This is achieved by means of a Holstein-type Hamiltonian, which makes the pointer's equilibrium position depend on the qubit state and thereby induces a state-dependent resonance shift. The mechanism enables a selective energy transfer to an attached spin system that acts as the quantum battery. We derive both local and global Lindblad master equations for the coupled system and identify the parameter regimes in which the engine operates efficiently. The figure of merit is the resulting steady-state population inversion of the battery, quantified in terms of ergotropy. Based on these results, we discuss the necessary conditions for practical implementations of the demon engine on realistic experimental platforms.

</details>


### [151] [Experimental Benchmarking of a 256-Qubit Room-Temperature NV-Diamond Quantum Processor with an Optically Programmable Biomolecular Quantum Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18245)
*Adams Ivanov,Daniela Herrmann,Erick Giovani Sperandio Nascimento*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了Zeus-256，一个室温氮空位(NV)金刚石量子处理器，具有256个同时活跃的处理器级逻辑量子比特(16×16阵列)。每个逻辑量子比特结合NV电子自旋系综、核自旋存储器和辅助资源。通过光学可编程的专有蛋白质-生色团层实现可重构生物分子量子总线。基准测试涵盖QFT、GHZ纠缠、Grover搜索、算术运算、变分算法、神经网络、图态和Shor家族电路，成功分解了11个8-84比特整数，其中84比特端点使用256量子比特和超4000万个门，执行时间551.694毫秒，展示了室温量子计算的重要进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算面临的主要挑战包括：(1)大多数量子处理器需要极低温环境运行，成本高昂且难以规模化；(2)现有量子系统的逻辑量子比特数量有限，难以执行复杂的量子算法；(3)量子比特之间的长程交互和可重构连接能力不足；(4)缺乏在室温条件下实现大规模量子计算的实际演示。本研究旨在开发一个室温运行的大规模量子处理器，利用NV金刚石系统的优势，结合创新的生物分子量子总线技术，实现256个逻辑量子比特的同时操控，并通过多种基准测试验证系统性能，推动实用化量子计算的发展。

Method: Zeus-256系统采用以下核心技术方法：(1)硬件架构：构建16×16阵列的256个处理器级逻辑量子比特，每个逻辑量子比特由NV电子自旋系综、核自旋存储器和辅助资源组成；(2)控制系统：使用RFSoC控制器生成微波、射频、光学、时序、测量和总线配置序列，支持OpenQASM 2.0电路的转译；(3)量子总线：设计光学可编程的专有蛋白质-生色团层，利用内部亚稳态三重态自旋介质实现可重构的生物分子量子总线，提供局域和长程量子比特交互；(4)基准测试：实施多类量子算法测试，包括量子傅里叶变换(QFT)、GHZ态制备、Grover搜索、算术运算、变分算法、量子神经网络、图态和Shor家族因子分解电路；(5)性能评估：通过Bell-CHSH不等式、保真度测量和成功率统计评估系统的量子特性和计算能力。

Result: Zeus-256系统在多个基准测试中展示了显著性能：(1)因子分解：成功恢复11个整数(8-84比特)的因子，每个实例进行10次尝试；84比特分解使用全部256量子比特和40,250,342个门操作，报告执行时间为551.694毫秒；(2)量子纠缠验证：Bell-CHSH测量得到S=2.8008±0.0446，明显违反经典界限(S≤2)，证实量子纠缠；(3)Grover搜索：三量子比特Grover搜索在1,024次测量中958次返回标记态(成功率93.55%)，接近理论值94.53%；(4)GHZ态保真度：30,000次测量的种群-宇称协议显示GHZ+态固定相位保真度为99.8960%，估计标准误差0.2885个百分点，保守物理95%置信区间为[98.2342%, 100%]；(5)系统规模：当前实现256个处理器级逻辑量子比特，工程目标是扩展至9,984个逻辑量子比特。论文指出时序指标不完整，尚未确立量子优势。

Conclusion: Zeus-256成功演示了首个具有256个同时活跃逻辑量子比特的室温NV金刚石量子处理器，代表了量子计算硬件发展的重要里程碑。通过创新的蛋白质-生色团生物分子量子总线实现了可重构的量子比特交互，系统在多项基准测试中表现出良好性能，包括高保真度的GHZ态制备(99.8960%)、显著的Bell不等式违反(S=2.8008)以及接近理论极限的Grover搜索成功率(93.55%)。84比特整数分解的实现展示了系统执行复杂量子算法的能力。尽管当前时序指标尚不足以证明量子优势，但室温运行特性极大降低了量子计算的实施门槛，为实用化量子计算开辟了新路径。未来向9,984逻辑量子比特的扩展目标显示了该架构的可扩展性潜力，有望在量子模拟、优化和密码分析等领域发挥重要作用。

Abstract: We report Zeus-256, a room-temperature nitrogen-vacancy (NV) diamond processor with 256 simultaneously active processor-level logical qubits arranged in a 16 x 16 array. Each logical qubit combines an NV electron-spin ensemble with nuclear-spin memory and ancillary resources. OpenQASM 2.0 circuits are transpiled into RFSoC-controlled microwave, radio-frequency, optical, timing, measurement and bus-configuration sequences. An optically programmable proprietary protein-chromophore layer with an internal metastable-triplet spin mediator provides a reconfigurable biomolecular quantum bus for local and long-range interactions. Benchmarks cover QFT, GHZ entanglement, Grover search, arithmetic, variational, neural-network, graph-state and Shor-family circuits. Factor recovery is reported for 11 integers of 8-84 bits, with 10 attempts per instance; the 84-bit endpoint uses 256 qubits and 40,250,342 reported gates, with a reported execution time of 551.694 ms. Bell-CHSH measurements yield S = 2.8008 +/- 0.0446. Three-qubit Grover search returns the marked state in 958 of 1,024 shots (93.55%), compared with an ideal reference of 94.53%. A separate 30,000-shot population-and-parity protocol yields fixed-phase GHZ+ fidelity of 99.8960%, an estimated standard error of 0.2885 percentage points and a conservative physical 95% interval of [98.2342%, 100%]. Timing metrics are partial and do not establish quantum advantage. Scaling to 9,984 processor-level logical qubits is an engineering target, distinct from the demonstrated register.

</details>


### [152] [A complete quantum process model for physically realistic photo-detection in the joint photon-number and spectral degrees of freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18267)
*Sadia Rafi,Aeysha Khalique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完整的量子过程模型来描述物理上真实的光子探测，该模型通过Kraus算符同时考虑了光子数效应和频率相关的探测器响应特性，能够处理实际探测器的非理想性和有限光谱窗口，用于优化量子效应的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的光探测理论模型通常只在光子数基础上处理探测器，忽略了其他相关自由度（如光谱特性）。然而，实际探测器具有频率依赖的响应特性和各种非理想性，因此需要建立更完整、更贴近物理现实的光子探测量子过程模型，以准确描述探测器行为并优化量子光学实验中量子效应的可观测性。

Method: 采用算符和表示（operator-sum representation）框架，通过显式推导Kraus算符来构建量子过程模型。该模型同时纳入了：(1)光子数效应；(2)频率相关的探测器响应函数；(3)实际探测器的非理想效率；(4)有限光谱窗口内的探测过程。该形式化方法可以直接替代量子光学分析中的理想化探测器模型。

Result: 建立了包含光子数和频谱特性的完整光子探测量子过程模型，明确给出了Kraus算符的具体形式。该模型能够准确描述实际探测器在有限光谱窗口内的响应行为，并可处理探测器的各种非理想性（如量子效率、暗计数等）。模型可直接应用于实际量子光学系统分析。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个更完整、更符合物理实际的光子探测理论框架，弥补了传统模型仅考虑光子数而忽略频谱特性的不足。通过该模型可以识别和优化探测器的合适探测窗口与入射光子光谱分布之间的匹配关系，从而最大化量子效应的可观测性，为实验光子学和量子光学实验设计提供了重要的理论工具。

Abstract: Photo-detection is a fundamental component of experimental photonics, yet theoretical models often treat detectors solely in the photon-number basis, neglecting other relevant degrees of freedom such as spectral characteristics. We present a complete quantum process model for physically realistic photodetection that incorporates both photon-number effects and frequency-dependent detector response, described through explicitly derived Kraus operators in the operator-sum representation. This formalism accommodates realistic inefficiencies, models detection within finite spectral windows, and can be directly substituted for idealized models in quantum optical analyses. The model leads to identifying the suitable detection window of the detectors relative to the spectral distribution of incident photons and conversely, in order to maximize the observability of quantum effects.

</details>


### [153] [V-type Atomic Variance Squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18362)
*Chenrui Bi,Hong-Mei Zou,Zhao-Yang Tong,Zijin Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了V型原子与耗散腔相互作用的方差压缩特性，发现原子方差压缩在Sx和Sy之间交替切换，且Sx分量的压缩深度仅由原子初态参数决定，弱腔-环境耦合和原子-腔失谐可显著增强Sx方差压缩的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子光学系统中，环境噪声会导致方差压缩随时间消失，影响高精度量子通信应用。因此需要研究V型原子与耗散腔相互作用系统中方差压缩的产生机制及其受初态参数和系统参数的影响规律，以寻找增强方差压缩鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 采用理论分析方法研究V型原子与耗散腔相互作用的方差压缩现象。首先探讨原子初态参数（特别是参数α）对Sx和Sy两个正交分量方差压缩的影响；然后系统分析腔-环境耦合强度、原子-腔失谐、SGI参数等系统参数对方差压缩深度和鲁棒性的作用规律。

Result: 研究结果表明：(1) 原子方差压缩在Sx和Sy之间交替切换，切换规律由初态参数α（从-π到π）控制，两个压缩区间关于α=π/4对称，各占π/2宽度；(2) 当α=π/14时，仅Sx分量产生方差压缩，但因环境噪声会随时间消失；(3) Sx的光学方差压缩深度完全由原子初态参数决定，与系统参数无关；(4) 弱腔-环境耦合和原子-腔失谐能显著增强Sx方差压缩的鲁棒性；(5) SGI参数对方差压缩影响极其微弱。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了V型原子-耗散腔系统中方差压缩的产生和演化规律，明确了原子初态参数对压缩分量选择和深度的决定性作用，以及系统参数对压缩鲁棒性的调控机制。研究发现通过优化腔-环境耦合强度和原子-腔失谐可有效对抗环境噪声影响，这些结果为高精度量子通信应用中实现稳定的方差压缩提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Variance squeezing of a V-type atom interacting with a dissipative cavity is investigated. First, the influence of atomic initial state parameters on the variance squeezing is explored. The results show that the atomic variance squeezing switches alternately between Sx and Sy as the initial state parameter alpha sweeps from -pi to pi. The two squeezing intervals are symmetric about alpha = pi/4, each with a width of pi/2. For alpha = pi/14, only the Sx component can generate variance squeezing and will eventually disappear with time evolution due to unavoidable environmental noise. Then, the influence of system parameters on the variance squeezing is also studied. It is found that, the optical variance squeezing depth of Sx is determined solely by the atomic initial parameters and is independent of the system parameters. Both weak cavity-environment coupling and atom-cavity detuning can significantly enhance the robustness of the Sx variance squeezing. The SGI parameter has an extremely weak influence on variance squeezing. These results are beneficial for high-precision quantum communication applications.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond Gaussian Entanglement: Practical Criteria for Polynomially Excited Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18365)
*Gábor Homa,Richárd Balka,Nikolett Német,András Bodor,Tamás Kiss,András Csordás*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由产生湮灭算符构成的多项式激发对多体高斯态子系统纠缠的影响,证明了NPT纠缠在多项式激发下的保持性,并建立了无限维正定性问题到有限维判据的约化方法,为纠缠动力学的理论分析和实验实现提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子系统中子系统间的纠缠在量子激发操作下如何演化是量子信息和量子光学的核心问题。现有研究缺乏对多项式激发(特别是二次激发)如何影响NPT(负部分转置)纠缠的系统性理解,且无限维系统的纠缠判定面临数学上的困难。本文旨在建立多项式激发与纠缠动力学之间的严格联系,并发展可处理的分析工具。

Method: 采用算符代数方法分析多项式激发对高斯态协方差矩阵的作用;利用部分转置操作和双线性表示矩阵将无限维正定性问题约化为有限维判据;通过辛对角化和谱分解构造Fock空间与位置表示之间的显式变换;针对三模混合高斯态的二次激发,引入非局域性参数κ追踪NPT纠缠的演化并计算量子负性;推导任意自由度相空间中一般二次算符协方差矩阵的闭式解析表达式。

Result: 证明了多项式激发保持多体高斯态任意两个子系统间已存在的NPT纠缠,且可在初始可分离子系统间产生新的NPT纠缠,使得NPT纠缠对数目单调不减;对一大类初始可分离纯态,成功将纠缠判定转化为双线性表示矩阵的正半定性检验;在三模系统中观察到在二次哈密顿量诱导的幺正演化下,NPT纠缠在长时间区间内保持,仅在短暂周期内出现PPT态;获得了相空间中一般二次算符协方差矩阵的通用解析公式。

Conclusion: 多项式激发是研究多体量子系统纠缠动力学的有力工具,其保持NPT纠缠并可产生新纠缠的性质为纠缠资源的操控提供了理论保障。约化到有限维判据的方法解决了连续变量系统纠缠判定的关键难题。构造的Fock-位置表示变换和协方差矩阵解析公式为实验实现提供了直接计算工具。该框架统一了数学基础、理论建模与实验可行性,为量子光学和量子信息处理中的纠缠工程开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Polynomial excitations consisting of creation and annihilation operators are studied. Subsystem entanglement is proven produced and preserved by such manipulations. NPT entanglement already present between any two subsystems of a general pure or mixed multipartite Gaussian state is preserved under all polynomial excitations. Nevertheless, these excitations can create NPT entanglement between initially separable subsystems, implying that the number of NPT entangled pairs cannot decrease. Polynomial excitations of a wide class of initially separable pure states are considered. For these states, the method reduces the infinite-dimensional positivity problem to a finite-dimensional one: after partial transposition, NPT entanglement is equivalent to a corresponding bilinear representation matrix not being positive semi-definite. Furthermore, using symplectic diagonalization and spectral decomposition, an explicit transformation is constructed between the Fock-space and position representations. It is applied to quadratic excitations of a three-mode mixed Gaussian state. The behavior of the NPT entanglement is tracked between subsystems as a function of a parameter $κ$, which tunes the non-locality of the excitation, and quantum negativity is evaluated. Under a chosen quadratic Hamiltonian, the resulting unitary time evolution maintains NPT entanglement for long intervals, interrupted only by short periods of PPT states. Finally, a closed analytical expression is derived for the covariance matrix of a general quadratic operator for an arbitrary number of degrees of freedom in phase space. Our framework directly connects mathematical foundations, theoretical modeling, and experimentally feasible implementations.

</details>


### [155] [Unequal Coherent Ergotropy at Equal Interferometric Visibility and Energy in Quantum-Controlled Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18474)
*Tonipe Anuradha,André H. A. Malavazi,Borhan Ahmadi,Lucas Pollyceno,Pedro R. Dieguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子系统中相干遍历功(coherent ergotropy)对路径可预测性的敏感性,通过Mach-Zehnder干涉仪中的量子比特热电池实验,证明干涉可见度并非衡量可提取功的完整指标,相干遍历功会受到路径可预测性的影响,且这种工作提取优势与控制器局部重置的Landauer成本相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遍历功(可单一提取的功)可分解为非相干和相干两部分贡献,但干涉可见度是否完整表征了可作为功提取的相干性尚不明确。研究者希望探究在相同干涉可见度、储存能量和非相干遍历功的情况下,不同量子干涉仪配置是否会产生不同的相干遍历功,以及这种差异与路径可预测性的关系。

Method: 采用Mach-Zehnder干涉仪实验方案,将热态制备的路径量子比特作为量子电池,通过两种量子控制干涉仪配置进行对比:一种是固定分束器在前、受控分束器在后;另一种顺序相反。两种配置使用相同元件(固定分束器和相干控制的分束器),仅改变作用顺序。在参数空间中比较两种配置的可见度、储存能量、非相干遍历功和相干遍历功。进一步分析退相干效应对电池的影响,并考察相对论性实现(Wigner旋转将粒子自旋与路径关联),通过追踪自旋自由度来研究退相干对相干遍历功的影响。

Result: 两种干涉仪配置在参数空间每个点上具有完全相同的可见度、储存能量和非相干遍历功,但相干遍历功存在差异。将受控分束器置于前端的配置在仅从输出电池提取功时,其相干遍历功始终不低于另一配置。在固定可见度和干涉相位条件下,相干遍历功的差异由该顺序产生的路径可预测性决定。这一表达式在分束器控制器上表现为纯度亏损,因此功提取优势伴随着控制器局部重置的Landauer边界成本,且在相同温度下该成本不会更大。在相对论性实现中,追踪自旋导致电池退相干,使得两种配置即使在非相对论性对应配置一致的参数区间内也能表现出不同的相干遍历功。

Conclusion: 干涉可见度不是衡量可作为功提取的相干性的完整指标。相干遍历功对路径可预测性敏感,而这种可预测性信息无法从输出干涉条纹中揭示。量子干涉仪元件的排列顺序会影响相干遍历功,这种工作提取优势与信息擦除的热力学成本(Landauer原理)紧密关联。研究结果揭示了量子相干性、路径信息和功提取之间的深层联系,为量子热力学和量子电池的研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The ergotropy, or unitarily extractable work, of a quantum system naturally splits into incoherent and coherent contributions, respectively set by population inversion and coherences in the energy eigenbasis. We show that this split makes the coherent ergotropy of a Mach--Zehnder qubit sensitive to path predictability that the output interference fringes do not reveal. We send the path qubit, prepared as a thermal quantum battery, through two quantum-controlled interferometers built from the same elements, a fixed beam splitter and a beam splitter coherently controlled to be present or absent, differing only in the order in which these two act. The two arrangements share identical visibility, stored energy, and incoherent ergotropy at every point in the parameter space. Yet, for work extracted from the output battery alone, placing the controlled beam splitter first never yields less coherent ergotropy, and at fixed visibility and interferometric phase the difference is governed by the path predictability that this ordering creates. The same expression reappears as a purity deficit on the beam-splitter controller, so the work advantage comes with a Landauer bound on the cost of locally resetting the controller that is never larger at the same temperature. We further examine the effects of decoherence on the battery and consider a relativistic realization in which a Wigner rotation correlates the particle's spin with its path. Tracing out the spin decoheres the battery and allows the two arrangements to exhibit different coherent ergotropies even in regimes where their non-relativistic counterparts coincide. These results show that interference visibility is not a complete measure of the coherence available as work.

</details>


### [156] [Experimental Realization of a C-Shunt Flux-Qubit Coupler for Controlled-Z Gates between Transmons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18477)
*Weiping Yuan,Silu Zhao,Li Li,Chenglin Deng,Zhenghe Liu,Yu Liu,Tianming Li,Mingchuan Wang,Bingjie Chen,Guihan Liang,Jiacheng Song,Jinming Guo,Shuaili Wang,Mingxuan Zhou,Xiaohui Song,Kai Xu,Heng Fan,Zhongcheng Xiang,Dongning Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验实现了一种transmon-C-shunt-transmon (TCT)器件，利用电容分流的磁通量子比特耦合器连接两个transmon数据量子比特，在近零残余ZZ相互作用的空闲区实现了99.7%以上的单量子比特门保真度，并通过45纳秒的非绝热CZ门达到99.23%的纠缠门保真度，展示了C-shunt耦合器在超导量子处理器中结合低相互作用空闲和快速纠缠操作的实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中快速纠缠门和低相互作用空闲状态需要不同的工作条件。现有的耦合器设计难以同时满足这两个需求。研究者希望开发一种新型耦合器架构，能够在空闲时提供近零残余ZZ相互作用以保持量子态相干性，同时在需要时能够实现快速高保真度的纠缠门操作，从而提升超导量子处理器的整体性能。

Method: 实验设计并制备了transmon-C-shunt-transmon (TCT)器件，其核心是采用磁通可调的三结电容分流磁通量子比特作为耦合器连接两个transmon数据量子比特。通过磁通调控，C-shunt耦合器提供正非谐性频谱来定制数据量子比特间的相互作用强度。利用条件Ramsey测量和交换测量识别出具有近零残余ZZ相互作用和抑制横向交换的空闲工作区。在此偏置点进行同步单量子比特交错随机基准测试以评估X门保真度。实现45纳秒的非绝热受控Z门并通过交错随机基准测试评估其保真度。使用数值模拟分析耦合器能量弛豫对CZ门保真度的影响，并预测延长耦合器寿命对门性能的改善效果。

Result: 在识别的空闲区，同步单量子比特交错随机基准测试显示X门保真度超过99.7%，与单独操作的结果一致，表明残余相互作用得到有效抑制。实现的45纳秒非绝热CZ门通过交错随机基准测试获得99.23(15)%的保真度估计。数值模拟结果表明耦合器能量弛豫是限制CZ门保真度的重要因素，延长耦合器寿命可显著改善门性能。实验数据验证了TCT器件在低相互作用空闲和快速纠缠操作之间的有效平衡。

Conclusion: 本研究成功展示了C-shunt磁通量子比特耦合器在超导量子处理器中的实验可行性，证明该架构能够有效结合低相互作用空闲状态与快速高保真度纠缠门操作。在空闲区实现了99.7%以上的单量子比特门保真度，45纳秒CZ门达到99.23%的保真度，满足容错量子计算的基本要求。研究指出耦合器能量弛豫是当前性能限制的主要因素，未来通过延长耦合器寿命有望进一步提升门操作性能。该工作为开发高性能超导量子处理器提供了一种有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Fast entangling gates and low-interaction idling require distinct operating conditions in superconducting quantum processors. We experimentally realize a transmon-C-shunt-transmon (TCT) device in which a flux-tunable, three-junction capacitively shunted flux-qubit coupler connects two transmon data qubits. The C-shunt coupler provides a flux-tunable, positively anharmonic spectrum for tailoring the interactions between the data qubits. Conditional Ramsey and swap measurements identify an idle region with near-zero residual ZZ interaction strength and suppressed transverse exchange. At this bias, simultaneous single-qubit interleaved randomized benchmarking yields X-gate fldelities above 99.7%, consistent with individual operation. A 45 ns nonadiabatic controlled-Z (CZ) gate achieves an interleaved randomized benchmarking fldelity estimate of 99.23(15)%. Numerical simulations identify coupler energy relaxation as an important limitation on CZ-gate fldelity and indicate that extending the coupler lifetime can improve gate performance. These results demonstrate the experimental feasibility of C-shunt flux-qubit couplers for combining low-interaction idling with fast entangling operations between transmon qubits.

</details>


### [157] [Lifting: Quantum State Discrimination Framework for Mid-Circuit Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18482)
*Chi Zhang,Neel Vora,Yilun Xu,Gang Huang,Fan Chen,Lei Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lifting框架，通过重复编码、神经解码和原位校准来改进量子计算中的中途测量（MCM）质量，解决了超导硬件噪声导致的量子态判别误差和随电路执行累积的态退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中的中途测量对纠错和结果依赖控制至关重要，但受限于超导硬件噪声。现有基于神经网络的量子态判别（QSD）方法虽有改进，但其误差仍受限于读出分布的贝叶斯风险下界。此外，随着连续测量的累积和电路执行的推进，量子态会逐渐退化，使得静态校准无法准确代表实际工作负载。因此需要一种能同时解决测量误差下界和态退化问题的新框架。

Method: 提出Lifting框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）重复编码：采集目标量子比特和辅助量子比特的联合同相-正交（I-Q）读出信号，提供更丰富的信息通道；（2）神经解码：使用神经网络处理多通道信号进行状态判别；（3）原位校准：在实际电路执行条件下对解码器进行上下文相关的训练校准。理论上证明了保留固定联合分布的额外通道不会增加贝叶斯风险，并建立了基于重叠度上界收缩的充分条件。

Result: 在三个IBM量子处理器上对重复稳定子测量、奇偶校验和量子隐形传态等工作负载进行了实验评估。实验结果验证了Lifting框架在实际量子硬件上的有效性，展示了相比传统方法在测量准确性和鲁棒性方面的改进。

Conclusion: Lifting框架通过整合多量子比特联合读出信息和执行环境相关的动态校准，有效提升了量子中途测量的可靠性，为实现更准确的量子纠错和复杂量子算法提供了实用解决方案。该方法在理论上保证不增加贝叶斯风险，并在实际超导量子处理器上得到验证，为改进量子计算硬件的测量性能提供了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum computing promises new capabilities beyond classical computation. Mid-circuit measurements (MCMs) enable error correction and outcome-dependent control, but their reliability is limited by noise on superconducting hardware. These operations depend on accurate quantum state discrimination (QSD), which assigns measured signals to state labels. Neural network-based (NN-based) QSD improves this assignment, yet its error remains bounded below by the Bayes risk of the available readout distributions. A complementary challenge is state deterioration as successive measurements accumulate and circuit execution advances, making static calibration less representative of the workload. We present Lifting, a QSD framework that addresses both challenges through repetition encoding, neural decoding, and in situ calibration. Encoding supplies joint in-phase and quadrature (I-Q) readout signals from the target and auxiliary qubits, while contextual calibration trains the decoder for the conditions encountered during execution. Our theoretical analysis shows that retaining additional channels from a fixed joint distribution cannot increase Bayes risk and establishes sufficient conditions for contraction of an overlap-based upper bound. Experiments across three IBM processors evaluate the framework on repeated stabilizer measurement, parity-check, and teleportation workloads.

</details>


### [158] [Steering the Fidelity in State Preparation on Noisy Bosonic Systems with Risk-Seeking Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18495)
*Riccardo Piantoni,Riccardo Pellini,Maurizio Ferrari Dacrema,Paolo Cremonesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险敏感优化的玻色子量子计算机状态制备方法,通过优化纯态子集的保真度而非平均保真度,实现了在损失一定平均性能的情况下,以特定概率获得高保真度量子态的目标,部分纯态的不保真度最高可降低四个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子态制备方法在随机场景下优化变分ansatz时,仅关注所有可能光子损失事件的平均保真度,忽略了系综中各纯态保真度的分布特性。然而在某些应用中,以一定概率获得高保真度量子态比提高整体平均保真度更为重要,因此需要一种能够优化保真度分布特定特征而非仅优化期望值的新方法。

Method: 从强化学习领域借鉴并改编了风险敏感优化框架的目标函数,该框架能够优化分布的特定特征而非仅优化其期望值。通过使用风险寻求型目标函数,优化从制备系综中采样的纯态子集的保真度,而非传统的平均保真度优化,从而实现高保真度概率与最低不保真度阈值之间的可控权衡。

Result: 实验结果表明,与标准的平均保真度优化方法相比,使用风险寻求型目标函数可将制备系综中部分纯态的不保真度降低多达四个数量级。此外,该方法能够有效控制获得高保真度态的概率与达到的最低不保真度阈值之间的权衡关系,为不同应用场景提供了灵活选择操作点的能力。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将风险敏感优化框架应用于玻色子量子计算机的状态制备问题,证明了通过牺牲部分平均保真度来优化保真度分布的尾部特性是可行且有效的。这种方法为需要高概率获得高保真度量子态的量子算法应用提供了新的优化策略,并通过可控的权衡机制使用户能够根据具体应用需求选择最优操作点。

Abstract: In order to execute quantum algorithms on bosonic quantum computers, it is necessary to encode their inputs into a desired target quantum state, with a process called state preparation. In real settings, state preparation methods operate in stochastic scenarios, where the prepared state is represented as a mixed state, that is a probabilistic ensemble of pure states. Therefore, existing state preparation methods optimize a variational ansatz by averaging over all possible sequences of photon loss events contained in the prepared ensemble, yielding an output state which approximates the desired target with a certain average fidelity. Such an approach, however, does not take into account directly the distribution of fidelities across states in the ensemble. In this work, we aim to maximize the fidelity of only a subset of pure states sampled from the prepared ensemble, at the cost of reducing the average fidelity overall.
