Conclusion: 本文建立了非微扰波动力学、拓扑质量抑制、手征选择和活性中微子质量动力学起源之间的桥梁。研究揭示了对称性破缺的完整动力学图景:从静态真空选择到动力学势垒溢出的连续演化过程。异宿轨道动力学提供了一种新的质量生成机制,能够自然解释极小质量尺度的起源,并为理解中微子的Majorana性质和手征不对称性提供了统一的动力学解释框架。这一框架超越了传统的静态SSB理论,为粒子物理中的质量生成和手征性问题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) is conventionally understood through static vacuum selection in low-energy approximations. However, the nonperturbative dynamical pathways connecting symmetric configurations to asymmetric states remain an open question. In this paper, we propose an operator-theoretic framework that characterizes $Z_{2}$ symmetry breaking in scalar field theory through spectral branching. Specifically, we identify an effective invariant manifold of the nonlinear Klein-Gordon equation termed the "symmetric dual breather mode," whose phase-space geometry is traced using a $t$-dependent finite-dimensional representation. Our analysis demonstrates that static SSB represents merely a small-amplitude limit ($A \ll φ_{v1}$). As the amplitude increases into the heteroclinic regime ($A > φ_{v1}$), the field dynamically overflows the central barrier, yielding a vanishing time-averaged order parameter ($\langle φ\rangle = 0$) and driving a geometric curvature cancellation ($\langle V''(φ) \rangle_{t} \to 0$). Governed by a universal scaling function $\mathfrak{F}(y)$, the logarithmic period divergence near the heteroclinic boundary naturally generates sub-eV effective mass scales without fine-tuning bare parameters. Furthermore, this dynamic barrier overflow naturally favors Majorana-like self-conjugate states over Dirac-type states. Additionally, nonlinear phase interference along the heteroclinic channel induces asymmetric phase-space contraction, decoupling the right-handed mode and dynamically giving rise to the left-handed Weyl equation. These results bridge nonperturbative wave dynamics, topological mass suppression, chiral selection, and the dynamic origin of active neutrino masses.

</details>


### [193] [Relativistic Covariance from Hamiltonian Hydrodynamics in the Chiral Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16095)
*ZiHao Liu,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从等时哈密顿形式推导了非耗散手征流体力学的协变方程,识别了手征括号中隐含的李-泊松结构,并证明了在洛伦兹推进约束下,手征哈密顿系数在所有亚光速流体速度下保持有限的充要条件是静止系中π介子传播速度不超过光速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的手征流体力学缺乏明确的哈密顿结构表述和对称性约束的系统性分析。需要建立一个从哈密顿形式出发的协变框架,明确洛伦兹推进约束对手征流体动力学系数的限制,并为未来构建热力学一致的耗散推广提供坚实的理论基础。

Method: 1. 从等时哈密顿形式出发推导非耗散手征流体力学的协变方程;2. 识别和分析手征括号中的李-泊松结构,该结构结合了流体运动与局域手征变换;3. 针对宇称不变的哈密顿量(关于手征荷密度和π介子梯度的二次型,无外部背景场),推导洛伦兹推进施加的约束条件;4. 求解这些约束的耦合非线性扇区,将其等价于对称张量的线性推进变换律;5. 从静止系数据确定速度依赖关系,分析哈密顿系数在不同流体速度下的有限性条件。

Result: 成功推导了手征流体力学的协变方程并揭示了其李-泊松结构。证明了洛伦兹推进约束的耦合非线性扇区等价于对称张量的线性变换律。在给定有限正静止系系数、有限普通流体能量密度、热力学导数有限以及动量-速度关系可双向求解的条件下,得到关键结果:手征哈密顿系数在所有亚光速流体速度下保持有限,当且仅当静止系中π介子传播速度不超过光速。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了非耗散手征流体力学的完整哈密顿-协变框架,明确了洛伦兹对称性对理论系数的本质约束,特别是将系数的全速度范围有限性与π介子因果性(亚光速传播)直接关联。这一形式化使对称性约束显式化,为未来构建热力学一致的耗散手征流体力学提供了严格的理论起点和约束条件。

Abstract: We derive the covariant equations of nondissipative chiral hydrodynamics from their equal-time Hamiltonian formulation and identify the Lie--Poisson structure implicit in the chiral brackets. This structure combines fluid motion with local chiral transformations. For a parity-invariant Hamiltonian quadratic in chiral charge densities and pion gradients, without external backgrounds, we derive and solve the constraints imposed by Lorentz boosts. Their coupled nonlinear sector is equivalent to the linear boost transformation laws of a symmetric tensor, which determine the velocity dependence from rest-frame data. For finite, positive rest-frame coefficients, finite ordinary-fluid energy density and thermodynamic derivatives, and a momentum--velocity relation that can be solved in either direction, the chiral Hamiltonian coefficients remain finite at every fluid speed below light speed if and only if the rest-frame pion propagation speed does not exceed the speed of light. This formulation makes symmetry constraints explicit and provides a starting point for future thermodynamically consistent dissipative extensions.

</details>


### [194] [Retarded Green Function For Bopp--Podolsky Electrodynamics in Four-Dimensional De Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16239)
*Wei Fan,Xiao Yan Chew*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维德西特时空上的经典Bopp-Podolsky电动力学,通过不变双张量展开方法求解了横向推迟格林函数,揭示了规范不变的电磁响应特性,包括奇异性抵消、波前软化、以及由大质量扇区主导的内部电磁尾效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德西特时空作为宇宙学重要背景,其高度对称性为研究高阶电动力学提供了理想场景。Bopp-Podolsky理论作为麦克斯韦理论的四阶推广,可能解决经典电动力学的发散问题。然而,曲面时空上BP电动力学的格林函数结构、规范不变响应以及全局拓扑约束尚未得到系统研究,特别是推迟格林函数的分布结构和奇异性行为需要明确刻画。

Method: 利用德西特时空的最大对称性,将矢量格林函数协变展开为有限个不变双张量基的线性组合,标量系数仅依赖于不变间隔。对于守恒源,四阶场算符可因式分解为麦克斯韦算符和大质量矢量算符的乘积,从而将BP推迟核表示为两者横向格林函数之差。通过不变双张量分解,将传播子方程约化为超几何主方程并确定其分布解的结构。进一步分析旋度作用后的规范不变场强核,推导视界正则的静态势,并通过对跖点电荷配置实现全局德西特时空上的总电荷为零约束。

Result: 发现麦克斯韦和大质量矢量扇区在短距离处的主导奇异性相互抵消,仅留下对数型主奇异性和软化的矢量势波前。规范不变的场强核在光锥上包含delta分布和正则的类时尾部,其中麦克斯韦主尾被旋度湮灭,内部电磁尾完全由大质量扇区产生。获得了精确的视界正则静态势,证明了库仑发散的抵消,成功恢复了平直空间BP势,并建立了广义高斯定律。在全局德西特时空上,通过具有光滑因式分解初始数据的对跖电荷配置实现了电荷守恒约束。

Conclusion: 本文系统构建了德西特时空上经典Bopp-Podolsky电动力学的格林函数理论框架,揭示了高阶电动力学在曲面时空中的独特因果结构:奇异性软化机制源于麦克斯韦和大质量模式的差分抵消,而规范不变的电磁尾效应完全由大质量扇区主导。该理论在经典层面解决了库仑发散问题,且无需引入量子Lee-Wick围道或特定真空假设,为曲面时空中的高阶场论及其宇宙学应用提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We study classical Bopp--Podolsky (BP) electrodynamics on four-dimensional de Sitter spacetime, focusing on its transverse retarded Green function and gauge-invariant electromagnetic response. Because de Sitter spacetime is maximally symmetric, its vector Green functions admit a covariant expansion in a finite basis of invariant bitensors, with scalar coefficients depending only on the invariant separation. For conserved sources, the fourth-order field operator factorizes into Maxwell and massive-vector operators, yielding the BP retarded kernel as the difference of their transverse Green functions. Using the invariant bitensor decomposition, we reduce the propagator equations to a hypergeometric master equation and determine the distributional structure of its solution. The common leading short-distance singularity of the Maxwell and massive-vector sectors cancels, leaving a logarithmic master singularity and a softened vector-potential wavefront. After one curl, the gauge-invariant field-strength kernel contains a null-cone delta distribution and a regular timelike tail. The smooth Maxwell master tail is annihilated by the curl, so the interior electromagnetic tail is generated entirely by the massive sector. We also derive an exact horizon-regular static potential, demonstrate the cancellation of the Coulomb divergence, recover the flat-space BP potential, and formulate the generalized Gauss law. On global de Sitter spacetime, the compactness of the spatial sections requires the total charge to vanish; we implement this constraint using an antipodal charge configuration with smooth factorized initial data. The analysis is classical and does not assume a quantum Lee--Wick contour or a preferred de Sitter vacuum.

</details>


### [195] [A first order nonabelian antisymmetric tensor model with hidden higher symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16250)
*Peter D. Jarvis,Jean Thierry-Mieg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个具有规范内部对称性的非阿贝尔复自对偶反对称张量模型,包含复自对偶反对称张量场多重态及其共轭场与无质量Yang-Mills规范场的相互作用,并揭示了模型具有的隐藏内部对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自对偶反对称张量场理论主要集中在阿贝尔情况,缺乏对非阿贝尔复多重态情况的系统研究。为了构建更完整的规范场论框架,需要发展具有规范内部对称性的非阿贝尔复自对偶反对称张量模型,并探索其可能存在的隐藏对称性结构。

Method: 采用一阶形式化方法在经典层面构建模型:将局域场在Dirac代数和内部矩阵基上进行分量展开;通过迹运算确保Lorentz不变性和规范不变性;应用适当的双线性形式;引入辅助占位场(这些场不是势场,但通过代数运动方程消去后成为曲率或场强);利用非规范群生成元的额外内部矩阵实现分量展开在乘法下的封闭性,这些矩阵混合内部分量并连接辅助曲率与势场。

Result: 成功构建了非阿贝尔复自对偶反对称张量模型,证明该模型在额外内部矩阵变换下保持不变,从而具有一种隐藏内部对称性。详细分析了规范群为SU(2)×U(1)的具体情况。作为更高对称性的候选,研究了Lie群SU(3)和Lie超代数SU(2/1)两种情况,其中额外矩阵分别通过Lie括号或分级Lie括号作用。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了具有规范内部对称性的非阿贝尔复自对偶反对称张量模型,揭示了模型所具有的隐藏内部对称性结构。该模型通过一阶形式化和Dirac代数分量展开方法实现了Lorentz不变性和规范不变性的统一处理。SU(2)×U(1)规范群的具体实现以及SU(3)和SU(2/1)作为更高对称性候选的探索,为进一步研究非阿贝尔自对偶场论及其在统一场论中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a nonabelian complex self-dual antisymmetric tensor model, with gauged internal symmetry, comprising a complex multiplet of massless self-dual antisymmetric tensor fields and its conjugate (interacting with massless Yang-Mills gauge fields). We build the model at the classical level, via a first order formulation, assigning local fields to a component expansion over the Dirac algebra and a basis of internal matrices, with Lorentz and gauge invariance ensured by tracing, and applying an appropriate bilinear form. The full expansion requires auxiliary place-holder fields, which are not potentials, but become curvatures (field strengths) after elimination via their algebraic equations of motion. The component expansion over the Dirac algebra is closed under multiplication by additional internal matrices, which are not gauge group generators, but mix internal components, and link auxiliary curvatures with potentials. The model is shown to be invariant under such transformations, and thus possesses a type of hidden internal symmetry. We analyze the specific case of gauge group $SU(2)\! \times \! U(1)$, and as higher symmetry candidates, we consider both the Lie group $SU(3)$ and the Lie superalgebra $SU(2/1)$ (with the additional matrices acting via Lie brackets, or graded Lie brackets, respectively).

</details>


### [196] [Regular-Zero Drinfeld-Sokolov Charges in Doubled Four-Dimensional Chern-Simons Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16271)
*Wentao Zeng,Yifan He,Xian Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双四维Chern-Simons理论经有限缺陷约化后的经典局部守恒荷，在特定边界条件下构造了局部圆积分，证明了它们的泊松对易性，并给出了经典局部阿贝尔泊松子代数条件存在性的严格论述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双四维Chern-Simons理论的有限缺陷约化框架下，需要系统地构造和证明经典局部守恒荷的存在性。在有限单零点处，扭函数诱导的仿射规范变换中的非齐次导数项消失，使得两个Gaudin流的留数计算按普通伴随作用变换，这为构造守恒量提供了技术可能性。研究目标是建立局部守恒荷的泊松交换性并证明其守恒性质。

Method: 针对每个单理想，使用主Drinfeld-Sokolov层次的Evans相容不变张量作为外部代数输入，构造局部圆积分。将问题分为同零点扇区、异零点扇区和混合扇区分别处理，逐一证明两两泊松对易性。在Stedman简单极点/简单零点边界哈密顿量、周期平凡单值扇区、以及边界权重独立于相空间变量的显式假设下，将约化哈密顿量逐项分解为有限极点、无穷远、有限零点和剩余第一类约束贡献项，从而推导出所构造荷的守恒性。明确区分有限缺陷约化空间、携带剩余约束的扩展空间以及物理商空间。

Result: 成功构造了局部圆积分形式的守恒荷，并在分扇区处理框架下严格证明了它们的两两泊松对易性。在特定边界条件和假设下，通过哈密顿量的项逐项分解，建立了这些守恒荷的守恒性。获得了经典局部阿贝尔泊松子代数的条件存在性陈述。

Conclusion: 在明确的技术假设下（Stedman边界条件、平凡单值扇区、相空间无关的边界权重），给出了双四维Chern-Simons理论有限缺陷约化后经典局部阿贝尔泊松子代数的条件存在性陈述。作者明确声明不主张泛函独立性、极大性或刘维尔完备性。高阶零点和非平凡单值扇区的推广不在本工作范围内。这是一个在特定约束条件下的严格数学物理结果，为可积系统理论提供了新的守恒量构造方法。

Abstract: We study classical local conserved charges obtained after finite-defect reduction of doubled four-dimensional Chern--Simons theory. At a finite simple zero, the inhomogeneous derivative term in the affine gauge transformation induced by the twist function vanishes, so the residue evaluations of the two Gaudin currents transform by the ordinary adjoint action. For each simple ideal, we use the Evans-compatible invariant tensors of the principal Drinfeld--Sokolov hierarchy as external algebraic input, construct local circle integrals, and treat the same-zero, distinct-zero, and mixed sectors separately, proving pairwise Poisson commutativity. Under the explicit assumptions of Stedman's simple-pole/simple-zero boundary Hamiltonian, the periodic trivial-monodromy sector, and boundary weights independent of phase-space variables, the reduced Hamiltonian decomposes term by term into finite-pole, infinity, finite-zero, and residual first-class-constraint contributions, which yields conservation of the constructed charges. We distinguish the finite-defect reduced space, the extended space carrying the residual constraint, and the physical quotient. The result is a conditional existence statement for a classical local Abelian Poisson subalgebra; we make no claim of functional independence, maximality, or Liouville completeness. Extensions to higher-order zeros and nontrivial monodromy sectors are outside the scope of this work.

</details>


### [197] [A No-Go Theorem for Nonspherical Bounces with a Single Negative Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16688)
*Guangshang Chen,Jianpeng Dai,Yang Xiao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了在平坦时空中单个实标量场的假真空衰变问题中,任何非球对称的局域化鞍点至少具有三个负模,从而从谱分析角度排除了具有单负模的非球对称反弹解,为假真空衰变的球对称反弹描述提供了补充的谱学理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假真空衰变传统上由具有单一负涨落模的球对称反弹解描述。这引发了一个基本问题:单负模要求本身能否独立于作用量最小化而强制要求球对称性?现有理论主要依赖作用量最小化来解释球对称反弹解的选择,但缺乏从负模数量(即谱性质)角度对非球对称解的排除性证明。本文旨在从谱分析的角度为球对称反弹解的唯一性提供独立的理论支撑。

Method: 采用严格的数学分析方法,针对平坦时空中具有光滑势能的单个实标量场系统,研究零初始速度的局域化非球对称鞍点解的稳定性谱。通过分析涨落算符的本征值问题,证明了非球对称配置必然导致至少三个负本征模的存在。进一步利用反射对称性等额外对称性约束,在六次标量模型中具体展示了如何收紧负模数量的下界。该方法独立于传统的作用量最小化论证,纯粹从谱性质出发建立约束。

Result: 严格证明了在平坦时空中单实标量场理论框架下,所有零初始速度的局域化非球对称鞍点至少具有三个负模。这一结果明确排除了具有单负模的非球对称反弹解候选者。在具有额外反射对称性的情况下(如六次标量模型所示),负模数量的下界可以进一步提高。该结果从谱分析角度独立地支持了球对称反弹解作为假真空衰变物理描述的合理性。

Conclusion: 本文从谱学角度为假真空衰变的球对称反弹描述提供了坚实的理论基础。通过证明非球对称局域化鞍点必然具有至少三个负模,独立于作用量最小化论证排除了单负模非球对称解的可能性。这一发现表明,单负模要求本身就能作为强制球对称性的充分条件,为理解假真空衰变机制提供了补充的、基于稳定性谱分析的新视角。该方法可能为更复杂场论系统中的类似问题提供分析框架。

Abstract: False vacuum decay is conventionally described by a spherically symmetric bounce with a single negative fluctuation mode. This raises a fundamental question: can the single negative mode requirement itself enforce spherical symmetry, independently of action minimization? For a single real scalar field with a smooth potential in flat spacetime, we rigorously prove that every localized nonspherical saddle with zero initial velocity has at least three negative modes. Additional reflection symmetries further tighten these bounds, as illustrated in a sextic scalar model. Our findings exclude nonspherical bounce candidates with a single negative mode, providing a complementary spectral foundation for the spherical bounce description of false vacuum decay.

</details>


### [198] [About P-adic Numbers and Professor Igor Volovich on the Occasion of his 80th Anniversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16708)
*Paul H Frampton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾了1987年Volovich关于p-adic弦的开创性工作，以及作者与Okada后续建立的p-adic弦费曼规则和相关非局域场论，这些理论在物理学中获得了广泛应用。文章还包含了作者1989-91年访问莫斯科的回忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾和总结p-adic弦理论的历史发展，特别是费曼规则的建立过程；记录该领域早期研究的重要历史时刻和学术交流，为理解p-adic弦理论的发展脉络提供历史视角。

Method: 历史回顾与学术综述方法：总结1987年Volovich的原始论文内容；阐述作者与Okada合作建立的p-adic弦费曼规则的理论框架；介绍由此发展出的非局域场论及其在物理学中的应用；结合个人回忆记录1989-91年莫斯科访问期间的学术交流。

Result: 系统性地呈现了p-adic弦理论的费曼规则建立过程；展示了相关非局域场论在多个物理领域的应用成果；提供了该理论早期发展阶段的历史记录和学术交流的第一手资料。

Conclusion: 本文作为历史回顾性论文，确认了p-adic弦理论及其费曼规则在理论物理中的重要地位，相关非局域场论已在多个物理应用中展现价值。文章不仅具有学术总结意义，还通过个人回忆保存了该领域发展的珍贵历史片段，对理解p-adic数学物理的发展历程具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We recall a 1987 paper written by Volovich and our subsequent work with Okada which established the Feyman rules for the p-adic string and the related non-local field theory which found diverse physical applications. We add some memories of visits to Moscow during 1989-91.

</details>


### [199] [TheoPhysEval: Assessing AI agents at autonomous theoretical physics problem solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16733)
*A. Ramesh Chandra,Aaghaz Mahajan,Ainesh Sanyal,Mianqi Wang,Shubhadeep Chakraborty,Sudip Chakraborty,Shailesh Chandrasekharan,Rama Govindarajan,Akshit Goyal,Jonathan J. Heckman,Yasir Iqbal,Divya Jaganathan,Saumav Kapoor,Andreas Karch,Pushkar Khandare,Sandeep Krishna,Anupam Kundu,Alok Laddha,Tongyan Lin,Swarnendu Maity,Nilmani Mathur,Shani Nadir Meynet,Alessandro Mininno,Abhijeet Mishra,Vidur Niranjan,Abhishodh Prakash,Pratik Rath,Durbar Roy,Debargha Sarkar,Srikanth Sastry,Gary Shiu,Aninda Sinha,Thomas Van Riet,Praneeth Netrapalli,Suvrat Raju*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套包含50个可验证答案的未发表理论物理研究级问题，测试了前沿AI智能体（特别是GPT-6-Astra配合Codex）的自主问题解决能力，结果显示其准确率达到98%，平均成本不到3美元/题，表明AI已具备执行理论物理研究中具体计算的能力，但尚未实现突破性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在多个领域展现出强大能力，但其在理论物理研究中的实际问题解决能力尚未得到系统性评估。现有评估缺乏研究级别、具有可验证答案的理论物理问题集，且AI智能体在无人类提示的情况下自主解决复杂物理问题的表现仍不明确。研究旨在构建标准化测试集，量化评估AI智能体在理论物理研究任务中的实际表现，并探讨AI具备强大问题解决能力但尚未产生理论物理突破的原因。

Method: 构建了包含50个未发表的研究级理论物理问题的测试集，这些问题具有可验证答案，专家通常需约一天时间解决。测试采用现成的AI智能体（主要是GPT-6-Astra结合Codex工具），在真实研究环境下赋予其文献搜索、编写数值和符号计算脚本等工具使用能力。智能体在无人类提示的情况下自主解决问题。进行了对照实验，包括限制网络搜索和背景文献访问，以评估智能体对文献的记忆程度。记录了问题解决的准确率和平均成本等指标。

Result: 最佳表现的智能体GPT-6-Astra（配合Codex）在该问题集上达到了98%的近乎完美准确率，平均每题成本不到3美元。当智能体被拒绝网络搜索和背景文献访问时，准确率仅出现小幅下降，表明智能体对文献存在显著的记忆效应。实验结果证明当前最先进的AI智能体能够准确执行理论物理研究中所需的具体计算任务，在研究级问题上表现出色。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前最先进的AI智能体已具备在理论物理研究中准确执行具体计算的能力，在研究级问题上达到极高准确率。然而，尽管具备出色的问题解决能力，AI智能体尚未在理论物理领域实现突破性发现。这一现象提示：解决已定义问题与产生原创性科学突破之间存在本质差异。文章对这些发现的更广泛意义进行了评论，并讨论了AI在具备强大计算能力的情况下仍未引领理论物理突破的可能解释，为理解AI在科学研究中的角色和局限性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: We present a suite of 50 unpublished research-level theoretical physics problems with verifiable answers. Without AI assistance, a typical problem in our collection could be solved by a world expert in about a day or less but would take considerably longer for experts in adjacent specialties. We test the ability of off-the-shelf AI agents to tackle these problems autonomously, without human hints. When given access to tools available in a realistic setting, including the ability to search the literature and write scripts for numerical and symbolic work, the best-performing agent, GPT-6-Astra harnessed in Codex, obtains the final answers with a near-perfect accuracy of 98% at an average cost under USD 3 per problem. On average, agents show only small changes in accuracy when denied web search and access to background research papers, suggesting significant memorization of the literature. These results suggest that state-of-the-art agents can accurately perform concrete calculations required across theoretical physics research. We comment on the broader implications of these findings and discuss possible explanations for the current lack of agent-led breakthroughs in theoretical physics despite their outstanding problem-solving abilities.

</details>


### [200] [Out-of-time-order hydrodynamics and scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16888)
*Marta Bucca,Akash Jain,Márk Mezei,Alexey Milekhin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将流体力学作为有效场论(EFT)推广到四重时间轮廓的Schwinger-Keldysh形式,用于描述乱序关联函数(OTOCs)所探测的扰码现象,并从最大混沌的SYK晶格模型微观推导出该EFT,证明了流体力学自由度的普适性延伸到OTOCs等复杂可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流体力学EFT在预测时序关联函数的长时大距离行为方面非常成功,但其对量子信息扰码(scrambling)这一由乱序关联函数(OTOCs)探测的现象的预测能力尚未明确。研究者希望探究流体力学框架能否扩展到描述更复杂的量子可观测量,特别是与量子混沌相关的OTOCs,并验证局域SL(2,R)对称性作为扰码机制的有效性。

Method: 1) 将Schwinger-Keldysh流体力学EFT推广到包含所有导数阶的四重时间轮廓形式,以捕获OTOCs;2) 引入局域SL(2,R)对称性作为理论构建的关键要素;3) 从微观理论出发,利用最大混沌低温极限下的SYK晶格模型推导出精确的EFT形式,并确定所有输运系数;4) 计算能量流的OTOC,分析其指数增长行为并与冲击波(scramblon)有效描述进行对比验证。

Result: 成功构建了描述OTOCs的全导数阶四重轮廓流体力学EFT;从SYK晶格模型微观推导确定了该模型的所有输运系数;计算得到的能量流OTOC呈指数增长且饱和混沌界(chaos bound);计算结果与基于冲击波/扰码子(scramblon)有效描述的预期相匹配,验证了理论的一致性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了流体力学自由度的普适性不仅适用于传统的时序关联函数,还能扩展到乱序关联函数(OTOCs)等更复杂的量子可观测量。通过引入局域SL(2,R)对称性和四重时间轮廓形式,流体力学EFT框架能够有效描述量子扰码现象,并在最大混沌系统(如SYK模型)中给出与微观理论一致的预测,为理解量子混沌的流体力学起源提供了新途径。

Abstract: Hydrodynamics as an effective field theory (EFT) has been immensely successful for predicting the behaviour of time-ordered correlation functions in the long-time, large-distance regime. In this paper, we analyse the predictions of hydrodynamics for scrambling as probed by out-of-time order correlators (OTOCs). To do so, we consider an all-derivative order generalisation of the Schwinger-Keldysh EFT of hydrodynamics extended to a four-fold time contour required to capture OTOCs. A crucial ingredient of the construction is a local SL(2,R) symmetry, which has previously been suggested as a mechanism for scrambling. We also derive this exact EFT from the microscopic formulation of the maximally chaotic low-temperature limit of the SYK lattice, thereby determining all transport coefficients for this model. As a novel application, we compute the OTOC of energy currents, find that its exponential growth saturates the chaos bound, and match it to expectations based on a shockwave (or scramblon) effective description. Our work shows that the universality of hydrodynamics degrees of freedom extends beyond traditional time-ordered correlators to more complex observables such as OTOCs.

</details>


### [201] [Metric and dilaton information from high-overtone quasinormal modes of analytic Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16973)
*Yu Shi,Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Changqing Liu,Weike Deng,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞内部几何如何通过线性扰动的高泛音谱编码度规和物质信息,特别是在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton黑洞中,揭示了实端点和复端点在不同时空连接下对谱响应的控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部对外部观测者不可见,但其几何信息可能通过线性扰动的高泛音谱特征反映出来。现有研究尚未充分阐明黑洞内部度规和支撑物质信息如何精确编码在谱结构中,特别是在包含dilaton场的解析黑洞模型中,需要系统研究不同时空连接类型(类空、类时、临界)下的谱响应机制。

Method: 研究采用Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton黑洞作为解析模型,引入中性谱观测场与dilaton场在固定紫外维度下耦合,构建对物质敏感的探测通道和动量响应通道。针对三种时空连接类型分析谱结构:1)实端点类空连接情况下,通过动量响应和质量标签依赖性分离度规指数和dilaton斜率符号;2)在因果阈值附近,分析复端点如何控制谱族,研究质量轮廓收敛至共同值时的分数修正和规则传播中的标签依赖性;3)在临界点(无内视界)和类时内部验证该机制的局部延续性。最后通过外部数值谱验证重构方案和主导复端点响应。

Result: 研究发现:1)对于实端点类空连接,动量响应和质量标签依赖性能够成功分离度规指数与带符号的dilaton斜率;2)在因果阈值附近,复端点控制谱族演化,质量轮廓趋向共同值并产生共享的分数修正,而规则波传播保持标签依赖性;3)该机制在临界点(无内视界)处存在,并能局部延续到类时内部;4)外部数值谱验证了重构方法的有效性以及主导复端点响应的预测;5)局部标度性和探测耦合确定候选响应,而波连接决定其在外部谱中的印记。

Conclusion: 黑洞内部几何和物质信息能够通过高泛音谱系统地编码并被外部观测提取。不同时空连接类型下的端点结构(实端点或复端点)控制着谱响应的不同特征:实端点类空连接允许分离度规和物质参数,复端点在因果阈值附近主导谱族行为并产生共享修正。该机制在临界点和类时内部具有普适性,为通过外部可观测量重构黑洞内部信息提供了理论框架,证明了局部标度、探测耦合和波连接在确定谱印记中的关键作用。

Abstract: Black-hole interiors are hidden from exterior observers, yet their geometry can enter the high-overtone spectrum of linear perturbations. We study how this spectrum encodes metric and supporting-matter information in analytic Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton black holes. Coupling a neutral spectator to the dilaton at fixed UV dimension provides a matter-sensitive channel alongside the momentum response. For real-endpoint spacelike connections, the momentum response and mass-label dependence separate the metric exponents from the signed dilaton slope. Near the causal threshold, a complex endpoint controls the spectral family: the mass profiles approach a common value, producing a shared fractional correction, while regular propagation retains label dependence. This mechanism is present at criticality without an inner horizon and continues locally into timelike interiors. Exterior numerical spectra support the reconstruction and the leading complex-endpoint responses. Local scaling and probe coupling set the candidate responses, while wave connection determines their imprint on the exterior spectrum.

</details>


### [202] [Modular Flow and Holographic Timelike Entanglement Entropy in AdS and Flat Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17165)
*Mehdi Hakami Shalamzari,Zahra Paygozar,Mahdi Kord Zangeneh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次显式构造了与类时极值曲线相关的模流，系统地建立了AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂中类空和类时区间的模流框架，并证明了这些模流的不动点能够重现全息（类时）纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时纠缠熵是探测全息理论的有效工具，但此前一直缺乏对其类时极值曲线构造背后模流的显式体实现。为了理解全息纠缠熵的深层机制，需要系统地构造边界对称生成元产生的模流，并将其延拓到体中，从而建立体模流与极值曲线以及全息纠缠熵之间的明确联系。

Method: 研究采用了以下系统方法：(1)从边界对称生成元出发，显式推导边界模流并将其延拓到体中；(2)对于类空区间，构造体模流并证明其决定的极值曲线的积分线元可重现标准全息纠缠熵；(3)将该方法推广到类时区间，显式构造模流，其不动点重现给出全息类时纠缠熵的极值类空和类时曲线；(4)将整个程序应用于flat₃/CCFT₂体系；(5)证明平直空间结果可以从AdS₃对应结果通过平直空间极限得到。

Result: 成功构造了AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂中类空和类时区间的模流。对于类空区间，体模流确定的极值曲线能够重现标准全息纠缠熵。对于类时区间，首次显式构造了与类时极值曲线相关的模流，其不动点准确重现了产生全息类时纠缠熵的极值曲线。flat₃/CCFT₂中的结果可以通过平直空间极限从AdS₃结果中恢复。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了类时区间模流的显式构造，填补了全息理论中的重要空白。建立了从边界对称生成元到体模流、再到极值曲线和全息纠缠熵的系统性联系，为理解全息纠缠熵提供了更深层的几何和动力学基础。该框架成功应用于AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂，并证明了两者之间通过平直空间极限的连续性，为全息对偶理论中模流的研究提供了完整的技术框架。

Abstract: Timelike entanglement entropy provides a useful probe of holographic theoris, yet an explicit bulk realization of the modular flow underlying its timelike extremal curve construction has so far been lacking. We construct modular flows associated with spacelike and timelike intervals in AdS$_{3}$/CFT$_{2}$ and extend this framework to flat$_{3}$/CCFT$_{2}$. Beginning with the boundary symmetry generators, we explicitly derive the boundary modular flows and extend them into the bulk, providing a systematic procedure that relates bulk modular flows to the extremal curves and holographic (timelike) entanglement entropy.
  For spacelike intervals, the resulting bulk modular flows determine the extremal curves, whose integrated line elements reproduce the standard holographic entanglement entropy. We then generalize this approach to timelike intervals, explicitly constructing the modular flows whose fixed points reproduce the extremal spacelike and timelike curves which gives the holographic timelike entanglement entropy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first explicit construction of the modular flow associated with a timelike extremal curve for a timelike interval.
  The same procedure is then carried over to flat$_{3}$/CCFT$_{2}$, which give rise to the holographic (timelike) entanglement entropy. Finally, we show that the flat space results obtained in this work can be recovered from their AdS$_3$ counterparts in the flat space limit.

</details>


### [203] [On the analytic continuation and asymptotic behaviour of the two-point function in massless $φ^4$-theory in Euclidean de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17215)
*Martin Beneke,Andrea F. Sanfilippo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧几里得德西特空间中带有λφ^4相互作用的无质量最小耦合实标量场的两点函数,计算了O(λ^2)阶的领头对数项(N^5LO),证明了其解析延拓到洛伦兹签名与随机形式主义的结果一致,并通过证明Borel可求和性表明欧几里得理论可用于提取洛伦兹德西特空间中无质量关联函数的晚期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在德西特空间的量子场论中,理解无质量场的长时行为和相互作用效应是一个重要但复杂的问题。欧几里得形式主义和随机形式主义提供了不同的计算途径,但二者的关系及其对洛伦兹时空物理量的预测能力需要被严格验证。特别是高阶微扰计算的收敛性和可求和性对于理解理论的非微扰性质至关重要。

Method: 采用欧几里得路径积分方法计算λφ^4相互作用理论中两点函数的领头对数项,精确到O(λ^2)阶(即√λ展开的第五次次领头阶N^5LO)。通过解析延拓技术将欧几里得结果转换到洛伦兹签名,并与随机形式主义的级数展开进行比对。研究随机级数的大阶行为,利用Borel求和技术分析级数的可求和性。

Result: 成功计算了不变距离函数的O(λ^2)阶领头对数项贡献。证明了欧几里得理论经解析延拓后与随机形式主义的计算结果完全吻合。确定了随机级数展开的大阶渐近行为,并严格证明了该级数的Borel可求和性,表明微扰级数虽可能发散但可通过Borel求和技术重构。

Conclusion: 欧几里得德西特空间的场论计算可以通过解析延拓可靠地预测洛伦兹德西特空间的物理量。随机形式主义级数的Borel可求和性为利用欧几里得方法提取洛伦兹德西特空间中无质量关联函数的晚期渐近行为提供了坚实的理论基础,为研究德西特空间量子场论的非微扰性质开辟了有效途径。

Abstract: For the two-point function of the massless, minimally-coupled, real scalar field in Euclidean de Sitter space with $λφ^4$-interaction, we compute the leading-logarithmic terms in the invariant distance of the two-point function to $O(λ^2)$, which counts as the fifth subleading order (N$^5$LO) in the $\sqrtλ$-expansion. We show that the analytic continuation to Lorentzian signature coincides with the expansion of the analogous quantity computed in the stochastic formalism. We determine the large-order behaviour of the stochastic series expansion and prove its Borel summability, which suggests that the Euclidean theory can be used to extract the late-time behaviour of massless correlation functions in Lorentzian de Sitter space.

</details>


### [204] [New analytic Janus and $S^4$ solutions for $\mathcal{N}=2^*$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17238)
*Fridrik Freyr Gautason*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个三参数族的正则解析超引力解,这些解对偶于四球面上的N=2*规范场论以及N=4超对称Yang-Mills理论中的U(1)²不变Janus界面。研究利用五维超引力模型处理弯曲畴壁解,发现隐藏的运动积分使得解析处理成为可能,并通过全息方法评估了场论拉格朗日量的单点函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在构造与特定场论系统对偶的超引力解,特别是对偶于四球面上N=2*规范场论和带有或不带N=2*质量的N=4超对称Yang-Mills理论中U(1)²不变Janus界面的超引力解。这类解析解的构造对于理解规范/引力对偶以及场论中界面现象的引力对偶描述具有重要意义。

Method: 采用耦合六个标量场的五维超引力模型,该模型源于IIB型超引力在五维球面S⁵上的一致截断。通过研究弯曲畴壁解并发现其中存在隐藏的运动积分,使得能够对解进行解析处理。将解的三个参数与场论中的源项和真空期望值相关联,并通过解的正则性条件限制参数的取值范围。最后利用全息方法计算Janus解族的场论拉格朗日量单点函数。

Result: 成功构造了三参数族的正则解析超引力解,这些解同时对偶于:(a)四球面上的N=2*规范场论;(b)N=4超对称Yang-Mills理论中具有U(1)²不变性的Janus界面(可带或不带N=2*质量项)。发现弯曲畴壁解具有隐藏的运动积分,使解析求解成为可能。建立了解的三个参数与场论源项和真空期望值之间的对应关系,并确定了正则性条件对参数范围的限制。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构造了对偶于四球面上N=2*规范场论和N=4 SYM理论中Janus界面的三参数族正则解析超引力解。通过五维超引力模型和弯曲畴壁解中隐藏运动积分的发现,实现了解的解析处理。解的参数与场论物理量建立了明确对应,正则性条件确定了参数的有效范围。该工作为理解相关场论系统的全息对偶提供了重要的解析工具,并为进一步研究Janus界面的全息性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct a three-parameter family of regular analytic supergravity solutions which are dual to (a) the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ gauge theory on the four-sphere, and (b) $\mathrm{U}(1)^2$-invariant Janus interfaces in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM with or without the $\mathcal{N}=2^*$ mass turned on. To this end we employ a five-dimensional supergravity model coupled to six scalars which arises as a certain consistent truncation of type IIB supergravity on $S^5$. The curved domain wall solutions feature a hidden integral of motion which allows for their analytic treatment. We relate the three parameters of the solution to field theory sources and vevs and show how regularity of the solutions restricts their range. We briefly discuss the holographic evaluation of the one-point function of the field theory Lagrangian for our family of Janus solutions.

</details>


### [205] [Holographic Pseudo Entropy from the Rindler Method in AdS and Flat Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17239)
*Mehdi Hakami Shalamzari,Zahra Paygozar,Mahdi Kord Zangeneh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Rindler方法研究了AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂中一般类时区间的全息类时纠缠熵(TEE)。研究发现类时极值曲线是连接黑洞内外视界的测地线,其长度iπℓ独立于区间选择,单独贡献虚部iπc/6;在平直空间全息中,CCFT₂平面上类时区间的全息TEE的实部和虚部相等,这与柱面情形形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时纠缠熵(TEE)作为类时区间的伪熵,其虚部在体相中来自类时测地线,但在Rindler图景中该测地线的几何意义尚未明确。研究者希望通过Rindler方法阐明类时极值曲线的几何含义,并系统研究AdS₃/CFT₂和flat₃/CCFT₂中一般类时区间的全息TEE性质。

Method: 采用Rindler方法对旋转BTZ黑洞进行Rindler变换,分析极值曲线的几何结构。在AdS₃/CFT₂中,研究两条位于内外视界的类空极值曲线,通过交换l_u↔ℓl_φ探讨其对称性,识别连接两视界的类时测地线。在平直空间全息中,遵循相同程序分析位于宇宙学视界和类时无穷远之间的曲线结构,计算CCFT₂平面上类时区间的全息TEE。

Result: 在AdS₃/CFT₂中,类时极值曲线被识别为连接旋转BTZ黑洞内外视界的测地线,其长度为iπℓ,与区间选择无关,单独贡献虚部iπc/6。在平直空间全息中,类空曲线位于宇宙学视界上,类时曲线位于宇宙学视界和类时无穷远之间。对于CCFT₂平面上的类时区间,全息TEE的实部和虚部相等,这与柱面情形存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 本文通过Rindler方法成功阐明了全息类时纠缠熵中类时极值曲线的几何意义:在AdS₃/CFT₂中为连接黑洞内外视界的测地线,其贡献的虚部具有普适性(iπc/6)且独立于区间参数。研究还揭示了平直空间全息中CCFT₂在不同拓扑(平面vs柱面)上的TEE性质差异,为理解类时纠缠熵的几何起源和全息对偶提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Via the Rindler method, we study holographic timelike entanglement entropy (TEE) for a general timelike interval in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ and flat$_3$/CCFT$_2$. The TEE is the pseudo entropy of a timelike interval. In the bulk, the imaginary part of the TEE comes from a timelike geodesic, but in the Rindler picture this geodesic has not been identified. After a Rindler transformation to a rotating BTZ black hole, the two spacelike extremal curves lie on the inner and outer horizons, and exchanging $l_u\leftrightarrow \ell l_φ$ takes one to the other. We find the geometric meaning of the timelike extremal curve: a geodesic stretched between the two horizons. Its length, $iπ\ell$, is independent of the interval, and it alone produces the imaginary part $iπc/6$. We follow the same procedure in flat space holography, where the spacelike curve sits on the cosmological horizon and the timelike curve lies between the cosmological horizon and timelike infinity. Evaluating the holographic TEE for a timelike interval in CCFT$_2$ on the plane, we find that, in contrast with the cylinder, the real and imaginary parts are equal.

</details>


### [206] [Love at First Loop: Quantum Deformability of Near-Extremal Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17259)
*Pablo A. Cano,Marina David,Roberto Emparan,Stefano Trezzi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了近极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞在量子涨落强烈的能量尺度下对标量场扰动的响应，发现其静态Love数不再为零（对于ℓ≥1的多极），并计算了完整的频率依赖的极化率，揭示了量子效应如何改变经典黑洞的潮汐形变性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典四维渐近平直广义相对论中黑洞的静态Love数为零，这是一个重要的经典结果。然而在近极端黑洞的近视界区域，当能量达到Schwarzian模式强烈量子涨落的尺度时，黑洞应作为具有态谱的量子系统处理。本文旨在研究量子效应如何改变这一经典零响应结果，探讨黑洞潮汐形变的量子本质。

Method: 1. 将近极端Reissner-Nordström黑洞视为具有离散能谱的量子系统；2. 通过虚跃迁计算静态极化率和Love数；3. 利用推迟格林函数定义潮汐响应（因喉部不再具有经典几何）；4. 计算格林函数在r₊ω领头阶的完整频率依赖性；5. 对保守部分进行重整化处理，近视界理论固定对数项系数，有限局域项作为四维理论的匹配数据；6. 分析耗散部分的频率依赖性和晚时行为。

Result: 1. 对于ℓ=0多极，由于标量场的平移对称性，静态Love数仍为零；2. 对于ℓ≥1多极，静态Love数非零——自旋ℓ中间态的有限转动能量破坏了导致经典结果为零的抵消机制；3. 保守部分需要重整化，包含近视界理论确定的对数项和能量依赖性；4. 耗散部分有限且方案独立；5. 对于ℓ≥1，当能量低于转动能隙时低频耗散消失，在能隙阈值处频率依赖变为非解析并产生晚时t⁻³/²尾波衰减。

Conclusion: 量子效应根本性地改变了黑洞的潮汐响应特性。近极端黑洞的静态Love数在ℓ≥1时不再为零，这源于量子态谱中中间态的有限转动能量。黑洞的响应表现出丰富的频率结构，包括需要重整化的保守部分和在转动能隙处出现非解析行为的耗散部分。这表明在强量子涨落区域，黑洞的潮汐形变必须从量子极化率的角度理解，而非经典几何响应，为理解黑洞的量子性质提供了新视角。

Abstract: The static Love numbers of black holes vanish in classical, four-dimensional, asymptotically flat General Relativity. We study how this changes for the response of near-extremal Reissner--Nordström black holes to a minimally coupled massless scalar, at energies where the Schwarzian mode of the near-horizon throat fluctuates strongly. The black hole then responds as a quantum system with a spectrum of states. Its static response is a polarizability, given by virtual transitions between them. For $\ell=0$ the static Love number vanishes, by the shift symmetry of the scalar, but for multipoles $\ell\geq1$ the static Love numbers are nonzero: the finite rotational energy of the spin-$\ell$ intermediate states spoils the cancellation responsible for the classical result. Since the throat no longer has a classical geometry from which to read off a tidal response, we define it through the retarded Green's function, and compute its full frequency dependence at leading order in $r_+ω$. Its conservative part requires renormalization: the near-horizon theory fixes the coefficient of its logarithm, and with it a dependence on the energy that no local term can remove, while its finite local terms are matching data of the four-dimensional theory. The dissipative part is finite and scheme independent. For $\ell\geq1$, it vanishes at low frequencies when the energy is below the rotational gap, while at the gap threshold it becomes non-analytic in frequency and produces a late-time $t^{-3/2}$ tail.

</details>


### [207] [Gauge-Invariant Brout--Englert--Higgs Mechanism without Spontaneous Breaking of Local Gauge Symmetry: A Quantum-Reference-Frame Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17264)
*Kei-Ichi Kondo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种规范不变的关系性框架来重新表述Brout-Englert-Higgs (BEH)机制,通过将Higgs场的规范取向作为动力学内部参考系,避免了对局域规范冗余赋予物理自发破缺的传统做法,并在电弱理论中展示了如何将SU(2)_L冗余关系化而保留残余电磁U(1)规范约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Higgs机制涉及对局域规范对称性的"自发破缺",但规范对称性本质上是冗余而非物理对称性,对其赋予物理破缺存在概念上的矛盾。为解决这一理论困境,需要建立一种规范不变的关系性表述,将Higgs场视为内部参考系而非破缺真空,从而在保持规范不变性的前提下理解质量生成机制,并澄清局域质量生成与全局对称性实现及拓扑阻碍之间的区别。

Method: 作者采用关系性量子参考系框架,将Higgs场的规范取向作为动力学内部参考数据(Higgs frame),相对于该参考系描述规范联络。在存在完整Higgs框架的正则区域上,利用Higgs动能项在规范轨道方向诱导正半定质量平方二次型,重现标准树图级矢量玻色子质量。完整Higgs框架产生规范不变的关系性联络,部分框架则仅在稳定子群H下定义并沿陪集方向G/H产生Higgs刚性。在量子场论中,归一化框架和关系性联络是非多项式复合变量,物理质量通过适当重整化的规范不变复合关联函数或残余带电扇区中的全着装算符来刻画。将此构造应用于电弱理论,展示Higgs框架如何将SU(2)_L冗余关系化同时保留残余电磁U(1)_em Gauss定律。

Result: 该框架成功复现了标准树图级矢量玻色子质量,并明确了不同粒子扇区的规范不变描述要求:W±玻色子和带电费米子模式需要电磁着装(electromagnetic dressing),而中性通道允许局域规范不变插入算符。该方法建立了Higgs着装、FMS复合算符和内部量子参考系语言之间的联系,在电弱理论中清晰地展示了SU(2)_L规范冗余的关系化处理与残余U(1)_em规范约束的保留。

Conclusion: 本文证明了BEH机制可以用规范不变的关系性语言完整表述,无需对局域规范冗余赋予物理自发破缺。通过将Higgs场作为动力学内部参考系,该框架保持了局域质量生成与全局对称性实现及拓扑结构的概念区分,为理解规范理论中的质量生成提供了概念上更清晰的理论基础,并统一了Higgs着装、复合算符和量子参考系等不同理论视角。

Abstract: The Brout--Englert--Higgs (BEH) mechanism can be formulated in gauge-invariant relational terms without assigning physical spontaneous breaking to a local gauge redundancy. We treat the gauge orientation of the Higgs field as dynamical internal reference data and describe the gauge connection relative to this Higgs frame. On a regular patch where the frame exists, the Higgs kinetic term induces a positive-semidefinite mass-squared quadratic form on the gauge-orbit directions and reproduces the standard tree-level vector masses. A complete Higgs frame yields a gauge-invariant relational connection, whereas a partial frame is defined only up to a stabilizer group $H$ and produces Higgs stiffness only along the coset directions $G/H$. In quantum field theory the normalized frame and relational connection are nonpolynomial composite variables; physical masses are therefore characterized through suitably renormalized gauge-invariant composite correlators, or through fully dressed operators in residual-charged sectors. Applying this construction to the electroweak theory, we show that the Higgs frame relationalizes the $SU(2)_L$ redundancy while leaving a residual electromagnetic $U(1)_{\rm em}$ Gauss law: $W^\pm$ and charged fermionic modes require electromagnetic dressing, whereas neutral channels admit local gauge-invariant interpolators. This formulation connects Higgs dressings, FMS composites, and internal quantum-reference-frame language while keeping local mass generation distinct from global symmetry realization and topological obstructions.

</details>


### [208] [Yang-Baxter deformed sigma models on $\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathrm{SO}(N)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17362)
*Danila Alexandrov,Alexey Bychkov,Ivan Kalinichenko,Alexey Litvinov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致对称空间SU(N)/SO(N)上二维sigma模型的Yang-Baxter形变,重点关注其紫外行为,发现紫外不动点由非紧致WZW模型SO(N,ℂ)/SO(N,ℝ)和N-1个紧致自由玻色子描述,并将构造推广至极大秩对称空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧致对称空间SU(N)/SO(N)上二维sigma模型的Yang-Baxter形变的紫外行为,这对于理解此类模型的重整化群流和量子性质至关重要。通过分析紫外不动点的结构,可以揭示这些可积形变系统的深层量子场论性质。

Method: 采用Yang-Baxter形变技术对紧致对称空间SU(N)/SO(N)上的二维sigma模型进行系统研究。通过分析重整化群流,确定紫外不动点的理论描述,并利用屏蔽流(screening currents)方法计算对不动点的首阶修正。将该分析框架推广应用于更广泛的极大秩对称空间类。

Result: 证明了紫外不动点由非紧致Wess-Zumino-Witten(WZW)模型SO(N,ℂ)/SO(N,ℝ)与N-1个紧致自由玻色子共同描述。成功确定了对该不动点的首阶修正,并将其识别为相应屏蔽流产生的微扰。成功将该构造方法推广至极大秩对称空间的更广泛类别。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Yang-Baxter形变的紧致对称空间sigma模型与非紧致WZW模型之间的深刻联系,揭示了其紫外不动点的精确理论描述为SO(N,ℂ)/SO(N,ℝ) WZW模型加上N-1个自由玻色子。通过屏蔽流机制阐明了偏离不动点的微扰结构,并证明该框架可推广至极大秩对称空间,为理解可积场论的量子性质提供了重要理论工具。

Abstract: We study Yang--Baxter deformations of two-dimensional sigma models on compact symmetric spaces $\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathrm{SO}(N)$, with particular emphasis on their ultraviolet behavior. We claim that the ultraviolet fixed point is described by the $\mathrm{SO}(N,\mathbb C)/\mathrm{SO}(N,\mathbb R)$ non-compact WZW model together with $N-1$ compact free bosons. We determine the leading correction to this fixed point and identify it with the perturbation generated by the corresponding screening currents. We also extend our construction to the broader class of symmetric spaces of maximal rank.

</details>


### [209] [Type I-II/II-I vortices from dimension-six self-interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17437)
*A. Alonso-Izquierdo,A. González-Parra,K. Slawinska,A. Wereszczynski,T. Winyard*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在阿贝尔-希格斯(Ginzburg-Landau)模型中引入六维自相互作用项，发现了丰富的多涡旋解结构，包括标准的I型和II型涡旋外，还识别出两种新型涡旋：I-II型(短程吸引长程排斥)和II-I型(短程排斥长程吸引)，极大地扩展了涡旋物理的相互作用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的阿贝尔-希格斯模型仅能描述I型(吸引)和II型(排斥)两种涡旋相互作用类型，由BPS极限分隔。为了探索更丰富的涡旋动力学行为和相互作用模式，需要研究高阶相互作用项对涡旋解结构的影响，从而揭示超出标准分类的新物理现象。

Method: 在标准的阿贝尔-希格斯(Ginzburg-Landau)模型中引入六维(dimension-six)自相互作用项，通过理论分析和数值计算研究修正后模型的多涡旋解。系统地研究涡旋间相互作用随距离的变化规律，分析不同模型参数和拓扑荷(缠绕数)对涡旋配置和稳定性的影响。

Result: 发现了两种新型涡旋相互作用机制：(1)I-II型涡旋表现出短程吸引、长程排斥的特性，轴对称多涡旋解是稳定的，但是否为基态取决于模型参数和拓扑荷；(2)II-I型涡旋表现出短程排斥、长程吸引的行为，多涡旋解会形成Abrikosov晶格的块状结构或团簇。这两种新型涡旋丰富了超出标准I型和II型分类的涡旋物理图景。

Conclusion: 通过在阿贝尔-希格斯模型中引入六维自相互作用项，成功构建了具有更丰富相互作用模式的涡旋理论框架。除了传统的I型和II型涡旋外，新发现的I-II型和II-I型涡旋展现出距离依赖的复杂相互作用行为，为超导体、宇宙弦等涡旋物理系统提供了新的理论视角和可能的实验预测。

Abstract: We show that adding a dimension-six self-interaction to the Abelian-Higgs or Ginzburg Landau model results in a remarkable and rich structure of multi-vortex solutions. Besides the standard type I (attractive) and type II (repulsive) regimes, separated by the Bogomol'nyi-Prasad-Sommerfeld limit (BPS), we identify two new possibilities: type I-II vortices, which attract at short distances and repel at long distances; and type II-I vortices, with the opposite behaviour. Specifically, within the type II-I regime, multi-vortex solutions form chunks of the Abrikosov lattice or clusters. Conversely, in the I-II regime, axially symmetric multi-vortices are stable; nevertheless, depending on the model parameters and topological charge (winding number), they can be either the ground state or not.

</details>


### [210] [Evading the Swampland de Sitter Obstruction via Thermodynamic Duality-Induced Horizon Corrections in Unimodular Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17438)
*Reza Baghbani*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了单模引力耦合幂律非线性电动力学在量子修正视界热力学框架下的早期宇宙学效应，通过热力学对偶原理导出的熵修正项产生新的Friedmann方程高阶项（H⁴和H⁶），这些量子引力修正能够在无需基本暴胀子势的情况下自然驱动宇宙暴胀，并与Planck 2018观测数据高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宇宙暴胀理论依赖于基本标量场（暴胀子）及其势能，但面临精细调节问题和沼泽地猜想的约束挑战。本文旨在探索一种基于量子引力效应的替代机制：通过热力学对偶原理导出的视界熵量子修正（对数修正和反面积修正），在单模引力和幂律非线性电动力学框架下，研究这些量子修正能否在高能早期宇宙中自然驱动暴胀，同时满足沼泽地纲领的理论约束并与观测数据相符。

Method: 1. 理论框架构建：将单模引力与幂律非线性电动力学耦合，引入基于热力学对偶原理的量子修正视界热力学，在Bekenstein-Hawking熵中加入对数修正和反面积修正项；2. 推导修正Friedmann方程：从修正的熵关系出发，导出包含H⁴和H⁶高阶项的修正Friedmann方程，这些项代表真实的量子引力效应；3. 相空间动力学分析：对修正方程进行全面的相空间分析，研究早期de Sitter吸引子的存在性和稳定性；4. 沼泽地猜想检验：分析修正因子F(H)如何改变有效慢滚参数，验证模型是否满足沼泽地de Sitter猜想和距离猜想；5. 观测量计算与数据对比：计算标量谱指数ns和张标比r等暴胀观测量，与Planck 2018数据进行对比验证。

Result: 1. 成功导出了包含H⁴和H⁶量子修正项的新型Friedmann方程，这些高阶项在高能暴胀阶段具有显著动力学作用；2. 相空间分析表明量子修正项能够驱动早期de Sitter类吸引子，为持续暴胀膨胀提供稳健机制，且无需引入基本暴胀子势能；3. 修正因子F(H)有效改变了慢滚参数，使模型在暴胀期间同时满足沼泽地de Sitter猜想和距离猜想的约束条件；4. 计算得到的标量谱指数ns和张标比r与Planck 2018观测数据高度吻合，显示出优异的观测一致性；5. 证明了热力学对偶诱导的视界修正为在量子引力框架内实现成功暴胀提供了可行且可检验的途径。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于热力学对偶原理的量子修正视界热力学能够在单模引力和非线性电动力学框架下自然产生宇宙暴胀。通过对Bekenstein-Hawking熵的对数和反面积修正，导出的高阶Friedmann方程项（H⁴和H⁶）代表真实的量子引力效应，在高能早期宇宙中动力学显著，能够驱动稳定的de Sitter暴胀吸引子。关键的是，该机制无需依赖基本暴胀子势能，同时满足弦论沼泽地纲领的理论约束，并且预言的暴胀观测量与Planck 2018数据精确符合。这表明热力学对偶诱导的视界量子修正为早期宇宙暴胀提供了一个有良好理论动机、可观测检验且与量子引力相容的新范式。

Abstract: We investigate the early-universe cosmological implications of Unimodular Gravity coupled to Power-Law Nonlinear Electrodynamics in the presence of quantum-corrected horizon thermodynamics derived from the principle of thermodynamic duality. By incorporating both logarithmic and inverse-area corrections to the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, we derive modified Friedmann equations exhibiting novel $H^4$ and $H^6$ terms that represent genuine quantum gravity effects. We demonstrate that these higher-order corrections become dynamically significant in the high-energy regime of cosmic inflation, naturally modifying the slow-roll dynamics without requiring a fundamental inflaton potential. A comprehensive phase-space analysis reveals that the quantum corrections drive early-time de Sitter-like attractors, providing a robust mechanism for sustained inflationary expansion. Confronting the model with the Swampland program, we show that the correction factor $\mathcal{F}(H)$ modifies the effective slow-roll parameters, enabling the model to satisfy the Swampland de Sitter and Distance conjectures during the inflationary epoch. We calculate the inflationary observables, including the spectral index $n_s$ and the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, and demonstrate that the model's predictions are in excellent agreement with the Planck 2018 observational constraints. Our results suggest that thermodynamic duality-induced horizon corrections offer a viable and testable pathway for realizing successful inflation within a well-motivated quantum-gravitational framework.

</details>


### [211] [Resonant JPZ algebras and 3D Chern--Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17480)
*Remigiusz Durka,Nelson Merino,D. M. Peñafiel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了六个共振JPZ代数的三维Chern-Simons引力理论,这些代数由Lorentz生成元、平移生成元和额外的类Lorentz生成元构成。研究发现其中三个代数同构于iso(2,1)⊕so(2,1),但在保持原始JPZ场识别时产生不同的引力实现,并系统分析了各代数的不变张量、规范曲率、场方程及其引力解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的三维引力理论框架需要扩展以包含更丰富的对称结构。通过引入额外的类Lorentz生成元Z_a,可以构造满足共振条件的新型JPZ代数,从而探索这些代数在Chern-Simons引力理论中的实现及其物理意义。半群展开方法恰好产生六种可能的代数配置,需要系统研究它们的几何和引力性质。

Method: 采用半群展开方法从Lorentz生成元J_a、平移生成元P_a和额外生成元Z_a出发,结合共振条件和Jacobi恒等式构造六个JPZ代数配置。对每个代数确定其不变张量、规范曲率和Chern-Simons作用量,推导场方程并表示为紧凑形式Ω_σF=0。利用直和同构关系iso(2,1)⊕so(2,1)对局域度规扇区进行分类,同时保持原始(ω,e,h)场的引力物理解释,将结果与已知的全局和渐近解联系起来。

Result: 确认了六个共振JPZ代数配置中,B_4、C̃_4和C_4三个代数同构于iso(2,1)⊕so(2,1),但在固定原始JPZ场识别时产生不同的引力实现。成功推导出各代数的完整Chern-Simons引力理论,包括不变张量、规范曲率、场方程及最大秩耦合扇区。场方程的紧凑形式清晰展示了附加场h^a对曲率、挠率和其他规范曲率分量的贡献方式。通过直和同构实现了对局域度规扇区的系统分类,并建立了与已知引力解的对应关系。

Conclusion: 本研究系统构建了六个共振JPZ代数的三维Chern-Simons引力理论框架,揭示了其中三个代数虽然代数同构但产生物理上不同的引力实现这一重要现象。附加场h^a的引入丰富了引力理论的几何结构,场方程的紧凑形式Ω_σF=0为理解这些扩展引力理论提供了统一的数学框架。通过保持原始场变量的引力解释,成功将抽象的代数结构与具体的时空几何联系起来,为研究更广泛的三维引力理论及其解提供了新的理论工具和分类方案。

Abstract: In this work we construct three-dimensional Chern--Simons gravity theories for the six resonant $JPZ$ algebras generated by the Lorentz generators $J_a$, translations $P_a$, and an additional Lorentz-like generator $Z_a$. The resonance condition together with the Jacobi identities leaves precisely six configurations arising from the semigroup expansions. Our main result is that three of them, $B_4$, $\widetilde C_4$, and $C_4$, are isomorphic to $iso(2,1)\oplus so(2,1)$, although they yield different gravitational realizations when we keep the original $JPZ$ field identification fixed. For each case $JPZ$, we determine the invariant tensors, gauge curvatures, Chern--Simons action, field equations, and the corresponding maximal-rank coupling sectors. The field equations can be written in the compact form $Ω_σ\mathbf F=0$, which makes explicit how the additional field $h^a$ contributes to the curvature, torsion, and the remaining gauge-curvature components. We use the direct-sum isomorphisms to classify the local metric sectors and to relate them to known global and asymptotic solutions, while keeping the gravitational interpretation tied to the original $(ω,e,h)$ fields.

</details>


### [212] [Beyond the circle: current algebras, geometry and higher gauge structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17781)
*R. Campoamor-Stursberg,A. Marrani,M. Rausch de Traubenberg*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究将圆替换为流形所导致的流李代数及其中心扩张和导子扩张，通过分离系数代数、调和分析和几何数据，利用微分模恰当形式描述中心扩张，并给出紧致流形和对称空间的显式构造，同时讨论了高阶李代数的中心扩张条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的流李代数理论基于圆周，但将其推广到一般流形上需要系统的数学框架。现有的调和构造方法与上同调理论、环几何理论之间的关系尚不清晰。此外，高阶李代数(order F)的中心扩张理论需要更深入的研究，特别是如何将动力学理论的要求与代数结构联系起来。

Method: 1) 将流李代数的构造分解为系数代数、调和分析和几何数据三个独立部分;2) 使用微分模恰当形式的商空间描述中心扩张;3) 通过无散度向量场给出显式的余循环;4) 利用稳定子方程确定相容的导子;5) 对紧致流形(如三维球面)和对称空间(如SL(2,R)的对称空间)进行具体分析;6) 比较Campoamor-Stursberg等人的调和构造方法与Coquereaux等人的上同调工作以及Castellani的环几何理论;7) 通过系数代数构造将流代数与高阶括号联系,并研究F阶李代数的中心扩张所满足的耦合二元和对称F元条件。

Result: 1) 建立了流形上流李代数中心扩张和导子扩张的系统理论框架;2) 给出了三维球面及其流-Virasoro扩张的紧致例子;3) 阐明了SL(2,R)及其对称空间的解析条件以及软几何中传输乘法的作用;4) 建立了调和构造方法与上同调理论、环几何理论之间的联系;5) 证明了F阶李代数的中心扩张需要满足耦合的二元和对称F元条件;6) 提供了具体的例子和阻碍性结果;7) 通过系数代数构造澄清了动力学理论的附加要求。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将流李代数理论从圆周推广到一般流形,建立了统一的数学框架来处理中心扩张和导子扩张。通过分离系数代数、调和分析和几何数据,该方法不仅系统化了现有的各种构造方法(调和方法、上同调方法、环几何方法),而且为高阶李代数的中心扩张理论提供了新的见解。研究表明,动力学理论对代数结构有特殊的要求,这可以通过系数代数构造来精确表征。该工作为无限维李代数、自由微分代数以及软几何等领域提供了重要的理论工具。

Abstract: Replacing the circle by a manifold leads to current Lie algebras and their central and derivation extensions. We separate the coefficient algebra, harmonic analysis and geometric data. Differentials modulo exact forms describe central extensions; divergence-free vector fields give explicit cocycles, whose compatible derivations satisfy a stabilizer equation. Compact examples include the three-sphere and its current--Virasoro extension. We also explain the analytic qualifications for SL(2,R) and its symmetric space and the role of transported multiplication in soft geometries. The recent harmonic constructions of Campoamor-Stursberg, Marrani and Rausch de Traubenberg are compared with the cohomological work of Coquereaux, Frappat, Ragoucy and Sorba and Castellani's loop geometry and infinite-dimensional free differential algebras. A coefficient-algebra construction connects currents to higher brackets and clarifies the additional requirements of a dynamical theory. For Lie algebras of order F, central extensions obey coupled binary and symmetric F-ary conditions, with explicit examples and obstructions.

</details>


### [213] [Construction and geometric properties of the fuzzy $CP^3$ onion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17807)
*Kohei Uno,Tomohiro Inagaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由同心模糊CP³构建的七维量子空间矩阵模型,证明其连续极限是CP³上的度量锥C(CP³)而非平坦的R⁷空间,并通过自由拉普拉斯算子谱、传播子和热扩散等多个方面验证了这一几何预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子空间的几何结构通常需要通过矩阵模型来描述,但其连续极限的几何性质往往不明确。本研究旨在严格确定由递增矩阵尺寸的同心模糊CP³构成的七维量子空间的连续极限几何,验证其是否为预期的度量锥结构而非平坦空间,从而为非交换几何和量子引力理论提供精确的几何预测。

Method: 采用矩阵模型构建由同心模糊CP³组成的七维量子空间,利用Schwinger玻色子提升的极分解和已知算子恒等式推导相邻层之间的映射关系。通过分析自由拉普拉斯算子在不同角动量扇区(CP³上p阶调和函数)的谱,计算自由传播子,以及研究热扩散过程中的r⁶测度和Bessel谱,系统地测试连续极限的几何性质。将结果与度量锥C(CP³)和平坦R⁷空间进行二阶收敛性比较。

Result: 在s波扇区(p=0)中,度量锥与平坦空间的结果一致;但在高角动量扇区(p≥1)中两者显著分离。在测试的每个扇区中,自由拉普拉斯算子谱以二阶收敛到度量锥的谱,在20480层时对p≤8的匹配精度达到1×10⁻⁹,而平坦空间的偏差为8-18%。自由传播子以相同阶数收敛到锥的传播子,热扩散再现了锥的r⁶测度和Bessel谱。相邻层映射与Kováčik-Tekel系数复制方案一致,对于CP¹情况简化为Kováčik-Tekel模糊洋葱结构。扭量纤维上的模式与S⁴上的调和函数精确简并。

Conclusion: 七维量子空间矩阵模型的连续极限被严格证明是CP³上的度量锥C(CP³)而非平坦的R⁷空间,这是一个几何预测而非输入假设。该结果在多个物理量(拉普拉斯算子谱、传播子、热扩散)上得到高精度验证,为非交换几何中的量子空间提供了明确的几何解释,并揭示了模糊空间层状结构与连续几何之间的精确对应关系。

Abstract: We consider the matrix model of a seven-dimensional quantum space built from concentric fuzzy $CP^3$'s of increasing matrix size, and show that its continuum limit is the metric cone $C(CP^3)$ over $CP^3$, not flat $R^7$. Every ingredient is fixed before any comparison, so this geometry is a prediction rather than an input. The two geometries agree in the $s$ wave ($p=0$) but separate in the higher angular sectors (harmonics on $CP^3$ of degree $p\geq1$): in each sector tested, the spectrum of the free Laplacian converges at second order to that of the cone, and at 20480 layers the cone is matched to $1\times10^{-9}$ for $p\leq8$, whereas flat space misses by 8-18%. The free propagator converges to that of the cone at the same order, and heat diffusion reproduces the $r^6$ measure and the Bessel spectrum of the cone. The maps between neighbouring layers are obtained in closed form from the polar decomposition of the second-quantized Schwinger-boson lift, using a known operator identity, and coincide with the Kováčik-Tekel coefficient-copying prescription. For $CP^1$ the construction reduces, away from the outer boundary, to the Kováčik-Tekel fuzzy onion. Modes along the twistor fibre are exactly degenerate with the harmonics on $S^4$.

</details>


### [214] [Supersymmetric Observables from Surface Defect Anomalies in 4d and 6d SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18086)
*Zi-Xiao Huang,Ma-Ke Yuan,Yang Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维和六维超共形场论中半BPS表面缺陷对超对称Casimir能量和超对称Rényi熵的普适对数项的贡献，通过等变积分缺陷反常多项式获得了用缺陷't Hooft反常表示的闭合表达式，并利用手征代数、五维定域化和全息探针膜等多种方法进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超共形场论中，表面缺陷会对重要的物理观测量（如Casimir能量和Rényi熵）产生贡献。研究者希望通过系统性的方法，利用缺陷的反常结构来计算这些贡献，特别是对于四维和六维理论中保持部分超对称的半BPS表面缺陷。这些计算不仅有助于理解缺陷的量子性质，还能通过多种独立方法进行交叉验证，从而深化对超对称场论中缺陷物理的理解。

Method: 本文采用等变积分方法处理缺陷反常多项式，并将其推广到超对称Rényi背景。具体而言：(1) 对四维N=4超Yang-Mills理论中保持横向旋转的Gukov-Witten缺陷，利用R-对称反常进行计算；(2) 对六维N=(1,0)理论中的N=(0,4)缺陷，考虑引力反常的额外贡献；(3) 使用手征代数、五维超对称定域化技术、大N极限下的全息探针膜方法以及定域手征费米子分析进行多角度验证；(4) 将框架应用于六维N=(2,0)理论中的Wilson面。

Result: 研究获得了用缺陷't Hooft反常表示的超对称Casimir能量和Rényi熵普适对数项的闭合表达式。对于四维N=4理论中的Gukov-Witten缺陷，两个观测量仅依赖于单一的R-对称反常；对于六维理论中的N=(0,4)缺陷，引力反常产生了额外的贡献项。通过手征代数、五维定域化、全息方法和定域手征费米子的计算，成功验证了这些结果的一致性，其中定域手征费米子精确再现了引力反常的贡献。该框架还成功恢复了六维N=(2,0)理论中已知的Wilson面结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个系统性框架，通过等变积分缺陷反常多项式来计算超共形场论中半BPS表面缺陷对Casimir能量和Rényi熵的贡献。结果表明，这些可观测量可以完全用缺陷的't Hooft反常（包括R-对称反常和引力反常）来表征。多种独立计算方法（手征代数、定域化、全息对偶和手征费米子）之间的一致性验证了该方法的有效性和普适性，为研究更广泛的超对称理论中的缺陷物理提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: We study the contributions of half-BPS surface defects to supersymmetric Casimir energy and the universal logarithmic term of supersymmetric Rényi entropy in four- and six-dimensional SCFTs. Using equivariant integration of defect anomaly polynomials and its extension to supersymmetric Rényi backgrounds, we obtain closed expressions for these observables in terms of defect 't Hooft anomalies. For Gukov--Witten defects preserving transverse rotations in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 4$ super Yang--Mills theories, both observables depend on a single R-symmetry anomaly. For $\mathcal{N} = (0, 4)$ defects in six-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = (1, 0)$ theories, additional terms arise from the gravitational anomaly. We provide complementary checks using chiral algebras, five-dimensional localization, and holographic probe branes at large $N$, while localized chiral fermions reproduce the gravitational-anomaly contributions. The framework also recovers Wilson-surface results in six-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = (2, 0)$ theories.

</details>


### [215] [Geometry and holography of $\mathrm{AdS}_2\times\mathrm{S}^2$: towards the field theory dual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18344)
*Miroslava Mosso Rojas,Anayeli Ramirez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了保持八个超荷的Type IIB超引力中AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ解的全息对偶，提出其对偶理论是定义在Σ上二维晶格上的N=4超共形量子力学，并通过非阿贝尔T对偶构造的具体例子验证了该提案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超对称AdS₂×S²解的全息对偶理论尚未被系统识别。特别是对于Type IIB超引力中保持八个超荷的AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ型解，需要确定其对偶的超共形量子力学理论，并理解其微观自由度如何与引力侧的膜配置和电荷分布相对应。

Method: 1) 分析AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ解的膜内容，识别出D1-D5-NS5交叉结构以及D3和D7味膜；2) 提出对偶的N=4量子力学理论组织在Σ空间的二维晶格上，沿x₁方向由线性quiver理论描述（对应U(1)不变解），沿x₂方向复制多个quiver，每个晶格单元的秩由相应的D1和D5电荷确定；3) 验证中心荷在leading order与全息计算一致；4) 研究massive Type IIA超引力AdS₃解的SL(2,ℝ)非阿贝尔T对偶以及AdS₃×S³×CY₂的SL(2,ℝ)×SU(2)非阿贝尔T对偶作为具体例子；5) 为这些解提出包含额外D3和D7膜的全局完备化方案，将沿x₂方向色膜与额外膜之间的基本弦解释为重子顶点；6) 将该提案推广到定义函数在x₁和x₂方向因子化的一般解类。

Result: 1) 成功识别了Type IIB中AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ解的膜配置为D1-D5-NS5交叉加上D3和D7味膜；2) 构造的二维晶格量子力学模型的中心荷与全息计算在leading order一致；3) 证明了massive Type IIA的AdS₃解的SL(2,ℝ)非阿贝尔T对偶以及AdS₃×S³×CY₂的SL(2,ℝ)×SU(2)非阿贝尔T对偶属于该解类；4) 为这些非阿贝尔T对偶解提出了包含额外D3和D7膜的全局完备化；5) 沿x₂方向的基本弦被成功解释为重子顶点；6) 该提案可推广到定义函数因子化的更广泛解类，包括AdS₂和S²具有相同warp因子的情况。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地提出了Type IIB超引力中保持八个超荷的AdS₂×S²×CY₂×Σ解的全息对偶理论框架——组织在二维晶格上的N=4超共形量子力学。通过非阿贝尔T对偶构造的具体例子验证了该提案的有效性，中心荷计算与全息结果一致。沿x₂方向的结构通过重子顶点得到物理解释。该工作为理解AdS₂/CFT₁对偶提供了新的视角，并为进一步研究更一般的AdS₂解的微观描述奠定了基础。

Abstract: We take a first step in identifying the superconformal quantum mechanics dual to supersymmetric AdS$_2\times$S$^2$ solutions. We focus on the Type IIB solutions of the form AdS$_2\times$S$^2\times$CY$_2\timesΣ$ that preserve eight supercharges, with $Σ$ a two-dimensional space parametrised by $(x_1,x_2)$, and read off their brane content, a D1-D5-NS5 intersection with D3 and D7 flavour branes. We propose that their dual $\mathcal{N}=4$ quantum mechanics is organised over a two-dimensional lattice of cells in $Σ$. Namely, it consists of copies of the linear quiver along $x_1$ dual to the U(1)-invariant solutions of the class, one for each interval along $x_2$, with ranks given by the D1 and D5 charges of the corresponding cell. Its central charge agrees with the holographic one at leading order. We show that the family of SL$(2,\mathbb{R})$ non-Abelian T-duals of the AdS$_3\times$S$^2\times$CY$_2$ solutions of massive Type IIA supergravity, constructed in previous work, belongs to this class, as does the SL$(2,\mathbb{R})\times$SU$(2)$ non-Abelian T-dual of AdS$_3\times$S$^3\times$CY$_2$, and work both out in detail. For both we propose global completions that introduce additional D3 and D7 branes. Along $x_2$ we interpret the fundamental strings stretched between the colour branes and the additional D3 and D7 branes as baryon vertices. Finally, we extend this proposal from the non-Abelian T-duals to the solutions of the class whose defining functions factorise in $x_1$ and $x_2$, including those in which the AdS$_2$ and S$^2$ factors have the same warp factor.

</details>


### [216] [Giant Graviton Expansions from Symmetric Functions and Free Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18452)
*Min-xin Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对四维N=4 U(N)超对称Yang-Mills理论中巨引力子展开的两个关系式给出了详细的代数证明,包括Gaiotto-Lee单求和展开和对称双求和展开,并讨论了相关的物理和数学问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巨引力子展开将超对称指标的有限秩修正用缠绕D3-膜的指标表示,已有两个关系式被提出但缺乏严格的代数证明。需要对这些展开关系进行系统的数学验证,并明确其解析延拓和系数的正当性,同时探讨更一般的三物种展开和Schur指标的模性质所引发的深层问题。

Method: 第一,利用Hall-Littlewood正交性和算子迹的初等计算证明了完整超共形指标的Gaiotto-Lee单求和展开。第二,通过分解精确自由费米子模乘积证明了带味Schur指标的对称双求和展开。对膜指标进行解析延拓的明确规定,并逐项证明求和系数的合理性。两个证明适用于不同的展开处方。

Result: 成功给出了Gaiotto-Lee单求和展开和对称双求和展开的严格代数证明,明确了膜指标的解析延拓形式,逐项验证了展开系数的正确性。但这两个证明未能建立Imamura提出的完整三物种展开。

Conclusion: 本文为四维N=4超对称Yang-Mills理论中的两个重要巨引力子展开关系提供了严格的数学证明,验证了不同展开处方下的有效性。然而,Imamura的完整三物种展开以及Schur指标的模性质仍然提出了需要进一步研究的物理和数学问题,这些问题有待后续工作解决。

Abstract: Giant graviton expansions express finite-rank corrections to supersymmetric indices in terms of indices for wrapped D3-branes. We give detailed algebraic proofs of two previously proposed relations for four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ $U(N)$ super-Yang--Mills theory. The first is the Gaiotto--Lee single-sum expansion of the full superconformal index, proved by Hall--Littlewood orthogonality and an elementary calculation of operator traces. The second is the symmetric double-sum expansion of the flavored Schur index, proved by factorizing its exact free-fermion mode product. We specify the analytic continuation of the brane indices and justify the sums coefficient by coefficient. The two proofs apply to different expansion prescriptions and do not establish the full three-species expansion of Imamura. We discuss the additional physical and mathematical questions raised by that expansion and by the modular properties of the Schur index.

</details>


### [217] [Black Strings and Black Holes: Worldsheet Spectra and Near-Extremal Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18591)
*Ziyi Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了 $\frac{SL(2,\mathbb R)\times U(1)}{U(1)}$ 规范化 WZW 模型的欧几里得配分函数，分析了不同紧化条件下的无视界几何和黑弦时空的世界面谱与时空谱，并在近极端情况下识别出产生 AdS$_2$ 喉部熵标度行为的候选态塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在深入理解规范化 WZW 模型的配分函数在不同几何背景（无视界几何和 Horne-Horowitz 黑弦）下的性质，特别是探索近极端黑洞/黑弦的微观态起源，以及如何从世界面理论中恢复时空谱和熵的标度行为，从而加深对弦论中黑洞微观态计数和 AdS/CFT 对应的理解。

Method: 首先通过完整性积分（holonomy integrals）研究世界面谱并获得特征标分解；然后对无视界几何直接执行展开的模积分以获得时空谱，并在非规范化极限下恢复 Maldacena-Ooguri-Son 谱；对带电三维黑弦进行维度约化得到二维带电黑洞；针对黑洞和黑弦的近极端区域，从世界面离散系列表示中提出两个候选态塔，分析其对配分函数的贡献。

Result: 成功获得了世界面谱的特征标分解和无视界几何的时空谱；在非规范化极限下恢复了 Maldacena-Ooguri-Son 谱；对于二维黑洞，识别出两个候选态塔能够产生预期的 AdS$_2$ 喉部的 $\frac{3}{2}\log T$ 熵标度行为；这些态塔仅在振幅发散临界温度以下出现；分析了这些态在近极端情况下对完整配分函数的贡献。

Conclusion: 本研究系统地建立了 $\frac{SL(2,\mathbb R)\times U(1)}{U(1)}$ 规范化 WZW 模型在不同几何背景下的配分函数计算框架，成功从世界面理论中导出时空谱并恢复已知结果。特别是在近极端黑洞情况下，识别出的候选态塔能够再现正确的熵标度行为，为理解黑洞微观态的弦论起源提供了重要线索，展示了世界面方法在研究黑洞热力学和量子性质方面的有效性。

Abstract: We study aspects of the Euclidean partition function of the $\frac{SL(2,\mathbb R)\times U(1)}{U(1)}$ gauged WZW model. The target space geometry is either a smooth horizonless geometry with a linear-dilaton asymptotic region, or the Horne--Horowitz black string, depending on which circles we unwind as the time-direction. We first study the worldsheet spectrum and obtain the character decomposition via the holonomy integrals. Next, we obtain the spacetime spectrum of the horizonless geometry by directly performing the unfolded modular integrals, and recover the Maldacena-Ooguri-Son spectrum in the ungauged limit. When the chirally charged three-dimensional black string is dimensionally reduced, the target space is instead a two-dimensional charged black hole. We study the near extremal regime of the black hole and black string. For the two dimensional black hole, we propose two candidate towers of states that produce the expected $\frac{3}{2}\log T$ of the AdS$_2$ throat from the worldsheet discrete series representation. These towers appear only below the temperature at which the full amplitude becomes divergent. We comment on the contribution of these states in the near-extremal limit of the full partition function.

</details>


### [218] [Carrollian Yang-Mills theories in the homogeneous temporal gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18715)
*Carmelo P. Martin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在齐次时间规范下对具有SU(N)规范对称性的电型和磁型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论进行BRST路径积分量子化，发现电型理论的所有相互作用顶点消失而退化为自由理论，磁型理论的基本场格林函数无圈图贡献且仅在树图层次存在三点和四点函数的耦合常数依赖，同时Wilson圈算符的格林函数在两种理论中表现出不同的平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Carrollian Yang-Mills理论的量子化性质，这是一类具有Carroll对称性（光速趋于零极限）的规范场论。通过量子化电型和磁型两种不同构型的理论，探索其量子结构、相互作用特性以及与传统Yang-Mills理论的区别，为理解平直时空的渐近对称性和全息对偶提供理论基础。

Method: 采用BRST（Becchi-Rouet-Stora-Tyutin）方法在路径积分形式下进行量子化。选择齐次时间规范作为规范固定条件，对基本表示下具有SU(N)规范对称性的电型和磁型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论分别进行处理。通过分析传播子、相互作用顶点结构以及费曼图的圈图贡献，系统研究基本场的格林函数和Wilson圈算符的格林函数的量子性质。

Result: 电型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论：所有相互作用顶点完全消失，理论退化为自由场论；Wilson圈的格林函数非平凡。磁型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论：基本场的格林函数（不涉及复合算符）没有圈图贡献；唯一依赖于耦合常数的连通格林函数是场ξ_i的三点和四点函数的树图贡献；涉及Wilson圈的格林函数是平凡的。两种理论展现出截然不同的量子结构和相互作用模式。

Conclusion: 电型和磁型Carrollian Yang-Mills理论在量子层面表现出根本性差异。电型理论由于相互作用顶点的消失而成为自由理论，但保留了非平凡的Wilson圈结构；磁型理论虽保留了树图层次的相互作用（仅限于ξ_i场的三点和四点函数），但不存在圈图修正，且Wilson圈格林函数平凡。这些结果揭示了Carrollian极限下规范场论的独特量子性质，为进一步研究Carroll时空的量子场论和可能的全息应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We quantize, in the homogeneous temporal gauge, both the electric and the magnetic Carrollian Yang-Mills theories with SU(N) gauge symmetry in the fundamental representation. We use the BRST method implemented in the path integral. We show that in the first case all the interaction vertices vanish, thus, giving a free theory. In the magnetic case, there are no loop contributions to the Green functions of the elementary fields --i.e., Green functions not involving composite operators-- and the only connected parts of such Green functions with a dependence on the coupling constant are the tree level contributions to the three and four point functions of the field $ξ_i$. We also show that in the magnetic case the Green functions involving only Wilson loops are trivial and not so in the electric case.

</details>


### [219] [Imaginary HTEE: Probing Criticality and Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18716)
*M. Ali-Akbari,M. M. Daryaei Goki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究带电AdS₅黑洞背景下全息类时纠缠熵(HTEE)的虚部,发现其作为赝熵虚部可探测强耦合系统临界行为,在临界点处初末态量子相干性达到最大,并解析获得临界指数θ_I = 1/2


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对强耦合系统临界点附近量子相干性和非局域量子信息性质的有效探测手段。全息类时纠缠熵的虚部作为一个新的量子信息量,可能为理解双场理论在存在化学势和温度时的临界行为提供新视角,特别是揭示初末态之间的量子相干性如何响应热力学相变

Method: 采用全息对偶原理,在带电渐近AdS₅黑洞背景中计算全息类时纠缠熵(HTEE)的虚部;将HTEE虚部与赝熵虚部建立联系;推导该量在无量纲比值μ/T(化学势/温度)变化下的解析表达式;分析稳定和不稳定热力学分支上的行为;研究临界点附近的标度行为,通过求导提取临界指数

Result: 解析推导出HTEE虚部的表达式,发现该量在不带电极限下消失,完全由非零化学势诱导;当μ/T接近临界值时,HTEE虚部的导数呈现平方根倒数发散;解析提取临界指数θ_I = 1/2;验证该指数同时支配固定化学势下的温度响应;结果表明初末态在临界点处达到最大量子相干性

Conclusion: 全息类时纠缠熵的虚部可作为强耦合系统临界性的非局域量子信息探针。化学势是诱导量子相干性的关键因素,临界指数θ_I = 1/2解析地刻画了系统在临界点附近的普适标度行为。该研究为利用全息方法理解量子相干性在热力学相变中的作用提供了新工具,并揭示了初末态量子相干性在临界点处的极大化现象

Abstract: We study the imaginary part of the holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE) in a charged asymptotically AdS$_5$ black-hole background whose dual field theory exhibits a critical point in the presence of a chemical potential $μ$ and temperature $T$. Identifying the imaginary part of the HTEE with the imaginary part of the pseudoentropy, we derive an analytical expression for this quantity and investigate its behavior on the stable and unstable thermodynamic branches. We find that the imaginary HTEE vanishes in the uncharged limit and is induced entirely by a nonzero chemical potential, signaling the emergence of quantum coherence between the initial and final states. As the dimensionless ratio $μ/T$ approaches its critical value, we obtain an inverse-square-root divergence in the derivative of the imaginary HTEE and extract the critical exponent $θ_I = 1/2$ analytically. We further show that the same exponent governs the temperature response at fixed chemical potential. Our results establish the imaginary part of the HTEE as a non-local quantum-information probe of criticality in strongly coupled systems, and they imply that the initial and final states become maximally coherent at the critical point.

</details>


### [220] [Late-time electric screening in dS from EAdS loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18782)
*Ernesto Bianchi,Sacha Ployet,Charlotte Sleight,Massimo Taronna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在标量QED理论框架下,利用EAdS重构的in-in形式计算了dS时空中规范场的单圈两点函数,揭示了Dirichlet模式的Schwinger型质量生成机制,建立了谱Cutkosky规则,并证明圈修正的光子表现为dS共振态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在de Sitter时空中理解量子场论的非微扰效应具有重要意义,特别是规范场的圈修正行为。需要发展系统的计算方法来处理dS时空中的单圈量子修正,理解边界流的反常维度、规范场质量生成机制以及与边界共形场论的对应关系,同时建立dS时空中的谱表示和Cutkosky规则

Method: 采用EAdS(欧氏反de Sitter)重构的in-in形式主义进行解析计算;对一般维度和标量质量参数计算标量QED理论中的单圈规范场两点函数;对in-in轮廓分支求和得到推迟dS气泡核;建立谱Cutkosky规则,将核函数在Casimir平面主级数切割上的不连续性用两粒子切割表示;通过色散关系确定核函数的完整非局域部分;在复Casimir平面进行解析延拓分析圈修正光子的共振极点结构

Result: Dirichlet EAdS规范场获得Schwinger型质量,其纵向模式由满足相反边界条件的带电标量复合态提供,而Neumann模式受规范不变性保护;在dS时空中,Dirichlet修正为边界流诱导反常维度γ_J,并改变电场的晚时衰减行为;bulk计算重现了边界多重态重组结果;完整气泡系数涉及₇F₆超几何函数,但切割简化为Gamma函数的闭合乘积;通过解析延拓完整重现单圈γ_J结果;在复Casimir平面上,圈修正的Dirichlet光子表现为第二黎曼面上的极点,频率发生单圈位移

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了dS时空中标量QED单圈规范场两点函数的完整解析计算框架,证明了Dirichlet规范场通过量子效应获得质量,边界流获得反常维度,并将圈修正光子自然诠释为dS共振态(准正规模)。谱Cutkosky规则和色散关系为dS时空中的量子修正提供了系统的计算工具,bulk计算与边界理论结果的一致性验证了AdS/CFT类型的对偶关系在dS时空中的适用性

Abstract: We compute the one-loop gauge-field two-point functions analytically in scalar QED on dS$_{d+1}$ for general dimension and scalar mass, using the EAdS reformulation of the in-in formalism arXiv:2007.09993. The Dirichlet EAdS gauge field acquires a Schwinger-type mass, with its longitudinal mode supplied by a composite of charged scalars obeying opposite boundary conditions, while the Neumann mode remains protected by gauge invariance. In dS, the Dirichlet correction induces an anomalous dimension $γ_J$ for the boundary current and modifies the late-time falloff of the electric field. This bulk calculation reproduces the boundary multiplet-recombination result of arXiv:2509.18888. Summing over the in-in contour branches gives the retarded dS bubble kernel, making direct contact with the Bros-Moschella framework. We establish a spectral Cutkosky rule that expresses the discontinuity of this kernel across the principal-series cut in the Casimir plane in terms of a two-particle cut, with both internal scalar lines placed on shell. Although the full bubble coefficient involves a ${}_7F_6$ hypergeometric function, the cut collapses to a closed product of Gamma functions and determines the complete non-local part of the kernel through a dispersion relation. Analytic continuation of the same cut reproduces the complete one-loop result for $γ_J$. In the complex Casimir plane, the loop-corrected Dirichlet photon appears as a pole on the second sheet and is therefore naturally interpreted as a dS resonance, or quasinormal mode, whose frequency is shifted at one loop.

</details>

### [221] [No-Go Theorem for Phantom Crossing in Scalar-Tensor Gravity with Non-Minimal Derivative Coupling and Soft Scale-Invariance Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14354)
*Reza Baghbani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了尺度不变的标量-张量理论(具有非最小导数耦合)能否实现DESI 2024观测偏好的幽灵穿越现象(w < -1),通过暗能量有效场论框架和沼泽地猜想约束,建立了三个严格的不可行定理,证明该类模型无法实现幽灵穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI 2024的重子声学振荡观测数据显示暗能量状态方程参数在低红移处穿越幽灵分界线(w < -1),即出现幽灵穿越现象。这促使研究者探索具有非最小导数耦合(NMDC)的尺度不变标量-张量理论是否能够为这一观测现象提供理论解释。

Method: 采用暗能量有效场论(EFT)形式化框架,系统推导无鬼自由度和梯度稳定性条件;结合量子引力的沼泽地猜想(包括de Sitter猜想和距离猜想)对理论模型施加约束;通过理论分析建立三个严格的不可行定理,检验尺度不变NMDC模型实现幽灵穿越的可能性。

Result: 建立了三个严格的不可行定理:(i)幽灵穿越要求ξ_eff < 0,这直接违反了无鬼稳定性条件ξ_eff > 0;(ii)沼泽地de Sitter猜想和距离猜想独立地阻碍幽灵穿越的实现;(iii)这些理论障碍相互增强。研究表明尺度不变NMDC模型无法解释DESI观测到的w < -1偏好。

Conclusion: 尺度不变的非最小导数耦合标量-张量理论无法实现DESI 2024观测偏好的幽灵穿越现象。理论稳定性条件(无鬼条件)与幽灵穿越要求根本不相容,且量子引力的沼泽地猜想提供了独立的理论障碍。这为超越该类模型的暗能量理论构建提供了明确的理论指导,表明需要探索其他理论框架来解释DESI的观测结果。

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI 2024) reveal a preference for dynamical dark energy with an equation of state parameter crossing the phantom divide ($w < -1$) at low redshifts. We investigate whether this phantom crossing can be realized within scale-invariant scalar-tensor theories featuring non-minimal derivative coupling (NMDC) between the scalar field and the Einstein tensor. Employing the effective field theory of dark energy formalism, we systematically derive the ghost-free and gradient stability conditions, and examine their compatibility with the swampland conjectures from quantum gravity. We establish three rigorous no-go theorems demonstrating that: (i)~phantom crossing necessitates $ξ_{\text{eff}} < 0$, directly violating the no-ghost stability requirement $ξ_{\text{eff}} > 0$; (ii)~the swampland de Sitter and distance conjectures independently obstruct phantom crossing; and (iii)~these theoretical obstructions are mutually reinforcing. Our results show that scale-invariant NMDC models cannot account for the DESI preference for $w < -1$, providing robust theoretical guidance for dark energy model building beyond this class.

</details>


### [222] [Non-Stationary Wormholes and Matter Source Reconstruction in General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14357)
*Geová Alencar,Tiago Crispim,Roberta Dárlla,Ricardo Landim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论中嵌入演化宇宙学背景的非稳态虫洞几何。通过Simpson-Visser类正则化和Cuscuton场构造了无初始奇点的宇宙学背景，分析了四维缺陷虫洞和Perry-Mann型拓扑荷虫洞的动力学特性，并证明了在可分离非稳态时空框架下，正则渐近平坦虫洞几何存在严格约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虫洞研究主要关注静态几何，而将虫洞嵌入演化宇宙学背景的非稳态情况尚未充分探索。研究者希望构造在正则（无奇点）宇宙学背景中的动力学虫洞模型，并理解将这类构造推广到任意空间几何时会遇到的限制，从而更全面地理解虫洞在现实宇宙演化中的可能性及其物质源要求。

Method: 采用Simpson-Visser类正则化方法处理(n+1)维FLRW度规的时间坐标，结合均匀Cuscuton场消除初始宇宙学奇点。具体分析两类动力学虫洞：(1)四维缺陷虫洞，用零势能正则标量场支撑；(2)Perry-Mann型拓扑荷虫洞，用拟物质场支撑空间结构。将分析推广到可分离非稳态时空，推导出约束允许物质源的标度条件，并检验最小耦合标量场和非线性电动力学场情况下的适用性。

Result: 成功构造了两类嵌入正则宇宙学背景的非稳态虫洞：四维缺陷虫洞可由正则标量场支撑，Perry-Mann型虫洞需要拟物质场支撑，两者的宇宙学演化均由Cuscuton场控制。在可分离非稳态时空框架下，导出的标度条件严格限制了物质源的函数自由度，消除了静态重构方案中存在的任意性。发现在满足光滑性和反射对称性条件的同时，无法获得正则的渐近平坦虫洞几何。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在正则宇宙学背景中构造特定非稳态虫洞的可行性，但同时揭示了将此类构造推广到任意空间几何时面临的根本性限制。标度条件的约束表明，动力学虫洞的构造比静态情况更加受限，特别是正则渐近平坦的非稳态虫洞在当前框架下无法同时满足所有物理要求。这些结果为理解虫洞在演化宇宙中的实现条件提供了重要的理论约束。

Abstract: We investigate non-stationary wormhole geometries in General Relativity embedded in evolving cosmological backgrounds. We employ a Simpson-Visser-like regularization of the temporal coordinate of the $(n+1)$-dimensional FLRW metric, together with a homogeneous Cuscuton field, to construct cosmological backgrounds in which the scale factor remains finite and non-zero at the would-be initial singularity. We then consider two specific dynamical wormhole geometries: the four-dimensional defect wormhole and the topologically charged Perry-Mann type wormhole. We show that the former can be supported by a canonical scalar field with vanishing potential, while the latter requires a mimetic matter sector to support its spatial structure, with the Cuscuton governing the cosmological evolution in both cases. We subsequently generalize the analysis to a separable non-stationary spacetime and derive a scaling condition that strongly constrains the admissible matter sources. In particular, for minimally coupled scalar and nonlinear electrodynamics fields, this condition removes the functional freedom available in static reconstruction schemes. We show that, within this framework, regular, asymptotically flat wormhole geometries cannot be obtained while simultaneously satisfying the required smoothness and reflection symmetry conditions. Our results illustrate both the possibility of constructing specific non-stationary wormholes in regular cosmological backgrounds and the restrictions that arise when attempting to generalize such constructions to arbitrary spatial geometries.

</details>


### [223] [Post-Newtonian secular dynamics of hierarchical triples II. Canonical derivation of the 1PN\,$\times$\,quadrupole cross terms and of the quadrupole-squared term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14505)
*Hideyoshi Arakida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过二级Lie-Hori-Deprit正则变换从1PN ADM三体哈密顿量推导出分层三体系统的长期演化哈密顿量,解决了相对论交叉项问题,验证了有效场论结果,并给出完整的四极矩平方阶长期哈密顿量及高阶修正项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层三体系统的后牛顿(1PN)长期演化理论存在未解决的相对论交叉项问题,特别是Lim和Rodriguez提出的振荡项以及1PN与四极矩相互作用项的独立验证缺失,需要建立严格的正则变换框架来系统处理这些高阶效应。

Method: 采用完全在正则变量中进行的二级Lie-Hori-Deprit变换处理1PN ADM三体哈密顿量;建立bookkeeping规则来枚举1PN参数δ和层级参数ε各阶的所有贡献;使用Tremaine虚拟时间方法处理共振情况;建立EIH和ADM描述之间的osculating-contact映射关系。

Result: 1PN单极相互作用简化为内质量的结合能重整化;Lim和Rodriguez发现振荡项的δε^(3/2)阶实际为空;电磁型1PN×四极矩交叉项与Kuntz等人的有效场论结果完全一致(磁项为首次独立推导);给出依赖于外轨道近心点幅角ω_2的次领头阶1PN×四极矩闭合形式;获得完整的四极矩平方阶长期哈密顿量,其2ω_2谐波分量为可消除的规范项;在LR三体系统的长期积分中,偏心率增强效应是Paper I的2-3倍,但LR的加速并合现象未出现。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分层三体系统1PN长期演化的完整正则理论框架,解决了相对论交叉项的争议,独立验证了有效场论结果,证明了EIH和ADM描述在1PN阶的等价性(仅在轨道根数定义上存在差异),并揭示了次领头阶ω_2依赖项对偏心率演化的重要控制作用,为精确模拟致密天体三体系统的长期动力学演化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the secular Hamiltonian of a hierarchical triple from the 1PN ADM three-body Hamiltonian by a two-stage Lie-Hori-Deprit transformation carried out entirely in canonical variables, and use it to settle the relativistic cross terms left open in Paper I. A bookkeeping rule enumerates all contributions to each order in the 1PN parameter $δ$ and the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon$. The 1PN monopole interaction reduces to a renormalization of the inner mass by its binding energy, and the order $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$, at which Lim and Rodriguez (LR) found a libration term, is empty. The electric and magnetic 1PN $\times$ quadrupole cross terms agree exactly with the effective-field-theory result of Kuntz, Serra, and Trincherini; for the magnetic term this is the first independent derivation. We give the next-to-leading order 1PN $\times$ quadrupole term in closed form; it depends on the outer argument of pericenter $ω_2$. At quadrupole-squared order we obtain the complete secular Hamiltonian, which generates the secular equations of Will and of Conway and Will; its $2ω_2$ harmonic is a gauge term, removable even on the commensurability between the ZLK period and the nodal precession, by a generator given in closed form via Tremaine's fictitious time. The EIH and ADM descriptions coincide at 1PN order; the formulations differ only in the definition of the elements, for which we give the osculating-contact map. In secular integrations of LR's triples, the eccentricity enhancement with the octupole is controlled by the $ω_2$-dependent part of the next-to-leading order term and is two to three times that of Paper I, while LR's accelerated mergers remain absent.

</details>


### [224] [Stability conditions for light rings in circular stationary axisymmetric spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14747)
*Jake O. Shipley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维圆对称静态轴对称时空中光环的稳定性问题，通过哈密顿形式和测地势的横向Hessian矩阵，将稳定性判据简化为纯曲率表达式，证明了稳定光环需满足物质聚焦效应强于局部潮汐剪切，并对多种物质模型进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光环是黑洞和致密天体时空结构的重要特征，其稳定性判定对理解强引力场物理至关重要。现有方法需要复杂计算，缺乏统一的几何-物理判据。本文旨在通过曲率张量建立光环稳定性的简洁判定条件，揭示物质分布与时空几何对稳定性的本质影响，并为不同物质模型提供普适性检验方法。

Method: 采用零测地线的哈密顿形式理论框架，利用时空的Killing对称性，将控制光环稳定性的测地势横向Hessian矩阵化简为纯曲率表达。通过适配光环的复零标架，用Ricci标量Φ₀₀和Weyl标量Ψ₀表示Hessian特征值μ₊=Φ₀₀±|Ψ₀|。结合Einstein场方程和Sachs光学方程，将稳定性条件转化为4πTₐᵦpᵃpᵇ>|Ψ₀|(物质聚焦主导局部剪切)。利用Hessian迹提供不依赖Weyl曲率的快速检验:违反严格零能量条件处光环必不稳定。将该判据应用于真空、电真空、带宇宙常数及标量场等具体物质模型。

Result: 稳定光环存在的充要条件为Φ₀₀>|Ψ₀|,即物质诱导的聚焦效应必须超过Weyl曲率引起的潮汐剪切。Hessian迹检验表明:严格零能量条件违反处光环必不稳定。对已知模型验证:成功重现真空和电真空(含或不含宇宙常数)的已知结果;证明最小耦合实标量场(任意自相互作用势)禁止稳定光环,因场必在两个Killing对称下不变;复标量场若在Killing坐标上携带调和依赖则可避开此限制,允许稳定光环存在。该方法提供了统一、简洁的几何-物理判据,计算成本低且物理图像清晰。

Conclusion: 本文建立了四维圆对称静态轴对称时空中光环稳定性的曲率判据:稳定性由Ricci标量和Weyl标量的竞争决定,物理上对应物质聚焦与潮汐剪切的对抗。Hessian迹提供了无需计算Weyl曲率的快速稳定性检验。该理论框架成功统一了已知结果,并揭示了标量场光环稳定性的对称性限制:实标量场禁止稳定光环,而复标量场可通过破缺对称实现稳定构型。研究为理解强引力场中的光传播和时空结构提供了几何洞察,对黑洞物理和引力波天文学具有重要意义。

Abstract: We consider the stability of light rings in four-dimensional circular stationary axisymmetric spacetimes. Using a Hamiltonian formalism for null geodesics and the Killing symmetries of the spacetime, we show that the transverse Hessian of the geodesic potential, which controls the light ring's stability, reduces there to a pure curvature expression. Its eigenvalues are $μ_{\pm} = Φ_{0 0} \pm \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$, where $Φ_{0 0}$ is a Ricci scalar and $Ψ_{0}$ a Weyl scalar of a complex null tetrad adapted to the light ring. Through the Sachs optical equations, these quantities are responsible for focussing and shear of a null geodesic congruence. Stability requires both eigenvalues to be positive, so a light ring is stable if and only if $Φ_{0 0} > \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$ there. Assuming the Einstein field equations with generic stress--energy tensor $T_{a b}$, we recast this as $4 πT_{a b} p^{a} p^{b} > \lvert Ψ_{0} \rvert$, where $p^{a}$ is the light ring's tangent vector: for stability, the focussing by matter must dominate the local tidal shear. The trace of the Hessian alone provides an inexpensive test of stability, requiring no knowledge of the Weyl curvature, since a light ring at which the strict null energy condition is violated cannot be stable. Applying the test to specific matter models, we recover known results for vacuum and electrovacuum, with and without a cosmological constant. We also demonstrate that stable light rings are forbidden for a minimally coupled real scalar field with any self-interaction potential, since such a field must be invariant under both isometries. We show that a complex scalar field escapes this obstruction whenever it carries harmonic dependence on the Killing coordinates.

</details>


### [225] [Analytic Electromagnetic Effective Source for Geodesic Orbit in Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14909)
*Xuchen Lu,Yungui Gong,Bokai Zhang,Chao Zhang,Wenting Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对施瓦西时空中测地运动构建了解析的电磁有效源,通过角动量补全和四足分解将矢量谐波穿刺模式表达为标量模式的有限和,推导了两种宇称扇区的耦合系数,并从麦克斯韦模式方程获得了有效源,为未来的1+1维残余场计算提供了源项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自力效应对于准确描述小天体在弯曲时空中的运动至关重要,而高阶自力计算面临技术挑战。有效源方法为解决这一问题提供了有前景的途径,但电磁场的有效源构建尚不完善。本研究旨在为施瓦西时空中的测地运动建立解析的电磁有效源表达式,以支持更高精度的自力计算和引力波物理研究。

Method: 采用有效源方法处理施瓦西时空中的电磁自力问题。首先通过角动量补全(angular completion)技术和四足基(tetrad)分解,将矢量谐波的穿刺模式(puncture modes)表达为标量模式的有限求和形式。然后系统推导了电磁场两种宇称扇区(偶宇称和奇宇称)的耦合系数。最后从麦克斯韦场方程的模式展开方程中提取有效源项,并验证点粒子的狄拉克δ函数贡献被成功抵消。

Result: 成功构建了施瓦西时空中测地运动的解析电磁有效源表达式。推导出了两种宇称扇区完整的耦合系数。验证表明点粒子的奇异δ函数贡献确实被抵消,有效源在粒子附近保持有限。针对偏心轨道的代表性算例显示,模式源在粒子近邻区域呈现良好的有限性和正则性,证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为施瓦西时空中的电磁自力问题提供了完整的解析有效源构造方案。通过角动量补全和四足分解技术,将复杂的矢量场问题简化为标量模式的处理,显著降低了计算复杂度。所得到的有效源表达式消除了点粒子的奇异性,为后续开展1+1维残余场数值计算奠定了理论基础,这对于实现高阶自力计算和提高引力波波形建模精度具有重要意义。

Abstract: Self-force effects are essential for accurately describing the motion of small bodies in curved spacetime. The effective-source approach privides a promising route to higher-order self-force calculations. In this paper, we construct analytic electromagnetic effective source for geodesic motion in Schwarzschild spacetime. An angular completion and a tetrad decomposition express the vector-harmonic puncture modes as finite sums of scalar modes. We derive the coupling coefficients for both parity sectors and obtain the effective sources from the Maxwell mode equations. The point-particle delta contribution cancels, and representative eccentric-orbit results show finite mode sources near the particle. These expressions provide the source terms for future $1+1$-dimensional residual-field calculations.

</details>


### [226] [Quantum Energy Inequalities with a twist: rotating frames along stationary worldlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14927)
*Christopher J. Fewster,Daniel Lomax Ford*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对闵可夫斯基时空中沿静态世界线的无质量标量场和电磁场，推导出量子能量不等式（QEI）边界的闭式表达式，并通过优化四元标架的角速度，发现电磁场的最紧QEI边界出现在Fermi-Walker四元标架下，而标量场的最优边界则不同于Fermi-Walker四元标架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然量子能量不等式（QEI）在平直和弯曲时空中对任意Hadamard量子场态都成立，但其一般形式的边界难以计算闭式解。现有研究（如Fewster & Thompson 2023）仅考虑了Frenet-Serret四元标架下无质量标量场的情况，存在局限性。因此需要系统研究不同四元标架选择对QEI边界的影响，并寻找最优边界。

Method: 1. 针对闵可夫斯基时空中具有恒定曲率不变量的静态世界线（满足四维Frenet-Serret方程的解，包括惯性、匀线性加速和匀速旋转世界线等特例），推导无质量标量场和电磁场的QEI边界闭式表达式；2. 考虑相对于Frenet-Serret四元标架具有恒定角速度的任意四元标架，计算不同四元标架下的QEI边界；3. 通过优化角速度参数，寻找最紧的QEI边界；4. 结合解析和数值方法研究最优边界的性质。

Result: 1. 成功获得了无质量标量场和电磁场在静态世界线上的QEI边界闭式表达式；2. 对于电磁场，最紧的QEI边界出现在Fermi-Walker四元标架下；3. 令人惊讶的是，对于无质量标量场，Fermi-Walker四元标架并不能给出最紧的QEI边界；4. 通过解析和数值分析确定了标量场的最优边界；5. 研究结果和方法显著改进了先前Fewster & Thompson（2023）的工作。

Conclusion: 本研究为闵可夫斯基时空中沿静态世界线的量子能量不等式提供了完整的闭式解，揭示了四元标架选择对QEI边界的关键影响。特别地，发现电磁场和标量场的最优四元标架存在本质差异：电磁场的最紧边界对应Fermi-Walker四元标架，而标量场则需要其他四元标架。这一发现深化了对量子场能量约束的理解，为量子场论在弯曲时空中的应用提供了重要的理论工具和方法论改进。

Abstract: The energy densities of the quantised scalar and electromagnetic fields need not be positive, but their averages along timelike worldlines are constrained by Quantum Energy Inequalities (QEIs) that hold in flat and curved spacetimes for arbitrary Hadamard quantum field states. However the general QEI bound is not easy to compute in closed form.
